### [53] [LGNNIC: Acceleration of Large-Scale GNN Training using SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07733)
*Liad Gerstman,Aditya Dhakal,Dejan Milojicic,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LGNNIC，一种利用SmartNIC（与远程内存节点共置）来降低分布式GNN训练通信开销的系统架构。LGNNIC将邻居采样和量化等预处理任务卸载到SmartNICs上，减少传输到计算节点的数据量并缓解网络拥塞，在标准GNN工作负载下最高实现62.4x的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图规模增长，在单节点CPU-GPU系统上存储和处理完整图不再可行。将图分布到多个远程内存节点虽然可行，但引入了训练期间节点间网络拥塞这一主要瓶颈。现有方法未充分利用现代系统中已存在的SmartNIC与远程内存共置的硬件配置来缓解这一问题。

Method: LGNNIC通过两种在SmartNIC上执行的互补技术来降低通信开销：(1) Neighbor Sampling（在远程节点上执行小批量采样），(2) Quantization（对采样后的批量数据进行量化）。同时设计了两种同步机制：低开销的DMA-based（DOCA-DMA）和高开销的socket-based方案作为对比基准。

Result: 在包含NVIDIA BlueField-2 SmartNIC远程内存节点和A100 GPU计算节点的PoC系统上评估：Neighbor Sampling在Sockets和DOCA-DMA下分别实现最高62.4x和17.5x的训练加速，主要归因于数据传输时间的减少；Quantization分别提供额外最高3.6x和1.3x的加速。

Conclusion: LGNNIC验证了利用SmartNIC卸载GNN预处理任务来缓解分布式训练网络瓶颈的有效性，在绝大多数配置下均展示了显著的训练加速效果，为未来大规模分布式GNN训练系统提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used across domains such as natural sciences, social network analysis, chip design, and recommendation systems. However, as graph sizes grow, storing and processing them entirely on a single-node CPU-GPU system becomes increasingly impractical. A promising approach is to distribute the graph across multiple remote memory nodes, though this introduces a major bottleneck: inter-node network congestion during training. To address this, we propose LGNNIC, a novel inter-node system architecture that leverages SmartNICs co-located with remote memory nodes-a configuration already available in modern systems-to reduce communication overhead in distributed GNN training. LGNNIC offloads key preprocessing tasks to SmartNICs, reducing the volume of data transferred to computational (training) nodes and alleviating network congestion. We introduce two complementary techniques executed on the SmartNICs during the preprocessing phase: Neighbor Sampling, which performs mini-batch sampling, and Quantization of the sampled batches. To evaluate LGNNIC under different communication infrastructures, we designed both an optimized low-overhead DMA-based synchronization mechanism and a high-overhead socket-based alternative used as a benchmark. We evaluate the core SmartNIC offloading mechanisms across standard GNN workloads and sampling hyperparameters using a proof-of-concept (PoC) system comprising one remote-memory node with an NVIDIA BlueField-2 SmartNIC and one compute node with an A100 GPU. Both Neighbor Sampling and Quantization on the remote node demonstrated substantial training speedups in most configurations. Neighbor Sampling achieved up to 62.4x and 17.5x speedups with Sockets and DOCA-DMA, respectively, primarily due to reduced data transaction time. Quantization provided additional speedups of up to 3.6x and 1.3x, respectively, by reducing data transfer.

</details>


### [54] [ElastiCo: Elastic Configuration and Interference-Aware Orchestration for GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07971)
*Jinghao Wang,Yihang Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Chunming Hu,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Albert Y. Zomaya,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ElastiCo是一个弹性共置框架，通过资源形状变换、弹性影子定价和干扰感知共置三种机制，使深度学习训练和离线大语言模型推理任务能够安全共享GPU资源，在不修改用户代码的情况下将平均JCT降低2.94倍、集群吞吐量提升2.02倍，并将GPU利用率从约25%提升至46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU集群需要同时服务深度学习训练和离线LLM推理工作负载，但现有调度器将两者视为隔离的资源消费者，采用刚性静态分配方式，导致大量GPU能力被浪费：训练任务预留整块设备却存在周期性空闲阶段，离线推理任务因突发需求模式而过度配置GPU。这种资源利用率低下的问题亟需解决。

Method: ElastiCo通过三种集成机制实现训练与推理工作负载的安全GPU共享：1) 资源形状变换（Resource Shape Transformation）——将每个任务表示为一系列可行的资源配置-性能配置族；2) 弹性影子定价（Elastic Shadow Pricing）——通过动态每资源影子价格将多资源分配问题分解为每个任务的配置选择子问题；3) 干扰感知共置（Interference-Aware Co-location）——使用基于硬件计数器和任务级特征训练的预测器来估计GPU共享下的成对性能退化。该系统实现为原生Kubernetes中间件，无需用户代码修改。

Result: 在64个GPU的测试平台和高达512个GPU的大规模轨迹驱动模拟中评估，平均JCT降低最多2.94倍，集群吞吐量提升2.02倍，GPU利用率从约25%提升至46%。

Conclusion: ElastiCo通过弹性共置策略有效解决了训练与推理工作负载共享GPU时的资源利用率问题，在不修改用户代码的前提下显著提升了集群性能和资源效率，为现代GPU集群调度提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern GPU clusters must simultaneously serve deep learning training and offline large language model inference workloads, yet existing schedulers treat these as isolated resource consumers with rigid, static allocations. This leaves substantial GPU capacity underutilized: training jobs reserve entire devices despite periodic idle phases, while offline inference tasks over-provision GPUs despite bursty demand patterns. We present ElastiCo, an elastic co-location framework that enables training and inference workloads to safely share GPUs through three integrated mechanisms. First, Resource Shape Transformation exposes each job as a family of feasible resource-performance configurations. Second, Elastic Shadow Pricing decomposes the resulting multi-resource allocation problem into per-job configuration selection subproblems via dynamic per-resource shadow prices. Third, Interference-Aware Co-location uses a predictor trained on hardware-counter and task-level features to estimate pairwise performance degradation under GPU sharing. Implemented as native Kubernetes middleware requiring no user-code modifications, ElastiCo is evaluated on a 64-GPU testbed and through large-scale trace-driven simulations (up to 512 GPUs), reducing the average JCT by up to 2.94x, increasing the cluster throughput by 2.02x, and increasing the GPU utilization from approximately 25% to 46%.

</details>


### [55] [eIRWR: Enhanced Iterative Random Walk with Restart for Scalable Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08073)
*Saiful Khan,Afrah Farea*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出eIRWR（增强迭代随机游走重启）方法，用于微服务架构中的根因分析。该方法通过幂律遥传锐化将重启质量集中在最可疑节点上，增广带自环和反向边的转移矩阵使概率在级联源处累积，并通过外层循环优化信念。在阿里巴巴微服务数据集（12K-25K节点）上，eIRWR达到MRR=0.75（中等可见性）和0.94（高可见性），比最优聚合基线提升2.8倍，17,000节点图上运行时间<25ms，适合在线部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的随机游走根因分析方法在重启时使用原始异常向量，该向量被下游"响亮"的受害者节点主导，忽略了"安静"的真正的故障源节点的定位信号，导致定位效果不佳。此外，以往工作中"弹性阻尼"（resilience damping）常被应用但缺乏系统分析，需要在公平基准下评估。

Method: (1) 通过受控消融实验证明转移矩阵上的"弹性阻尼"在数学上等价于提高重启概率，因此用重启调优的PPR作为公平基准。(2) 提出eIRWR方法，包含三个关键增强：a) 通过幂律遥传锐化（power-law teleportation sharpening）将重启质量集中在最可疑节点上；b) 在转移矩阵中加入自环和反向边，使概率在级联源头累积；c) 通过外层循环迭代精化信念。

Result: 在阿里巴巴微服务追踪数据集的三张大规模拓扑图（12K-25K节点）上，eIRWR在中等根因可见性下达到MRR=0.75，较最佳聚合指标基线提升2.8倍，并远超重启调优的PPR。在高可见性下达到MRR=0.94。在17,000节点的图上运行时间不足25ms，适合在线部署。

Conclusion: eIRWR通过针对性地集中重启质量、增广图结构以强化源头信号累积，以及外层信念迭代，显著超越了现有重启随机游走基线。其毫秒级运行时间使其具备实际在线根因分析部署的可行性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice architectures needs to pinpoint the originating faulty service responsible for the cascading symptoms seen across hundreds or thousands of interdependent services. Graph-based random walk methods propagate anomaly evidence over the service dependency graph. However, existing anomaly-restart walks leave much of the localization signal unused: they restart from the raw anomaly vector, which is dominated by loud downstream victims rather than the quieter source. Through a controlled ablation, we first show that the "resilience damping" often applied to the transition matrix is mathematically equivalent to raising the restart probability; we therefore benchmark against a restart-tuned Personalized PageRank (PPR) rather than its default configuration. We then present Enhanced Iterative Random Walk with Restart (eIRWR), which (a) concentrates restart mass on the most suspicious nodes through power-law teleportation sharpening, (b) augments the transition matrix with self-loops and backward edges so that probability accumulates at cascade sources, and (c) refines its belief across an outer loop. On three large-scale topologies (12K-25K nodes) from the Alibaba Microservice Trace Dataset, eIRWR attains a Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of 0.75 at moderate root-cause visibility, a 2.8 times improvement over the best aggregate-metric baseline and well above a restart-tuned PPR. At high visibility, it reaches MRR= 0.94, while running in under 25ms on graphs with 17,000 nodes, making it suitable for online deployment.

</details>


### [56] [Effect of Abstractions and Prompting Strategies on LLM-Guided High-Performance Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08085)
*Jiří Klepl,Maty'aš Brabec,Martin Kruliš*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过PolyBench基准测试套件，对比了LLM直接生成优化C代码与使用传统自动优化框架（如计算管线/优化调度）两种路径在并行HPC代码优化中的表现，结果表明LLM在给定明确优化目标时直接生成代码，在性能和有效性方面均优于传统自动化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码性能优化需要深厚的底层硬件知识和并行处理经验，对开发者极具挑战。LLM具备高级代码语义理解能力，有望实现超越传统可验证自动优化范围的变换。但传统自动化优化依赖领域特定表示（DSL）和受限变换规则以确保可验证性，本文旨在探究这些传统抽象是否有助于提升LLM引导优化的性能与正确性。

Method: 以PolyBench基准测试套件为评估平台，设计对比实验：一组是LLM在给定具体优化目标条件下直接生成优化的C代码；另一组是LLM构建计算管线和优化调度（经由传统自动调优框架）。通过比较两组方案生成代码的实测性能和有效性（正确性）指标来评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，在所评估的设置下，LLM在获得明确优化目标后直接生成C代码，其测量性能更优，有效性（合法性）比率也更高，优于通过传统框架创建计算管线和优化调度的方法。

Conclusion: 研究建议未来应探索替代性的可验证LLM代码优化路径，而非固守传统基于DSL和受限变换的自动化框架抽象。

Abstract: Code performance optimization is a vital aspect of modern software development, as it enables faster response times and reduced resource usage. These optimizations require a deep understanding of low-level hardware details and the intricacies of parallel processing, making them challenging even for experienced developers. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), which are increasingly capable of generating and understanding code, there is growing interest in incorporating these models into automated code optimization processes. Traditionally, this automation involves transcribing the source code into a domain-specific representation that can be auto-tuned using grid search or machine learning algorithms, while adhering to strict rules and a limited set of feasible transformations to ensure verifiability. LLMs incorporate high-level code semantics and can thus perform transformations that go beyond verifiable automated optimizations. This paper investigates whether the traditional abstractions used in automated code optimization improve the performance and correctness of LLM-guided optimizations of parallel HPC applications. We evaluate this using the PolyBench benchmark suite and demonstrate that, in our evaluated setting, LLMs provided with specific optimization goals achieve better measured performance and validity rates when generating C code compared to creating computation pipelines and optimization schedules with established frameworks, suggesting that future development should explore alternative approaches for verifiable LLM-guided code optimization.

</details>


### [57] [OasisKV: Scaling In-Decode KV Cache Beyond HBM with Lookahead Sparse Prefetching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08097)
*Can Xiao,Sukmin Cho,Junbong We,Zhixiong Niu,Jianyi Cheng,Yiren Zhao,Youngjin Kwon,Yongqiang Xiong,Rui Ma,Junyi Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OasisKV是一种以内存为中心的LLM推理系统设计，通过将KV缓存从HBM解耦，利用解码期注意力天然稀疏的特性，借助投机解码的lookahead token预测未来重要KV块并预取到HBM中，从而缓解HBM容量压力，实现推理吞吐量的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务越来越多地受内存而非算力限制。随着长上下文和长形式推理负载的普及，KV缓存主导了LLM token生成（decode）阶段的内存占用和内存流量。HBM容量成为稀缺且昂贵的资源，严重限制了推理批大小和系统吞吐量。

Method: OasisKV将完整KV缓存存储与HBM解耦，利用解码期注意力天然稀疏的特性，仅在HBM中保留最相关token的KV条目。通过投机解码（SD）的lookahead tokens准确预测未来重要token，采用高效的注意力后台流水线识别重要KV块，并从更高容量内存层（主机或远程内存）预取到HBM中，在下一次解码步骤前暂存。

Result: 基于vLLM实现。在2048-token KV预算下，lookahead预测的准确性使精度与full attention相差仅0.7个百分点。在推理负载上相较于稠密vLLM获得1.69倍吞吐提升（仅0.1点精度损失），在多GPU长上下文服务中最高达2.1倍。在prefill-decode分离场景下，吞吐约为稠密方案的2倍，每个请求的KV减少6.5-9.7倍，decode节点主机内存占用比完整KV传输少2.2-2.6倍。

Conclusion: OasisKV证明了通过在解码期利用注意力稀疏性并配合投机解码的预测能力，可以将KV缓存从昂贵的HBM中解耦到更廉价的内存层级，在不显著损失精度的前提下大幅提升LLM推理系统的吞吐量和资源效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference serving is increasingly constrained by memory rather than compute. As long-context and long-form reasoning workloads become more prevalent, the key-value (KV) cache dominates both memory footprint and memory traffic during LLM token generation, i.e., decode. In particular, HBM capacity has become a scarce and costly resource that heavily limits inference batch size and system throughput. This paper presents OasisKV, a memory-centric LLM inference system design that alleviates HBM capacity pressure by decoupling full KV-cache storage from HBM during LLM decoding. Because decode-time attention is naturally sparse, OasisKV keeps only the KV entries of the most relevant tokens in HBMs for attention computation. We observe that future important tokens can be predicted accurately in advance using lookahead tokens drafted by speculative decoding (SD). OasisKV employs an efficient attention background pipeline to identify important KV blocks. They are then prefetched from higher-capacity memory tiers (e.g., host or remote memory) and staged in HBMs before being used in the next decode step.
  We implement OasisKV based on vLLM. The lookahead prediction is accurate enough to keep accuracy within 0.7 points of full attention under a 2,048-token KV budget. This lets OasisKV turn sparsity into throughput gain: $1.69\times$ over dense vLLM on the reasoning workload at 0.1 points of accuracy loss, and up to $2.1\times$ on multi-GPU long-context serving. Under prefill--decode disaggregation, OasisKV reaches about $2\times$ dense throughput while admitting each request with $6.5$--$9.7\times$ less KV and holding $2.2$-$2.6$ less decode-node host memory than full KV transfer.

</details>


### [58] [C2C-Explorer: An Exploration Framework for Chip-to-Chip Interconnect Architectures in LLM Cloud Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08611)
*Jiayi Li,Di Wu,Qingxu Li,Hongxiao Zhao,Jiaqi Yang,Anjunyi Fan,Wenbin Zhang,Boqiang Wu,Shuting Liu,Shifeng Fang,Jianbo Dong,Dimin Niu,Bonan Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了C2C-Explorer，一个面向LLM工作负载的芯片间（C2C）通信架构自适应贝叶斯设计空间探索（DSE）框架，集成了LLM工作负载驱动的流量生成器、可扩展的互连模拟器（最高512芯片）和指标驱动的评估器，在真实LLM负载下实现C2C架构的系统性协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的规模化扩展需要多处理器芯片架构，使得芯片间（C2C）通信变得至关重要。但设计高效的C2C硬件架构面临三大挑战：无法生成真实的LLM特定C2C流量、难以在规模上精确模拟硬件级通信、以及指数级C2C设计空间的探索效率低下。

Method: 提出C2C-Explorer，一个自适应贝叶斯设计空间探索框架，包含三个核心组件：LLM工作负载驱动的流量生成器、可扩展的互连模拟器（支持switch和full-mesh拓扑，最高512芯片）、以及指标驱动的评估器，三者集成形成工作负载到硬件的优化流水线。模拟器采用混合周期与事件模型加速大规模模拟。

Result: C2C模拟器经FPGA验证，在多种流量模式下端到端时序误差为2.46-8.23%；混合周期-事件模型相比纯周期精确基线加速模拟最多7.8倍；在32-XPU的DeepSeek-R1-671B推理工作负载上，C2C-Explorer识别出的配置使goodput提升44.1%，内存减少98.4%。

Conclusion: C2C-Explorer展示了在真实LLM工作负载驱动下进行C2C架构协同设计的可行性，同时具备高精度和高模拟速度，并已开源。

Abstract: The scaling-up of large language models (LLMs) necessitates computing systems to have multi-processor-chip architectures, elevating the importance of chip-to-chip (C2C) communication. However, designing efficient C2C hardware architectures for LLM workloads faces three key challenges: generating realistic LLM-specific C2C traffic, accurately simulating hardware-level communication at scale, and efficiently exploring the exponentially large C2C design space. We propose C2C-Explorer, an adaptive Bayesian DSE framework that integrates a LLM-workload-driven traffic generator, a scalable interconnect simulator (switch/full-mesh, up to 512 chips), and a metric-guided evaluator into a workload-to-hardware optimization pipeline, enabling systematic C2C architectural co-design under realistic LLM workloads. Validated against FPGA-based C2C prototypes, the C2C simulator achieves 2.46-8.23% end-to-end timing error across diverse traffic patterns. Its hybrid cycle and event model further accelerates large-scale simulation by up to 7.8$\times$ over a pure cycle-accurate baseline. Applied to a 32-XPU DeepSeek-R1-671B inference workload, C2C-Explorer identifies configurations that improve goodput by 44.1% and reduce memory by 98.4%. C2C-Explorer is open-source and available at https://github.com/Selinaee/C2C-Explorer.

</details>


### [59] [Evaluating and Improving Weak Scalability Analysis of Visualization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08166)
*Marvin Petersen,Jonas Lukasczyk,Christoph Garth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有可视化大规模数据集的弱可扩展性评估方法存在缺陷：传统方法仅通过简单缩放输入数据量来增大问题规模，但许多可视化算法的工作负载不仅取决于输入规模，还取决于数据复杂度和输出规模，导致弱可扩展性评估不准确。作者提出了一种在共享内存环境下缓解不同缩放方法引起的工作负载不一致性的方法，并呼吁重新审视可视化可扩展性研究的评估与报告方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱可扩展性是评估算法在大规模下适用性的重要指标，但当前研究中普遍使用的简单数据缩放方法（仅增加输入大小）无法真实反映可视化算法的工作负载变化，因为许多算法的工作负载还受数据复杂度和输出规模等因素影响。这种不匹配导致弱可扩展性评估结果失真，需要一种更有效的方法来缓解该问题。

Method: 作者在多种数据集和多个算法上系统性地对比了不同的数据缩放方法，识别出不同缩放方法在不同算法和数据集上的适用性差异。在此基础上，提出了一种在共享内存环境下能够有效缓解不同缩放方法导致的工作负载增长不一致性的方法。

Result: 研究表明不同数据缩放方法在不同算法和数据集上的适用性各不相同，现有简单缩放方法会导致弱可扩展性评估不准确。作者提出的方法能够有效缓解各缩放方法之间工作负载增长不一致的问题。

Conclusion: 作者希望通过这项工作推动可视化社区对如何评估和报告可扩展性研究展开更深入的讨论，以建立更科学、更准确的可扩展性评估标准。

Abstract: Research on visualizing large-scale datasets traditionally relies on empirical evaluation of scalability, determining the effectiveness of specific computation methods, algorithmic strategies, or implementations. Weak scalability, which assesses the algorithm's performance as problem size and computing resources increase, is a valuable indicator for a method's applicability at scale. However, sufficiently large data sets with increasing size are needed for weak scalability studies. To this end, it is customary to use simple scaling techniques to increase problem size by generating larger input data sets from a base data set. Nevertheless, many visualization algorithms' workload depends on factors beyond input size, such as input data complexity or output size, leading to inaccuracies in the attributed weak scalability. In this work, we highlight different common data scaling methods on multiple algorithms and data sets, recognizing that the suitability of scaling approaches varies across algorithms and data sets. We present a method that effectively mitigates the observed inconsistencies in workload increases for the different scaling methods in a shared-memory setting. With this work, we aim to further the discussion on how to evaluate and report scalable visualization research.

</details>


### [60] [OpRAG: A Resource-Deterministic Runtime for GPU-Backed Multi-Stage RAG Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08340)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpRAG是一个面向GPU多阶段RAG工作流的资源确定性分布式运行时系统，通过将嵌入、检索、推理、记忆和upsert建模为一等算子并降级为通信感知执行图，结合Arrow零拷贝数据平面、持久化worker、有界队列、CPU tokenizer预取、批量GPU嵌入和检索/生成重叠执行，在不修改LLM解码内核的情况下，相比现有框架在端到端GPU流水线实验中提升了15.66%~20.71%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统虽然LLM解码是GPU密集型的，但周围编排层（orchestration layer）通过序列化开销、碎片化调度、低效批处理和CPU-GPU流水线停滞等问题限制了端到端性能。现有框架要么提供灵活的控制流但缺乏分布式可扩展性，要么提供可扩展的任务并行但未将RAG阶段暴露为具有确定性执行语义的资源感知算子。

Method: OpRAG将嵌入、检索、推理、记忆和upsert建模为一等算子，并将其降级为通信感知的执行图。系统采用六个关键技术：Arrow零拷贝数据平面、持久化worker（避免频繁启动开销）、有界队列（控制背压）、CPU tokenizer预取（隐藏CPU-GPU延迟）、批量GPU嵌入（提高GPU利用率）以及检索/生成重叠执行（消除流水线停滞）。

Result: 在端到端GPU流水线实验中，OpRAG相对最近竞争者提升16.16%（Llama3-8B）和15.66%（Mistral-7B），相对RayScalableRAG提升20.57%和20.71%，相对LangChain/LangGraph/CrewAI/AutoGen中最优基线提升17.77%和17.48%。在Higress风格查询服务中，混合检索延迟降低59.20-59.62%，生成场景延迟降低52.48-53.55%，同时保持100%的Recall@5。

Conclusion: 优化分布式编排层可以在不修改LLM解码内核的情况下显著提升GPU-backed多阶段RAG的性能，证明了系统层面的编排优化（而非内核级优化）是提升RAG端到端性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems combine preprocessing, embedding, retrieval, memory access, context construction, generation, and vector-index updates. Although LLM decoding is GPU-bound, the surrounding orchestration layer can still limit end-to-end performance through serialization overhead, fragmented scheduling, inefficient batching, and CPU--GPU pipeline stalls. Existing frameworks provide flexible control flow, while distributed runtimes provide scalable task parallelism, but neither exposes RAG stages as resource-aware operators with deterministic execution semantics. We present OpRAG, a resource-deterministic distributed runtime for GPU-backed multi-stage RAG workflows. OpRAG models embedding, retrieval, reasoning, memory, and upsert as first-class operators and lowers them into communication-aware execution graphs. It combines an Arrow zero-copy data plane, persistent workers, bounded queues, CPU tokenizer prefetching, batched GPU embedding, and overlapped retrieval/generation execution to reduce non-model overhead around LLM inference. We evaluate OpRAG using Llama3-8B and Mistral-7B with FlashAttention~2, BF16 execution, and 32K RAG chunks. In end-to-end GPU pipeline experiments, OpRAG improves over the nearest competitor by 16.16% for Llama3-8B and 15.66% for Mistral-7B, and over RayScalableRAG by 20.57% and 20.71%, respectively. Against LangChain, LangGraph, CrewAI, and AutoGen, OpRAG is 17.77% and 17.48% faster than the best framework baseline. In Higress-style query serving, OpRAG reduces hybrid retrieval latency by 59.20--59.62% and generation-scenario latency by 52.48--53.55%, while preserving 100% Recall@5. These results show that optimizing the distributed orchestration layer can substantially improve GPU-backed multi-stage RAG without modifying the LLM decoding kernel.

</details>


### [61] [Empirical Analysis of Cloud-Edge Infrastructure Complexity: Practitioner Pain Points and Architectural Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08400)
*Pawissanutt Lertpongrujikorn,Hai Duc Nguyen,Mohsen Amini Salehi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文基于101次半结构化访谈（覆盖86个组织）的实证分析，量化验证了云原生开发中的主要瓶颈是部署复杂性（38.6%）和入职难度（35.6%），并指出开发者更关注生产力（53.5%）和自动化（44.6%）而非性能优化。文章进而提出四种架构方向（统一对象抽象、内部开发者平台、声明式AI/ML服务管线、基于WebAssembly的轻量级边缘运行时），强调安全性、运维集成和多租户隔离是生产就绪的前提，论证分布式计算采用的主要障碍并非执行性能而是基础设施复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云、边缘和IoT计算的普及带来了分布式应用的机遇，但基础设施复杂性严重阻碍了开发者的生产力和创新。现有研究缺乏基于大规模实证数据对云原生开发痛点的系统量化分析，因此作者希望通过访谈研究来明确实际瓶颈，为架构演进提供数据支撑。

Method: 采用质性实证研究方法：对86个组织的101名从业者进行半结构化访谈，对访谈数据进行定量化统计分析，识别开发和运维的痛点分布及开发者优先级排序；基于实证结果提出并分析四种架构方向的可行性与适用性。

Result: 量化结果显示部署复杂性（38.6%）和入职难度（35.6%）是主导性运维瓶颈；开发者优先考虑生产力（53.5%）和自动化（44.6%）而非原始性能优化。四种架构路径被验证为可有效缓解上述痛点，但需满足安全、运维集成和严格多租户隔离等生产前提。

Conclusion: 分布式计算采用的主要障碍是基础设施复杂性而非执行性能；提供声明式治理的高层抽象（跨越多种计算范式）是缓解这一障碍的可行架构路径，未来需构建涵盖多方利益相关者的生态系统以实现新型基础设施范式的落地。

Abstract: The proliferation of cloud, edge, and Internet of Things (IoT) computing has created unprecedented opportunities for distributed applications. However, this architectural shift introduces profound infrastructural complexity, acting as a significant barrier to developer productivity and innovation. In this paper, we present an empirical analysis based on 101 semi-structured interviews across 86 organizations to investigate the state of cloud-native development practices, pain points, and expectations. Our findings quantitatively validate that deployment complexity (38.6%) and onboarding difficulty (35.6%) are the dominant operational bottlenecks, while developers heavily prioritize productivity (53.5%) and automation (44.6%) over raw performance optimization. Based on these empirical insights, we examine four architectural directions that address the validated pain points: unified object abstractions (Object-as-a-Service), platform engineering via Internal Developer Platforms, declarative AI/ML serving pipelines, and lightweight edge runtimes based on WebAssembly. Furthermore, we detail the multi-stakeholder ecosystem required for adopting novel infrastructure paradigms, emphasizing that security, operational integration, and strict multi-tenant isolation are prerequisites for production readiness. Our results demonstrate that the primary barrier to distributed computing adoption is not execution performance but infrastructural complexity, and that declaratively governed, higher-level abstractions across multiple paradigms offer viable architectural paths toward alleviating it.

</details>


### [62] [FlashBoot: Sub-Second Weight Loading for Large Models at Rack Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08482)
*Issac Zhu,Hscos Zhang,Keith Jiang,Jack Li,Hugh Yin,Jason Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashBoot是一个面向MoE弹性部署场景的权重加载子系统，通过FabricArena连续可寻址的张量内存布局和FlashLoad/FlashClone两项核心技术，解决了权重加载中的三大结构性问题（内存碎片化、NCCL通信开销、串行克隆路径），在NVL72上实现单节点加载提速50倍、机架级并发加载提速270倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旗舰MoE模型在总参数量和专家数两个维度同时快速增长，导致弹性部署场景中GPU权重加载成为延迟预算的主要瓶颈。即便在GB300 NVL72这样的顶级硬件上，现有加载器也无法充分利用带宽，存在三个结构性损耗：C1权重内存碎片化为数万个per-tensor对象导致传输远低于链路带宽；C2跨节点复制受NCCL通信建立开销（10-110秒）阻塞；C3现有跨节点GPU克隆路径串行且无法扩展到多节点并发启动。

Method: FlashBoot采用硬件友好、框架与工作流协同设计的方案，建立在SGLang之上。核心组件包括：1) FabricArena——一种连续、可导出且支持节点间寻址的张量内存布局，解决内存碎片化问题；2) FlashLoad——从CPU以单一批量、零拷贝方式加载权重；3) FlashClone——通过远程映射机制从远端GPU复制驻留模型，消除了NCCL通信建立开销。

Result: 在NVL72平台上使用DeepSeek-V4-Pro和DeepSeek-V4-Flash进行实验：FlashClone映射远端权重内存仅需约10毫秒（对比NCCL的10-110秒），每个克隆操作可持续保持≥700GB/s的传输速率。相比现有最优方法，FlashBoot将单节点权重加载加速最多50倍（从20.1秒降至0.4秒），机架级并发权重加载加速超过270倍（从87秒降至0.32秒）。

Conclusion: FlashBoot通过硬件友好的内存布局设计与框架工作流协同优化，系统性解决了MoE大规模弹性部署中权重加载的带宽利用瓶颈，实现了数量级的性能提升，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Flagship Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are growing fast along two axes at once: total parameter count and the number of experts. In elastic deployment scenarios, many GPUs across many nodes must become serving-ready quickly, and this growth makes weight loading a noticeable part of the latency budget. Even on NVIDIA's GB300 NVL72, today's state-of-the-art loaders leave most of that bandwidth unused. The losses are structural: (C1) weight memory is fragmented into tens of thousands of per-tensor objects, so transfers run far below link bandwidth; (C2) cross-node replication is gated by NCCL communicator setup, which costs 10-110 s before a single weight byte moves; and (C3) the existing cross-node GPU->GPU clone path is serial and scales poorly to concurrent multi-node bring-up. We present FlashBoot, a hardware-friendly, framework-workflow co-designed weight-loading subsystem built on SGLang. At its core is FabricArena, a contiguous, exportable and inter-node addressable tensor memory layout. On top of it, FlashLoad loads from CPU as a single bulk, zero-copy transfer, and FlashClone replicates a resident model from a remote GPU via a remote-mapping mechanism that removes NCCL setup. In experiments on NVL72 with DeepSeek-V4-Pro and DeepSeek-V4-Flash, FlashClone maps remote weight memory in ~10 ms (versus 10-110 s for NCCL) and sustains >=700 GB/s per clone. Against the state of the art, FlashBoot accelerates single-node weight loading by up to 50x (from 20.1 s to 0.4 s) and concurrent rack-level weight loading by >270x (from 87 s to 0.32 s). Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [63] [Randomized Tree-Intersection Leader Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08817)
*Yuval Emek,Shay Kutten,Ido Rafael,Gadi Taubenfeld*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在CONGEST模型中用于同步完全图n节点随机领导者选举算法，通过可调参数ℓ在时间复杂度和每节点消息复杂度之间实现平滑权衡，同时保持总消息复杂度为O(√n log^1.5 n)的严格亚线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式网络中进行领导者选举时，时间复杂度和消息复杂度之间存在权衡。现有算法往往难以同时兼顾两者，尤其是在带宽受限的网络中，需要一种可调节的算法来让系统设计者根据具体带宽和时延约束灵活配置。

Method: 算法采用动态截断的ℓ叉树扩展，并结合新颖的"静默脉冲"验证机制。通过强制扩展形成精确容量的近似完全树，节点可以安全聚合拓扑权重而不会超出亚线性消息上界。通过调整分支参数ℓ，可以在O(log_ℓ √(n log n))的时间复杂度和O(ℓ)的每节点消息复杂度之间进行光滑切换。

Result: 算法实现了O(log_ℓ √(n log n))轮时间复杂度和O(ℓ)每节点消息复杂度，总消息复杂度为O(√n log^1.5 n)。当ℓ=O(1)时，每节点消息开销最小；当ℓ增大时，可将选举时间压缩至O(1)轮。

Conclusion: 该算法为CONGEST模型中的领导者选举提供了高度可调的权衡框架，使低带宽网络可以以最小每节点消息负担运行，高带宽网络则可以接近常数时间内完成选举，具有广泛的实际部署适应性。

Abstract: We present a randomized leader election algorithm for synchronous complete $n$-node graphs in the \textsf{CONGEST} model that introduces a highly tunable trade-off between time complexity and the per-node message complexity. By adjusting a single branching parameter, $\ell$, system designers can smoothly shift the algorithmic burden from execution time to per-node message complexity, all while maintaining a strictly sublinear total message complexity of $O(\sqrt{n} \log^{1.5} n)$. We achieve this by utilizing dynamically truncated $\ell$-ary tree expansions coupled with a novel ``silent pulse'' verification mechanism. By forcing the expansions to form exact-volume almost-complete trees, nodes can safely aggregate topological weights without overshooting the sublinear message bounds. Specifically, our algorithm achieves $O(\log_\ell \sqrt{n \log n})$ time (round) complexity and $O(\ell)$ per-node message complexity. This flexibility allows networks with tight bandwidth constraints to operate with a minimal $O(1)$ per-node burden, while high-bandwidth environments can collapse the election into $O(1)$ time units.

</details>


### [64] [Universal Rendezvous of Anonymous Agents with Footprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08981)
*Bibhuti Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文回答了Das和Pelc（SPAA 2026）提出的开放问题：在带足迹（footprints）的匿名图模型中，是否存在对所有可行实例通用的汇合（rendezvous）算法。作者给出了肯定答案，提出了一种对任意连通图（有限或可数无限）上所有实例都有效的通用汇合算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在匿名图上两个匿名移动代理的确定性汇合问题是分布式计算中的经典问题。Das和Pelc在SPAA 2026论文中提出了一个开放问题：在带足迹模型下，是否存在对所有可行实例通用的汇合算法。回答这个问题是本文的核心动机，因为通用算法的存在性决定了该模型的理论边界和实用性。

Method: 本文考虑带足迹的模型：代理每次访问未标记节点时，会留下永久且相同的足迹。作者设计了一个通用的确定性汇合算法，适用于所有底层图为连通图（有限或可数无限）的可行实例。该算法不依赖于图的具体结构信息，仅利用足迹提供的记忆功能来实现两个代理的确定性相遇。

Result: 作者提出了一个在带足迹模型下对所有连通图（有限或可数无限）上的所有可行实例都能保证汇合的通用算法，从而证明了通用汇合算法存在于该模型的所有实例类别中，对开放问题给出了肯定的回答。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在带足迹的匿名图模型中，即使不事先知道图的具体结构，仍然存在能够统一解决所有可行汇合实例的确定性算法。这是对Das和Pelc开放问题的肯定解决，丰富了匿名代理汇合问题的理论成果。

Abstract: Deterministic rendezvous for two anonymous mobile agents starting simultaneously from two distinct nodes of an anonymous connected graph and navigating synchronously in the graph requires that they meet at some node. An instance of the rendezvous problem is the underlying graph, together with two distinct nodes that are the initial positions of the agents. Such an instance is said to be feasible if there is a deterministic algorithm guaranteeing rendezvous, possibly valid only for this instance. A rendezvous algorithm is said to be universal for a class of instances if it guarantees rendezvous for all feasible instances from this class.
  We consider the model with footprints: whenever an agent visits an unmarked node, it leaves a permanent footprint on it, and all footprints are identical. This paper aims at answering the open problem from the paper by Das and Pelc (SPAA 2026), asking whether there exists a universal rendezvous algorithm for the class of all instances in the model with footprints. We propose a universal rendezvous algorithm for the class of all instances where the underlying graph is connected (finite or countably infinite) in the model with footprints. This shows the existence of a universal algorithm for the class of all instances in the model with footprints, which is an affirmative answer to the open problem.

</details>

*Gunika Verma,Aashutosh A,Pooja Srinivas,Yogesh Simmhan,Ayush Choure,Harshit Shah,Mayukh Das,Prashant Sasatte,Chetan Bansal,Abhijit Pai,Suraj Dixit,Achint Agrawal*

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向大规模云数据库NoSQL工作负载的完整基准测试管道：从真实Cosmos DB集群中提取开源工作负载，提出衡量终端用户体验质量的新指标DRV（Distressed Resource Volume），开发基于非参数统计模型的策略仿真框架LoadStar，并设计了Luna负载预测模型和Orbit PAM算法来优化副本放置与迁移，实验显示可降低尾错误且节省高达35%的资源，已投入生产并预计每年节省数亿美元成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模托管云数据库依赖负载打包与迁移（PAM）算法来实现资源效率和可靠性，但缺乏公开的NoSQL工作负载数据严重限制了该领域的研究进展。作者希望通过提供真实生产环境的工作负载和可复用的基准管道来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先从Cosmos DB真实集群中开源NoSQL工作负载并提取统计特征；提出DRV指标量化终端用户体验的QoS；构建LoadStar仿真框架，采用非参数统计模型估计真实流量模式下的QoS；定义副本放置到VM节点的资源优化问题，开发Luna模型预测未来负载分布，并设计Orbit PAM算法利用预测结果触发和再平衡受压副本以减少尾错误。

Result: 通过LoadStar在真实工作负载上的验证，Orbit算法相比现有Cosmos DB策略和最差适配（worst-fit）优化基线，在更低错误率下实现更高负载吞吐，资源节省高达35%。该方案已部署至生产环境，可每年节省数亿美元成本，同时为数百万客户提升服务可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文通过开源工作负载、新指标、仿真框架、预测模型和优化算法构建了一套完整的资源中心型NoSQL工作负载基准管道，证明了基于预测的PAM策略能显著优于现有启发式策略，兼具学术价值和实际生产经济效益。

Abstract: Large-scale managed cloud databases leverage sophisticated load Packing and Migration (PAM) algorithms, which provide the efficiencies necessary for running these services at scale on cloud resources. Research into optimizing the resources and reliability of cloud databases at massive scales is limited by a lack of public NoSQL workloads. We address this in the context of Cosmos DB, Microsoft's flagship cloud-hosted NoSQL database. We first propose open-source NoSQL workloads from real Cosmos DB clusters, and analyze these traces to derive a novel reliability metric, Distressed Resource Volume (DRV), which captures the quality of service experienced by the end user. We then develop an open-source policy simulation framework, LoadStar, powered by a non-parametric statistical model of estimating the QoS of real traffic patterns. These form a reusable benchmark pipeline for validating policies for resource-centric NoSQL workloads. We then define a resource optimization problem for placing Cosmos DB replicas onto VM nodes, develop the Luna model for forecasting future load distributions, and the Orbit PAM algorithm that uses these forecasts to trigger and rebalance stressed replicas, to reduce tail-errors. Our experiments, validated using LoadStar for these workloads, demonstrate Orbit's benefits over the existing Cosmos DB policy and a worst-fit optimized baseline, with higher load delivered at lower error rates and up to $35\%$ reduction in resources. These have been deployed in production, with potential savings of $\$100M$s/yr while improving service reliability for millions of customers.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Fast Contractions: Attenuation and Recovery of Matrix-Engine Speedups in High-Order Finite Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09175)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Zeyu Song,Wubing Wan,Jiayu Fu,Zekun Yin,Yuyang Jin,Xiaohui Duan,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了ARM LX2处理器（Lineshine超算旗舰）上矩阵引擎（SME）相对于传统SIMD（SVE）在SPECFEM3D主导刚度算子中的实际性能差距，发现SME的4倍理论单精度峰值优势在完整算子中仅剩1.1倍，通过分解诊断定位瓶颈（逐点计算、间接场移动、同步、不规则系数交付），并通过显式SIMD、场布局调整和向量分块系数流等技术将加速比提升至1.6倍，论证了实现矩阵引擎性能需对整个算子路径进行协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代处理器提供的矩阵引擎峰值算力远超传统SIMD，但科学应用难以端到端享受这一优势。作者旨在剖析SPECFEM3D刚度算子在配备SME的Arm LX2 CPU上的性能差距来源，并寻找系统性的缓解策略。

Method: 采用因子化诊断方法，将完整算子性能损失归因于逐点计算、间接场移动与同步、不规则系数交付等环节；分别采用显式SIMD缓解逐点工作、改变场布局缓解间接移动与同步、向量分块系数流降低不规则访问成本，并给出无收缩控制界以量化收缩本身的增益上限。

Result: SME的4倍单精度峰值优势在孤立张量收缩中降至2.2倍，在完整算子中仅剩1.1倍；经显式SIMD优化后升至1.3倍，进一步结合场布局调整与向量分块系数流后在高阶可达1.6倍；收缩本身的理论增益上界仅为1.11–1.32倍。

Conclusion: 要实现矩阵引擎的端到端性能优势，必须对整个算子路径（含逐点计算、数据布局、访存模式等）进行协同设计，而非仅替换收缩内核。

Abstract: Modern processors increasingly provide matrix engines whose peak arithmetic throughput greatly exceeds conventional SIMD, but scientific applications rarely realize this advantage end to end. We examine this gap in SPECFEM3D's dominant stiffness operator on the Arm LX2 CPUs that power the flagship Lineshine supercomputer. Against a matched, high-performance SVE baseline on the same cores, SME's $4\times$ single-precision peak advantage falls to $2.2\times$ for isolated tensor contractions and $1.1\times$ for the complete operator. Our factorized diagnostic attributes the loss to pointwise computation, indirect field movement and synchronization, and irregular coefficient delivery. Explicit SIMD mitigates pointwise work, raising the full-operator speedup to $1.3\times$. Field-layout changes mitigate indirect movement and synchronization, while vector-blocked coefficient streaming reduces irregular-access costs; together they raise speedup to $1.6\times$ at high order. A contraction-free control bounds further contraction-only gains at $1.11$--$1.32\times$. Realizing matrix-engine performance therefore requires co-designing the entire operator path, not merely replacing its contraction kernel.

</details>


### [67] [UnionSparse: An Index-Efficient Sparsity Framework for Low-Bit Sparse LLM Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09291)
*Tianhao Jiang,Hang Gu,Teng Wang,Qianyu Cheng,ZhenDong Zheng,Cheng Tang,Qiyue Su,Wenqi Lou,Lei Gong,Chao Wang,Xi Li,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: UnionSparse是一个面向边缘GPU的低比特稀疏LLM推理框架，通过引入有效负载-元数据比（PMR）指标，结合索引高效位图编码（IE-BME）和低比特共享内存并行解码（LSPD）内核，解决了量化后稀疏元数据开销不成比例导致的SpMM瓶颈问题，在W4A4量化下相比现有方法实现了1.43x-3.46x的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的LLM推理需要同时利用稀疏性和低比特量化来满足内存、延迟和功耗限制。然而，量化虽然缩减了权重负载的大小，却未能成比例地减少稀疏元数据（如索引）的开销，导致索引传输和nonzero提取成为SpMM计算中的关键瓶颈。现有方法未能充分解决这一问题。

Method: 提出两个核心贡献：(1) 引入PMR（Payload-to-Metadata Ratio）指标来衡量负载与元数据的比例，证明提升PMR可以提高解码阶段的有效计算强度；(2) 设计UnionSparse框架，包含IE-BME（Index-Efficient Bitmap Encoding）编码方案用于摊销元数据开销并让稀疏遍历与片段组装对齐，以及基于LSPD（Low-Bit Shared-Memory Parallel Decoding）的SpMM内核以改进小批量执行效率。

Result: 在W4A4量化和30%-70%稀疏度条件下，UnionSparse相比FlashLLM和SpInfer分别获得2.30x和1.43x的性能提升，相比CUTLASS和cuBLAS Tensor Core分别获得1.56x和3.46x的提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了有效负载提取效率（payload-extraction efficiency）是低比特稀疏推理在边缘GPU上的一阶关注问题，UnionSparse证明了通过优化PMR可以显著提升边缘场景下的推理性能。

Abstract: Edge LLM inference combines sparsity and low-bit quantization to meet device memory, latency, and power limits. Yet quantization shrinks weight payloads without proportionally reducing sparse metadata, so index traffic and nonzero extraction become critical SpMM bottlenecks. We introduce the Payload-to-Metadata Ratio (PMR) and show that improving PMR raises effective compute intensity in decoding.
  We present UnionSparse, an index-efficient framework that combines Index-Efficient Bitmap Encoding (IE-BME) with a SpMM kernel using Low-Bit Shared-Memory Parallel Decoding (LSPD). IE-BME amortizes metadata and aligns sparse traversal with fragment assembly, while LSPD improves small-batch execution. Under W4A4 quantization and 30%--70% sparsity, UnionSparse outperforms FlashLLM and SpInfer by 2.30x and 1.43x, and CUTLASS and cuBLAS Tensor Core by 1.56x and 3.46x, respectively. These results establish payload-extraction efficiency as a first-order concern for low-bit sparse inference on edge GPUs. Source code is available at: https://github.com/Victor-Alen/UnionSparse.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond the Limits: Flexible and Congestion-Aware Cluster Scheduling for the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09308)
*Oliver Larsson,Thijs Metsch,Cristian Klein,Erik Elmroth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补策略改善云环境工作负载调度：(i)引入软SLO限制允许受控超卖和容忍短暂违规以提高集群效率；(ii)基于最后一级缓存（LLC）和内存带宽等共享资源的实时拥塞感知调度与重调度。实验结果表明软SLO限制相比硬限制方法减少49%的纠正性重调度动作，资源感知调度降低8%的节点级拥塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云环境工作负载调度基于对应用资源需求和硬件利用率的简化假设，忽视了应用级性能目标与硬件资源争用，导致资源使用效率低下和性能退化。当前方法存在两个关键局限：一是服务级目标（SLO）强制执行过于严格，导致资源利用不足和能耗效率低下；二是缺乏对共享资源（如LLC和内存带宽）的拥塞感知。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：(i)集成软SLO限制，允许受控超卖（overcommitment）并容忍轻微、瞬时的违规，以提升集群效率；(ii)引入基于共享资源（LLC和内存带宽）实时拥塞信息的资源感知调度与重调度机制，在调度决策中纳入应用级灵活性和硬件级洞察。

Result: 实验结果显示：软SLO限制相比硬限制方法减少了49%的纠正性重调度动作，同时保持可接受的性能保证；资源感知调度将节点级拥塞降低了8%，并进一步缓解了SLO违规。

Conclusion: 将应用级灵活性（软SLO）与硬件级洞察（共享资源拥塞感知）结合到调度与重调度决策中，能够同时提升集群资源利用效率和性能保证，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Workload scheduling in cloud environments often relies on simplistic assumptions about application resource needs and hardware utilization. Overlooking application-level performance objectives and hardware resource contention that leads to inefficient resource usage and degraded performance. This paper addresses two key limitations of current approaches. First, unnecessarily strict enforcement of service level objectives (SLOs) often leads to resource underutilization and poor energy efficiency. Second, lack of congestion awareness in shared resources such as last-level cache (LLC) and memory bandwidth. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies to address these limitations: (i) integrating soft SLO limits that allow controlled overcommitment and tolerate minor, transient violations to improve cluster efficiency, and (ii) introducing resource-aware scheduling and rescheduling based on real-time congestion insights for shared resources such as last-level cache (LLC) and memory bandwidth. Our results show that soft SLO limits reduce corrective rescheduling actions by 49% compared to hard-limit approaches while maintaining acceptable performance guarantees. Additionally, resource-aware scheduling decreases node-level congestion by 8% and further mitigates SLO violations, demonstrating the effectiveness of incorporating application-level flexibility and hardware-level insights into scheduling and rescheduling decisions.

</details>


### [69] [How Accurately Can the Energy Use of Spark Applications Be Estimated Based on Resource Utilisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09359)
*Youssef Moawad,Kathleen West,Vasilis Bountris,Philipp Thamm,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以Apache Spark on Kubernetes为案例，对比基于模型的能量估算与Intel RAPL硬件计数器实测值，验证在不同云环境（AWS裸金属和本地集群）中，使用外部监控信号相比Spark任务轨迹能降低软件包能量误差，将低估从-29.58%降至-24.41%（AWS）及从-24.00%降至-16.22%（本地）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境中用户无法直接访问节点级硬件能量计数器，阻碍了透明的可持续性核算。能量和碳归因方法依赖功率模型和资源利用率轨迹，但这些估算的准确性必须在仍能获取直接计数器时进行验证。

Method: 以Apache Spark on Kubernetes作为案例研究的数据流运行时和集群资源管理器，在AWS裸金属云和本地集群上，将基于模型的能量估算与Intel RAPL包和DRAM能量进行对比，比较不同的CPU使用信号和内存系数。

Result: 外部监控相比Spark任务轨迹改善了包级能量误差的符号偏差：AWS上低估从-29.58%降至-24.41%，本地集群上从-24.00%降至-16.22%。

Conclusion: 利用外部监控信号驱动的功率模型比依赖Spark内部任务轨迹的能量估算更准确，可为无硬件计数器访问权限的分布式数据流处理场景提供更可靠的可持续性核算方法。

Abstract: Distributed batch data processing applications are widely executed on cloud-based resources where restricted user access to node-level hardware energy counters hinders transparent sustainability accounting. Energy and carbon attribution methodologies therefore depend on power models and available resource utilisation traces, yet the accuracy of these estimates has to be validated while direct counters are available. In this work, we use Apache Spark running on Kubernetes as a case-study dataflow runtime and cluster resource manager to compare model-based energy estimates to Intel RAPL package and DRAM energy on an AWS bare-metal cloud and an on-premises cluster, comparing different CPU usage signals and memory coefficients. We show that external monitoring improves signed package-energy error relative to Spark task traces, reducing underestimation from -29.58% to -24.41% on AWS and from -24.00% to -16.22% on-premises.

</details>


### [70] [$\tilde{\text{O}}$ptimal Distributed Maximum Flow Approximation in Undirected Planar Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09500)
*Yaseen Abd-Elhaleem,Michal Dory,Oren Weimann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个分布式近最优的~O(D)轮(1-o(1))-近似算法，用于一般无向平面图上的最大st-流问题，移除了以往算法中s和t必须位于同一面的限制，并消除了n^{o(1)}因子。核心贡献是将Reif的经典集中式算法转化为分布式实现，通过在平面偶图G*上进行递归切口过程模拟，尽管实际通信只能在原图G上进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图上的分布式算法是近年来的研究热点。对于最大st-流问题，已有精确算法达到~O(D^2)轮，但近似算法（~O(D)轮）仅适用于s和t在同一面的特殊情况。本文旨在消除这一限制，同时去除n^{o(1)}因子，实现在一般无向平面图上接近最优的分布式近似算法。

Method: 本文的主要技术贡献是将Reif的经典集中式算法进行分布式实现。具体方法包括：在平面偶图G*上执行精心设计的递归切口（incision）过程，难点在于需要在偶图G*上模拟动态变化（切口操作），而实际通信只能在输入图G上进行。

Result: 提出了第一个分布式~O(D)轮(1-o(1))-近似算法，适用于一般无向平面图上的最大st-流问题，移除了s和t必须位于同一面的限制，并消除了n^{o(1)}因子。

Conclusion: 本文解决了分布式最大st-流近似算法中s和t位置限制的问题，通过Reif算法的分布式实现，达成了与无向平面图最短路径近似算法（Rozhon等人STOC'22）相匹配的~O(D)轮复杂度边界，是平面图分布式优化算法的重要进展。

Abstract: Persistent efforts in recent years have been devoted to devising distributed algorithms for fundamental optimization problems in planar graphs. In particular, for Single-Source Shortest-Paths, there is an $\tilde O(D^2)$-rounds exact algorithm [Li, Parter STOC'19] for directed planar graphs, and an $\tilde {O}(D)$-rounds $(1+o(1))$-approximation algorithm [Rozhon, Grunau, Haeupler, Zuzic, Li STOC'22] for undirected planar graphs (where $D$ is the graph's hop-diameter). Recently [Abd-Elhaleem, Dory, Parter, Weimann PODC'25], a matching bound for the exact case was obtained for the Maximum $st$-Flow problem. Namely, an $\tilde O(D^2)$-rounds exact algorithm for directed planar graphs. However, for the approximate case, they give a $D\cdot n^{o(1)}$-rounds $(1-o(1))$-approximation algorithm for undirected planar graphs that works only for the special case where both $s$ and $t$ lie on the same face.
  In this paper, we remove the restriction that both $s$ and $t$ must lie on the same face (we also eliminate the $n^{o(1)}$ factor). Namely, we present the first distributed near-optimal $\tilde{O}(D)$-rounds $(1-o(1))$-approximation algorithm for Maximum $st$-Flow in general undirected planar graphs. Our main technical contribution is a distributed implementation of the classical Reif's [SICOMP'83] centralized algorithm. This is achieved by a careful recursive incision procedure on the planar dual $G^*$ of the graph $G$. It is challenging, because we need to simulate dynamic changes (incisions) over the dual graph $G^*$, while we can only communicate over the input graph $G$.

</details>


### [71] [A Preliminary Study on Simultaneous Coscheduling for Discrete GPU vs. Fused GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09647)
*Poorna Gunathilaka,Nabayan Chaudhury,Kirshanthan Sundararajah,Wu-chun Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究初步评估了NVIDIA GH200超级芯片上的CPU-GPU协同调度，与离散H100 PCIe平台对比，以稀疏共轭梯度（CG）为案例，考察三种内存管理范式（显式拷贝、托管内存、映射内存）下的不同工作划分。结果显示GH200使多种混合CPU-GPU工作划分具有竞争力，并让托管内存在多个矩阵上变得实用，表明集成式CPU-GPU平台可同时提升协同调度工作负载的性能和可编程性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU-GPU协同调度可同时利用两种处理器执行应用，但其效率受工作负载划分和内存架构的显著影响。作者旨在评估GH200这类集成式CPU-GPU平台相对于传统离散H100 PCIe平台在协同调度场景下的性能与可编程性权衡。

Method: 以稀疏共轭梯度（CG）算法为案例研究，在GH200和H100 PCIe平台上对比多种CPU-GPU工作划分方案，并评估三种内存管理范式：显式拷贝（explicit copy）、托管内存（managed memory）和映射内存（mapped memory），分析运行时间和编程复杂度之间的权衡。

Result: 与H100 PCIe平台相比，GH200使得多种混合CPU-GPU工作划分变得具有竞争力，并且在多个矩阵上使托管内存范式变得实用可行，减少了手动CPU-GPU数据搬移的开销。

Conclusion: 集成式CPU-GPU平台（如GH200）能够同时改善协同调度工作负载的性能和可编程性，为未来的异构计算架构设计提供了有价值的参考方向。

Abstract: CPU-GPU coscheduling enables simultaneous execution of an application across both processing units, but its efficiency depends on workload partitioning and memory architecture. This preliminary study evaluates coscheduling on the NVIDIA GH200 Superchip compared to a discrete H100 PCIe platform. Using sparse conjugate gradient (CG) as a case study, we assess various work divisions across three memory-management paradigms: explicit copy, managed memory, and mapped memory. Our evaluation highlights the run time and programmability tradeoffs of reducing manual CPU-GPU data movement. The results show that compared with the H100 PCIe platform, GH200 makes several hybrid CPU-GPU work divisions competitive and makes managed memory practical for several matrices. These results suggest that integrated CPU-GPU platforms such as GH200 can improve both performance and programmability for coscheduled workloads.

</details>


### [72] [SpSYRK: Half the Work in Distributed Sparse Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09713)
*Thomas McFarland,Julian Bellavita,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用对称性加速分布式稀疏SYRK（C = AA^T）计算的新算法，通过仅计算输出矩阵的下三角部分来减少通信和计算开销，在Perlmutter超级计算机的32个节点上相比优化的Sparse SUMMA实现了2倍加速，并可作为现有应用的无缝替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵的对称秩-k更新（SYRK）在机器学习、图分析和基因组学中广泛应用，尤其是Jaccard相似度计算等场景。然而，现有的分布式稀疏矩阵乘法算法（如Sparse SUMMA）将稀疏SYRK当作通用乘法处理，没有利用输入输出矩阵中的对称性，导致性能未得到充分发挥。因此需要专门为稀疏SYRK设计利用对称性的分布式算法。

Method: 论文提出了两种分布式稀疏SYRK算法变体：(1) 将输出矩阵的块下三角部分分配给进程网格的下三角区域，仅计算对角块的下三角部分，从而减少每个进程的通信量和计算量；(2) 另一种变体通过重新排序通信过程，避免具体化A的转置A^T，减少内存开销和通信成本。

Result: 在Perlmutter超级计算机的32个节点上，算法的第一种变体在局部乘法占主导的矩阵上比优化的Sparse SUMMA获得2倍加速；在通信受限的输入上优势收窄，这与成本模型基于算术强度预测的结果一致。第二种变体则修正了这一不足，在高进程数下持续实现更优的扩展性。

Conclusion: 该算法可作为任何通过分布式SpGEMM例程计算C = AA^T的应用的即插即用替代方案，其三角输出可直接被后续操作消费，同时减少计算和内存占用，具有广泛的适用性和部署价值。

Abstract: The symmetric rank-$k$ update (SYRK), $\C = \A\A^\top$, computes the dot product between each pair of rows of $\A$, producing the Gram matrix $\C$. Its sparse variant underpins similarity search in machine learning, graph analytics, and genomics, including Jaccard similarity on datasets too large for a single node. Yet, despite the symmetry in its inputs and outputs, existing distributed sparse matrix multiplication algorithms, such as Sparse SUMMA, treat sparse SYRK as generic multiplication, leaving performance untapped.
  In this paper, we present distributed sparse SYRK approaches that leverage symmetry. The approach partitions the off-diagonal blocks of the output between the upper and lower triangular portions of the process grid and computes only the lower-triangular part of each diagonal block, reducing per-process communication and computation compared with state-of-the-art distributed SpGEMM. A second variant reorders communication to avoid materializing $\A^\top$.
  On 32 nodes of the Perlmutter supercomputer, the algorithm achieves a $2\times$ speedup over an optimized Sparse SUMMA on matrices where local multiplication dominates the runtime; the advantage narrows on communication-bound inputs, a dependence the cost model predicts from the arithmetic intensity. Our variant rectifies this and consistently achieves superior scaling at high process counts. The approach is a drop-in replacement for any application computing $\C = \A \A^\top$ via a distributed SpGEMM routine, and its triangular output can be consumed directly by subsequent operations, reducing both compute and memory footprint.

</details>


### [73] [Defining Decentralization: An Ontological Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09748)
*Jakub Kacper Szeląg,Aydin Abadi,Mohammad Naseri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出计算机科学中"去中心化"概念缺乏统一定义的问题（称之为"去中心化难题"），提出基于图论的形式化本体框架，将去中心化定义为通信系统的关系性和主体特定属性，将其与分布式明确区分，并提出两个新度量指标（Void Tolerance和Imperviousness），同时提供浏览器端实现工具，在联邦学习和区块链场景中验证了该框架的一致性和可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "去中心化"这一概念在计算机科学中已存在半个多世纪，但始终缺乏普遍适用的统一定义。随着去中心化AI和机器学习范式（如协作训练、分布式推理、区块链、智能体AI）的兴起，去中心化常被作为核心设计目标，但现有方法常将去中心化与信任分布或具体实现范式混淆，导致系统分析不一致、作品间可比性受限、形式化推理严谨性不足。

Method: 本文分析了去中心化的形式语义、认识论和实用主义基础，引入基于图的去中心化本体，将其定义为计算机通信系统的关系性且主体特定的属性。该框架从形式上区分了去中心化与分布式，并通过两个新度量——Void Tolerance（空洞容忍度）和Imperviousness（不渗透性）来支持评估。同时提供基于浏览器的实现，支持对任意系统进行自动分类和度量计算。

Result: 在联邦学习和区块链架构上的实例化评估表明，该框架能够产生一致、可比较的评估结果，而这些案例中现有定义往往会得出不完整或相互矛盾的结论，验证了该框架作为跨异构系统分析去中心化的领域无关基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为去中心化提供了领域无关的形式化分析基础，解决了去中心化难题，支撑跨异构计算通信系统中去中心化的严谨形式化推理和可比性分析。

Abstract: Decentralization as a concept in computer science has existed for over half a century. Despite its fundamental role across domains such as security, distributed computing, artificial intelligence, cloud infrastructures, and Internet of Things (IoT) architectures, there remains no universally accepted definition of decentralization applicable across computer communication systems. This has become increasingly problematic with the emergence of decentralized AI and machine learning paradigms, including collaborative training, distributed inference, blockchain-based, and agentic AI, where decentralization is often treated as a core design objective. Meanwhile, existing approaches frequently conflate decentralization with related notions such as distribution of trust or specific implementation paradigms. Such ambiguity creates inconsistencies in system analysis, limits comparability between works, and weakens the rigor of formal reasoning surrounding communication architectures and protocol design. In this work, we define this research gap as the Decentralization Problem.
  We analyze the formal-semantic, epistemological, and pragmatic foundations of decentralization and introduce a graph-based ontology defining it as both relational and subject-specific property of computer communication systems. The framework formally distinguishes decentralization from distribution and supports evaluation through two novel metrics: Void Tolerance and Imperviousness. We also provide a browser-based implementation that enables automated classification and metric computation of arbitrary systems. Instantiations to federated learning and blockchain architectures show consistent, comparable assessments where existing definitions produce incomplete or contradictory conclusions, providing a domain-independent foundation for analysing decentralization across heterogeneous systems.

</details>

### [74] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将人工智能应用于银行欺诈识别，重点开发基于机器学习（尤其是人工神经网络）的欺诈交易检测模型。通过多种预处理技术（处理不平衡数据、特征变换与工程）提升检测精度，实验结果表明逻辑回归（AUC≈0.946）与堆叠泛化（AUC=0.954）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于新冠疫情促使大量银行业务转向线上平台，银行欺诈案件显著增多，且犯罪分子利用慈善基金进行诈骗。因此需要借助机器学习算法对在线银行交易进行有效分析和识别，以应对这一日益严峻的数字社会安全问题。

Method: 采用多种机器学习算法对银行交易数据进行分析，提出针对高度不平衡数据集的处理技术、特征变换和特征工程方法以提升检测准确性。核心模型基于人工神经网络，同时对比多种算法，并采用堆叠泛化（stacked generalization）策略集成模型以优化AUC指标。

Result: 不同算法的结果经过可视化对比，逻辑回归算法表现最佳，AUC值约为0.946；堆叠泛化方法进一步提升了性能，AUC达到0.954。

Conclusion: 人工智能算法在银行欺诈识别中具有显著的有效性，堆叠泛化优于单一算法，表明集成策略能够进一步提升欺诈交易检测的准确性，该研究方向对数字社会中的金融安全具有重要意义。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [75] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流域检测与K-means聚类的无监督火区分割算法，用于替代传统均匀网格的离散化方式来进行野火预测。实验表明，在六个法国省份和六个预测模型上，火区分割方法一致优于网格方法，平均IoU提升3-6%，且计算轻量（<10秒），完全可并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野火预测模型普遍将研究区域离散化为均匀网格，忽略了火点异质的空间分布特征。作者认为数据的离散化方式比预测模型本身更重要，因此需要一种更加贴合历史火点空间分布规律的预测单元定义方法。

Method: 提出无监督火区分割算法，结合流域检测（watershed detection）与K-means聚类，直接从历史火灾模式中定义预测单元，替代传统均匀网格。实验在六个法国省份和六个不同预测模型上进行对比评估。

Result: 火区分割方法在所有测试条件下一致优于基于网格的方法，平均IoU提升3-6%（取决于空间尺度）；方法计算开销极低，每个配置运行少于10秒，且可完全并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化方式能够为短期野火预测带来显著且可复现的性能提升，这表明预测单元的空间划分策略应被视为与预测模型同等重要的设计因素。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [76] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全格局演变，提出基于多模态基础的安全威胁分类体系，分析了从单模态到多模态情境下威胁模型和安全假设的转变，并总结了最新的MLLM安全策略、开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通过模态对齐与融合实现了更强的理解与推理能力，但这一架构变革重塑了机器学习的安全格局。模型复杂性增加和跨模态交互引发新型安全威胁（如模态集成受损、模态错位、融合安全风险），这些威胁不再符合单模态假设下的威胁建模框架，而现有安全解决方案也难以覆盖新约束。因此需要系统性地重新审视和分析MLLMs的安全格局。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法：（1）提出多模态对齐的安全威胁分类体系（taxonomy），并分析威胁模型转变，涵盖对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱攻击和幻觉；（2）总结更新后的安全假设，并据此归类组织近期MLLM安全策略；（3）讨论开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Result: 构建了面向MLLMs的威胁分类体系，识别出模态集成受损、模态错位和融合安全风险等新型威胁类别；系统梳理了安全假设的更新以及对应的安全策略进展；指出了多模态系统需要更原则性和可扩展的安全机制的开放挑战。

Conclusion: MLLMs的安全格局已从单模态范式发生根本性转变，需要全新的威胁建模框架和更原则性、可扩展的安全机制来应对多模态交互带来的新型风险，未来研究应聚焦于解决当前安全策略在跨模态场景下的局限性。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [77] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将两种经典统计估计器适配到现代深度学习框架中，用于量化认知不确定性（模型不确定性）和偶然不确定性（数据内在噪声/局部数据稀缺），并借助近似的Fisher信息矩阵实现规模化扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽然性能强大，但缺乏对不确定性的量化能力。传统统计估计器虽然理论上成熟，但难以直接应用于大规模深度架构。需要一种方法能够在实际规模的深度模型上区分两种来源的不确定性——一是模型自身的认知不确定性，二是数据本身噪声或局部数据稀缺带来的偶然不确定性，从而提升模型的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: 本文的核心方法是将两种经典统计估计器进行改造，使其适配深度学习场景。关键创新在于利用近期在近似Fisher信息矩阵方面的研究进展，使得估计器能够扩展到实际规模的深度神经网络架构上，从而实现对认知不确定性和偶然不确定性的分别量化。

Result: 实验结果显示，每个测试点受两种不确定性来源的影响各不相同，这验证了所提出估计器的实际效用，表明该方法能够帮助改善真实世界应用的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文通过将经典统计估计器与深度学习相结合，实现了一种可扩展的不确定性量化方法，能够区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，为提升实际应用中的模型鲁棒性提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [78] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillConsist是一个用于检测LLM Agent Skill声明与实际实现行为不一致的系统。它通过LLM将声明与实现内容分离成行为记录并构建行为图，利用双向图对齐和图差分技术检测冲突。在包含319个不一致和314个一致的633-Skill基准上，包级检测F1达87.93%，比最优基线提升20.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill不一致可能暴露未披露的危险行为或导致错误的Skill选择。现有检测方法要么基于预定义类别评估行为或安全属性图，要么像PL-HCL那样用LLM学习元数据、指令和资源间的一致性。但存在两个关键挑战：(1)声明与实现行为可能混合在文本和代码中；(2)简洁的声明可能对应多个连接的实现步骤。这两个挑战导致现有方法难以准确检测不一致性。

Method: SkillConsist采用以下方法：(1)用LLM将声明和实现内容分离为两侧的行为记录，声明侧对应文本，实现侧对应代码，并结合静态分析补充实现记录；(2)在声明侧和实现侧分别构建行为图；(3)从任一侧的行为记录出发，进行双向图对齐，在另一图搜索候选子图并沿行为关系扩展，直到完整表达源侧行为；(4)通过图差分识别对齐子图间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 构建了633-Skill基准（来自ClawHub的500个最热门公共Skill和133个Skill-Inject包），包含319个不一致和314个一致的Skill，以及442个局部不一致标注。包级检测精度86.85%、召回率89.03%、F1 87.93%，F1较最优基线提升20.43个百分点；局部化检测精度67.60%、召回率58.14%、F1 62.52%。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过行为图双向对齐和图差分的方式，有效解决了声明与实现内容混合以及声明与多步骤实现间映射的挑战，在Agent Skill一致性检测上显著优于现有基线方法，验证了图对齐方法在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [79] [PhysAttNet: Enhancing Predictive Performance in Industrial and Astrophysical Time Series via Physics-Informed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07681)
*Amal Saadallah,Julia Tjus,Petra Wiederkeher,Wolfgang Rhode*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysAttNet，一种物理信息驱动的注意力框架，通过三种可微正则化项（对齐、平滑、稀疏）将物理结构先验引入轻量级CNN预测器，在铣削切削力预测和耀变体耀斑预报两个应用中显著提升了时序预测的准确性、泛化性和对关键事件的结构性预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理过程的时间序列预测要求模型在噪声、变异性和测量不确定性下保持可靠，同时捕捉具有物理意义的局部时间结构。虽然CNN因计算效率和表征能力强被广泛使用，但其学到的时序注意力模式往往不稳定或不具有物理一致性，降低了鲁棒性、泛化性和可解释性。

Method: PhysAttNet在轻量级CNN预测器上增强了一个受物理领域知识正则化引导的注意力头。训练时施加三种互补约束：对齐正则化（鼓励注意力遵循输入信号中平滑、峰值中心的时间结构）、平滑正则化（强制注意力随时间连续演化）和稀疏正则化（促进对信息丰富区间的选择性聚焦）。这些可微正则项在无需标注解释或人工监督的情况下引入物理引导的归纳偏置。

Result: 在两个不同应用（铣削切削力预测和耀变体耀斑时间序列预报）上的实验表明，PhysAttNet在预测准确性、泛化能力和对结构重要事件的预测性能方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: 物理信息正则化能够有效引导CNN的注意力机制产生更一致、可解释的时序表征，从而提高物理过程时间序列预测的准确性和鲁棒性，且无需额外的人工标注。

Abstract: Accurate and robust time series forecasting is essential in many applications involving physical processes, such as manufacturing monitoring and astrophysical event detection. In these settings, predictive models must remain reliable under noise, variability, and measurement uncertainty while capturing temporally localized structures corresponding to physically meaningful events. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for such tasks due to their computational efficiency and strong representational capacity. However, their learned temporal representations often exhibit unstable or physically inconsistent attention patterns, reducing robustness, generalization, and interpretability. This paper introduces PhysAttNet, a physics-informed attention framework for time series forecasting. PhysAttNet augments a lightweight CNN forecaster with an attention head guided by domain-informed regularization reflecting the structural properties of physical signals. Specifically, three complementary constraints are imposed during training: an alignment regularization that encourages attention to follow smooth, peak-centered temporal structures derived from the input signal, a smoothness regularization that enforces continuous temporal evolution, and a sparsity regularization that promotes selective focus on informative intervals. These differentiable regularization terms introduce physics-guided inductive bias without requiring annotated explanations or manual supervision. Experiments on two distinct applications, namely predicting cutting forces during milling and forecasting flares in blazar time series, demonstrate that PhysAttNet improves forecasting accuracy, generalization, and prediction performance on structurally important events.

</details>


### [80] [CODS: Iterative Bellman-Residual Data Selection for Reusable Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07719)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODS是一种评论家引导的离线强化学习数据子集选择方法，通过迭代训练算法匹配的评论家并获取高残差转移来冻结可复用子集。在10%预算下，CODS在20个有效D4RL任务-算法组合中保留了96.6%的合格池性能，在19/20个单元上超过ReDOR和OPER，且在整个跟踪扩展中保持高绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习从固定转移池反复训练策略，导致跨种子和超参数的数据冗余成本高昂；而朴素子采样可能移除长时程信用分配所需的稀有转移。现有方法（如优先回放）产生动态工件，一次性残差选择则无法随评论家变化刷新评分，因此需要一种能生成静态可复用子集的选择机制。

Method: CODS采用评论家引导的选择器，交替进行两阶段操作：一是拟合与算法匹配的评论家（algorithm-matched critic），二是获取高残差转移（high-residual transitions），最终冻结可复用的数据子集。与优先回放的单次运行不同，CODS在评论家变化时刷新残差分数，并支持多轮获取直到饱和。

Result: 在10%预算下，CODS在20个有效D4RL任务-算法组合中保留96.6%的合格池性能；在19/20个单元上优于ReDOR和OPER，在20/20上优于所有其他子集基线；所有6个子集优势在预声明的层级推断和Holm校正下仍显著。固定选择器更新次数时，5轮获取比1轮提升4个代表单元11.23分并趋于饱和。整个跟踪扩展保留95.4%的ALFWorld和96.5%的GSM8K率。

Conclusion: CODS是一种可复用的选择程序而非形式化的核心集（coreset）保证。等次数与等时间的评估证明其计算优势来源于数据复用而非单次运行加速。机制与破坏干预揭示了CODS既能有效富集稀疏奖励，也对外点（outliers）敏感。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning repeatedly trains policies from a fixed transition pool, making redundant data costly across seeds and hyperparameters, while naive subsampling can remove rare transitions needed for long-horizon credit assignment. We introduce CODS, a critic-guided selector that alternates between fitting an algorithm-matched critic and acquiring high-residual transitions before freezing a reusable subset. Unlike prioritized replay, CODS produces a static artifact; unlike one-shot residual selection, it refreshes scores as the critic changes. At a 10\% budget, CODS retains 96.6\% of eligible-pool performance across 20 valid D4RL task--algorithm cells. It exceeds ReDOR and OPER on 19/20 cells and every other subset baseline on 20/20; all six subset advantages remain significant under predeclared hierarchical inference with Holm correction. Holding total selector updates fixed, five acquisition rounds improve four representative cells by 11.23 points over one round and saturate thereafter. Equal-pass and equal-hour evaluations clarify that reuse, rather than a single-run speedup, creates the compute advantage. Mechanism and corruption interventions expose both useful sparse-reward enrichment and sensitivity to outliers. Finally, a whole-trace extension retains 95.4\% of pooled ALFWorld success and 96.5\% of pooled GSM8K exact match. CODS is therefore a reusable selection procedure, not a formal coreset guarantee.

</details>


### [81] [Neural Operators for Immersed-Boundary Soft Swimmers Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07722)
*Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Yizheng Wang,Navid Valizadeh,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于神经算子的代理模型，用于时序预测平面和三维鳗鱼游泳者周围的流体动力学场（速度、涡量、压力），在多个未见的高雷诺数轨迹上实现了3.5%-5.6%的全局相对L2误差（平面模型压力和三维模型压力误差分别为3.51%和19.2%），证明了可变形游泳体流场神经代理模型的可行性，同时指出压力精度和物理一致性是未来改进的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真浸没边界法模拟虽然能解析变形游泳体与其周围流场的耦合运动，但计算成本过高，限制了其在工程设计、参数研究和控制等需要反复评估场景中的应用。因此需要开发高效的代理模型来替代昂贵的仿真。

Method: 该方法使用神经算子作为代理模型，从自适应流固耦合模拟中导出的规则网格场进行训练。模型以游泳体几何形状和雷诺数为条件输入。平面模型联合预测两个速度分量、标量涡量和压力；三维模型采用三种目标专属模型共享多通道输入，分别预测三维速度、涡量和压力。

Result: 平面模型在5个未见高雷诺数轨迹上的全域全局相对L2误差为3.51%。三维模型在5个未见范围内轨迹上，速度预测误差为3.44%，涡量预测误差为5.58%，压力预测误差为19.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，场解析神经代理模型用于移动边界游泳体流场是可行的，但压力预测精度和物理一致性仍需进一步改进，是后续发展的优先方向。

Abstract: High-fidelity immersed-boundary simulation resolves the coupled motion of a deforming swimmer and its surrounding flow, but the resulting cost limits repeated evaluations for engineering design, parameter studies, and control. We develop neural-operator surrogates for temporal prediction of the hydrodynamic fields generated by planar and volumetric eel swimmers. The surrogates are trained on regular-grid fields exported from adaptive fluid--structure simulations and are conditioned on swimmer geometry and Reynolds number. The planar model jointly predicts two velocity components, scalar vorticity, and pressure. On five held-out high-Reynolds-number trajectories, its full-domain global relative L^2 error is 3.51 %. The volumetric formulation uses three target-specific models with a common multichannel input: one model predicts three-dimensional velocity, one predicts vorticity, and one predicts pressure. Their full-domain global relative L^2 errors on five held-out within-range trajectories are 3.44 %, 5.58 %, and 19.2 %. Together, the results demonstrate the feasibility of field-resolved neural surrogates for moving-boundary swimmer flows while identifying pressure accuracy and physical consistency as priorities for further development.

</details>


### [82] [Finite Constant Frontiers and Auditable Regret Certificates for Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07725)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种常数感知的比较协议，用于公平比较平均奖励强化学习中发布的下界与上界保证。作者构造了通信MDP的有限显式下界证书（二进制树状两状态块），通过精确的轨迹级Bernoulli KL散度证明了新的下界系数，将已有0.015的系数提升至0.0200（中等场景）至0.0291（更强条件下），提高了94%，极限系数为(1/32)√((A-3)/A)。同时给出了跨度约束乐观学习器的可审计组合规则用于上界，并形式化了期望转换与常数可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励强化学习的regret下界虽然已知到对数因子，但已发表保证的数值常数难以直接比较，因为概率模式、结构参数、对数归一化、先验信息和规划假设各不相同。缺乏统一的比较协议导致理论上界与下界之间的关系不透明，需要建立常数感知的对比框架。

Method: 引入常数感知比较协议（constant-aware comparison protocol）；构造二元树状两状态块结构的有限下界证书；证明中使用精确的轨迹级Bernoulli KL散度，并显式跟踪动作预算、直径、占用、导航成本和终止偏差；提出跨度约束乐观学习器的组合规则用于上界；并通过受控诊断实验测试直径依赖、bonus-width交互、跨度错误指定等因素。

Result: 构造的有限下界证书将已发表的系数0.015提升至0.0200（中等场景）和0.0291（更强动作、直径和视界条件下），提升幅度达94%；极限系数为(1/32)√((A-3)/A)。上界方面，给出了可审计的组合规则但未宣称具体系数，因为自适应方向方差和规划证书仍开放。

Conclusion: 本文提供了常数级精确的后悔下界证书和统一的比较协议，显著改善了下界系数的紧致性，并为上界提供了可审计但尚不完整的组合框架。同时形式化了有效期望转换和常数可比性，为后续在该领域的常数级比较建立了标准。

Abstract: Average-reward reinforcement-learning regret is known up to logarithmic factors, but the numerical content of published guarantees is difficult to compare because probability mode, structural parameter, logarithmic normalization, prior information, and planning assumptions differ. We introduce a constant-aware comparison protocol and derive an explicit finite lower certificate for communicating MDPs. The construction is a binary tree of two-state blocks; its proof uses exact trajectory-level Bernoulli KL divergence and keeps action budget, diameter, occupancy, navigation cost, and terminal bias explicit. A common closed-form envelope improves the published coefficient $0.015$ across a finite frontier: $0.0200$ in a moderate regime and up to $0.0291$ under stronger action, diameter, and horizon conditions, a $94\%$ increase. The limiting coefficient is $\frac1{32}\sqrt{(A-3)/A}$. For upper bounds, we give an auditable composition rule for a span-constrained optimistic learner, but do not claim a coefficient while adaptive directional-variance and planning certificates remain open. We also formalize valid expectation conversion and constant comparability. Controlled diagnostics test diameter dependence, bonus-by-width interactions, span misspecification, and the finite lower certificate on its exact family.

</details>


### [83] [LUCID: Latent-Skill Unified Control via Imagined Dynamics for Long-Horizon Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07746)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Angelo Cangelosi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID是一个分层模型强化学习框架，通过学习到的动力学模型进行想象展开来规划可复用的技能，解决长时程人形机器人移动操作任务中的复杂序列决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程人形移动操作需要组合多种全身技能并做出可靠的高层决策。现有方法通常依赖脚本化规划器、有限状态机或任务特定的无模型策略来协调预训练技能，这限制了处理复杂任务序列的能力。

Method: LUCID首先通过对抗模仿学习训练一个结构化潜在条件化的低层策略，然后将其冻结，同时联合学习一个高层策略和宏动力学世界模型。世界模型预测潜在决策引起的时间扩展状态转移，使高层策略能够通过想象展开进行优化。

Result: 在多种模拟多物体重排场景中，LUCID相比基线方法在完整任务成功率和部分完成率上均有提升，证明了其在复杂序列移动操作任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: LUCID通过分层模型强化学习与宏动力学世界模型相结合，在长时程人形移动操作任务中展现了优于现有方法的性能，验证了其作为一种通用框架在复杂序列任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires composing versatile whole-body skills and reliable high-level decision making. Existing methods often coordinate pretrained skills with scripted planners, finite-state machines or task-specific model-free policies, restricting their ability to handle complex task sequences. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{LUCID}, a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that plans over reusable skills through imagined rollouts of a learned dynamics model. LUCID first trains a structured latent-conditioned low-level policy via adversarial imitation and then freezes it while jointly learning a high-level policy and macro-dynamics world model. The world model predicts the temporally extended state transitions induced by latent decisions, enabling high-level policy optimization through imagined rollouts. We evaluate our framework across various simulated multi-object rearrangement scenarios. Experimental results show that LUCID improves the full-task success and partial-completion rates compared to prior baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex sequential loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [84] [From Benchmark Performance to Tool Deployment: Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07770)
*Mike Szklarzewski,CJ George,Gavin Smithson,Christopher Stokes,Dakota Fulp,William M. Jones,Benjamin Wynn,Alexander Ur,Agit Yesiloz,Clint Kallenbach,Mark Swartz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Sharmistha Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了19种无监督异常检测模型在具有挑战性的BowTie制造数据集上的表现，发现其性能远不如在MVTec AD等标准基准上的报告结果稳定，对预处理高度敏感且跨条件不一致。基于此发现，作者开发并初步部署了一个统一的人机协同检测框架，用于制造零件检测，结合图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测和集成验证引擎，取代了先前的人工目检与文档工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术基准上的异常检测模型通常报告良好性能，但缺乏对真实工业条件下模型行为（如反射表面、细微缺陷、轮廓特征差异）的深入理解。本文旨在揭示基准性能与实际部署之间的差距，并提供一个可落地的解决方案。

Method: 首先在BowTie制造数据集上系统评估19种无监督异常检测模型，考察其在不同条件下的稳定性和对预处理的敏感性，并进行共识审计以评估名义数据质量的影响。基于评估结果，设计并部署一个人机协同检测框架，包含四个核心组件：热力图引导的缺陷审查、SAM精炼候选区域供检查员接受/拒绝/边界调整、存在标注时的掩码评估、以及用于检查员一致性和培训的审查历史记录。

Result: 模型在BowTie数据集上的性能不稳定，对预处理高度敏感，跨条件表现不一致，没有单一方法表现出统一的鲁棒性；名义数据质量影响部署效果。所开发的统一人机协同框架已初步部署，替代了先前的人工目检和文档流程。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了基准性能与部署现实之间的显著差距，所提出的人机协同框架为弥合这一差距提供了实用的工业落地路径。

Abstract: Automated anomaly detection methods often report strong performance on curated academic benchmarks, but their behavior under real-world industrial conditions is less clear. In this work, we evaluate 19 unsupervised anomaly detection models on the BowTie dataset, a challenging manufacturing dataset with reflective surfaces, subtle defects, and profile-specific variation. In contrast to benchmark results, we observe that model performance is less stable than typically reported on standard benchmarks such as MVTec AD, highly sensitive to preprocessing, and inconsistent across conditions, with no single approach emerging as uniformly robust; a consensus audit further indicates that nominal-data quality affects deployment.
  Motivated by these findings, we developed and initially deployed a unified human-in-the-loop framework for manufactured-part inspection that combines image annotation, AI-assisted defect detection, and an integrated validation engine, replacing a prior manual visual inspection and documentation workflow. The system supports heatmap-guided defect review, SAM-refined candidate regions for inspector acceptance, rejection, or boundary adjustment, mask evaluation where annotations exist, and review history for inspector consistency and onboarding. Together, the results highlight the gap between benchmark performance and deployment reality, and provide a practical framework for addressing it.

</details>


### [85] [The Sample Complexity of Policy Learning with Mu-Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07772)
*Gene Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了μ-重置交互协议下基于策略的强化学习，解决了策略可实现性对该问题样本复杂度作用的开放问题，并揭示了复杂度对时间跨度H的依赖由重置分布的覆盖性概念决定：在全策略集中度有界时样本复杂度下限为exp(Ω(H))，在推前集中度有界时紧致刻画为exp(Θ(√H))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决KLS25提出的关于策略可实现性在μ-重置交互协议中作用的开放问题，明确该问题样本复杂度的精确界。

Method: 在μ-重置交互协议框架下，区分两种覆盖性假设（全策略集中度与推前集中度），分别建立样本复杂度的下界与紧的上界匹配分析。

Result: 在全策略集中度有界条件下证明exp(Ω(H))的样本复杂度下界；在推前集中度有界条件下证明H的指数依赖可紧致刻画为exp(Θ(√H))。

Conclusion: μ-重置协议下基于策略强化学习对时间跨度H的依赖由覆盖性假设决定，不同集中度假设导致截然不同的指数复杂度特征。

Abstract: We study policy-based reinforcement learning under the $μ$-resets interaction protocol of Kakade and Langford [KL02]. This interaction protocol enables the learner to sample trajectories from a given exploratory reset distribution $μ$, in addition to the starting distribution. We resolve the question raised by [KLS25] on the role of policy realizability for the sample complexity of this problem. Critically, the dependence on horizon $H$ is governed by the notion of coverage assumed of the reset distribution. Under bounded all-policy concentrability, we show a $\exp(Ω(H))$ sample complexity lower bound; with bounded pushforward concentrability, we show the dependence on horizon is tightly characterized as $\exp(Θ(\sqrt H))$.

</details>


### [86] [Shape Mutating Expert Compression:LorExperts and BTExperts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07814)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,Sourjya Roy,Bowen Bao,Thiago Crepaldi,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LorExperts是一种保留路由器的MoE压缩方法，通过将专家聚类、在每个簇中保留一个全精度主导专家、并用低秩校正表示其他成员，实现约50%的专家压缩而不牺牲精度，且相比D^2-MoE随专家数增加优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE语言模型虽然部署时每token计算量低，但压缩大量专家权重矩阵代价高昂。已有方法如REAP的专家剪枝/合并会损失精度并需要重训路由器；D^2-MoE的低秩delta分解虽然保留所有专家和路由器，但随着专家数量增加，单一共享组件无法逼近大量近正交专家，性能急剧下降。

Method: LorExperts的核心思想是：虽然MoE专家权重近似正交，但专家会组织成功能性的共激活社区（co-activation communities），这与权重相似性无关。基于此发现，LorExperts将专家聚类，每个簇保留一个全精度主导专家，其余成员用对局部主导专家的低秩校正来表示。该方法保留所有专家和原始路由器（无需重训路由器）。此外还提出重建微调过程和BTExperts——一种由主导和校正构成的树形组织，实现在推理时共享计算的摊还。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B和Gemma-4-26B-A4B上约50%专家压缩率下，LorExperts在大多数任务上比基线更好地保持下游准确率和困惑度；与D^2-MoE相比，其优势随专家数E的增加而扩大。

Conclusion: LorExperts通过利用专家间的功能性共激活社区结构，实现了无需路由器重训练的MoE高效压缩，在保持精度的同时显著优于现有方法，且其优势随专家规模扩大而增强。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models deliver high capacity at low per-token compute, but deploying them cheaply requires compressing their many expert weight matrices. Expert pruning (e.g., REAP) and merging reduce cost but sacrifice accuracy and require retraining the router; low-rank delta decomposition of experts (e.g., D^2-MoE) preserves all experts and the router, but degrades sharply as the expert count grows because a single shared component cannot approximate many near-orthogonal experts.
  Because MoE expert weights are near-orthogonal, a single shared component (as in prior delta decomposition) scales poorly with the expert count; we show that experts nonetheless organize into functional co-activation communities that are decoupled from weight similarity. Building on this, we introduce LorExperts, a router-preserving compression method that clusters experts, keeps one full-precision dominant per cluster, and represents the remaining members as low-rank corrections to their local dominant. LorExperts retains all experts and the original router (no router retraining). At ~50% expert compression on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, LorExperts preserves downstream accuracy and perplexity better than the baselines on most of the tasks; the margin over D^2-MoE grows with expert count E. We further give a reconstruction fine-tuning procedure for LorExperts, and BTExperts, a tree organization of dominants and corrections that enables inference-time amortization of shared computation.

</details>


### [87] [From token probabilities to calibrated confidence: An empirical study of mathematical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07827)
