*Avery Ma,Lorne Schell,Vin Bhaskara,Leila Pishdad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在数学问答任务中的置信度估计与校准问题。研究发现，虽然单个token概率容易过饱和，但聚合整个序列的token概率仍能捕捉正确与错误生成之间的细微但一致的差异，从而提供更有信息量的置信度估计。多轮方法（如自验证和Monte Carlo Dropout）能产生校准良好的置信度估计，而Platt scaling和isotonic回归两种后处理校准方法虽能大幅降低域内校准误差，但其数据效率随数据集难度变化，且校准映射在跨数据集和模型间的迁移具有不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM的token概率往往过度自信，但作者试图探究这些现成可得的信号是否仍能为数学问答提供良好校准的置信度估计，以填补置信度估计在该任务中的有效性研究空白。

Method: 对比了单遍估计器（复用原始生成的token概率）与多遍估计器（通过验证或随机前向传播获取额外置信度信号）。提出了两种多遍方法：通过重新提示进行自验证（含低成本原位变体）以及基于随机前向传播变异导出来自Monte Carlo Dropout的置信度。此外评估了Platt scaling和isotonic回归两种后处理校准方法。

Result: 聚合全序列token概率比单个token概率能提供更具信息量的置信度估计；多遍方法能达到校准良好的置信度估计；两种后处理校准方法均大幅降低域内校准误差，但其数据效率随数据集难度不同，且校准映射在跨数据集和模型迁移时表现不对称。

Conclusion: 即使token概率单独看容易饱和，通过全序列聚合仍可获得有意义的置信度信号；多遍策略和后处理校准各有优势，但校准方法的迁移性需要谨慎对待。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for large language models (LLMs) aims to estimate the probability that a generated answer is correct, while calibration aligns these estimates with empirical accuracy. Prior work has shown that token probabilities are often overconfident, we investigate whether these readily available signals can nevertheless provide well-calibrated confidence estimation for mathematical question answering. We compare single-pass estimators, which reuse token probabilities from the original generation, with multi-pass estimators, which obtain additional confidence signals through verification or stochastic forward passes. While individual token probabilities can be highly saturated, we find that aggregating token probabilities over the full sequence captures small but consistent differences between correct and incorrect generations, yielding more informative confidence estimates. Multi-pass methods can yield calibrated confidence estimates. We study two such approaches: self-verification through re-prompting, including a lower-cost in-situ variant, and Monte Carlo Dropout, which derives confidence from variation across stochastic forward passes. We further evaluate two post-hoc calibration methods, Platt scaling and isotonic regression, both of which substantially reduce in-domain calibration error. However, their data efficiency varies with dataset difficulty, and the calibration mappings often transfer asymmetrically across datasets and models.

</details>


### [88] [TEMPER: Tensorized Efficient Manifold-constrained Parameterization for Expressive Residual Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07851)
*Yuxuan Gu,Wuyang Zhou,Huijun Xing,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPER提出了一种张量化高效流形约束参数化方法，通过将残差路由中的生成器表示为基于张量网络的张量，在保持流形约束路由接口的同时大幅减少参数量（8个残差流时比mHC少84%附加参数），并在语言建模和常识推理任务上匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Hyper-connections (HC)及流形约束变体(mHC)虽然通过多残差流和动态信息流提升了残差路由的表达力，但其生成器（pre-branch聚合、残差混合、post-branch重分配）采用稠密、非结构化的参数化方式，导致参数量随残差流数量快速增长，形成生成器层面的瓶颈。

Method: TEMPER将残差路由中的生成器表示为跨输入流、特征和输出流三个模态的多路张量，并使用张量网络进行参数化。这种结构化低秩公式在保留token相关的流形约束路由接口的同时，通过张量秩控制学习到的路由子空间的维度（全秩可恢复稠密路由），并建立了生成器近似误差与路由logits及路由块输出差异之间的理论界。

Result: 在语言建模和常识推理任务上，TEMPER匹配或超越现有方法，同时大幅减少附加参数。在8个残差流设置下，TEMPER达到最佳CORE分数，同时比mHC少使用约84%的附加参数，展现出更强的性能-参数效率权衡。

Conclusion: TEMPER通过张量网络参数化残差路由生成器，有效解决了现有方法中参数量随残差流数量快速增长的问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升参数效率，并具备良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Residual connections rely on a static residual pathway, and are essential for training deep neural networks. Hyper-connections (HC) increase the expressivity of residual routing by incorporating multiple residual streams and learning dynamic information flow, while manifold-constrained (mHC) variants stabilize training through doubly stochastic residual mixing. However, a generator-level bottleneck remains in existing methods: they use dense, unstructured generators for pre-branch aggregation, residual mixing, and post-branch redistribution, which results in parameter count growing rapidly with the number of streams. To address this issue, we propose \underline{\textbf{T}}ensorized \underline{\textbf{E}}fficient \underline{\textbf{M}}anifold-constrained \underline{\textbf{P}}arameterization for \underline{\textbf{E}}xpressive Residual \underline{\textbf{R}}outing (\textbf{TEMPER}), which represents these generators as multi-way tensors over the input-stream, feature, and output-stream modes, and parameterizes them using tensor networks. Such a structured low-rank formulation is shown to preserve token-dependent manifold-constrained routing interface while substantially reducing parameter growth. It also promotes interpretability and intuition, as: i) tensor ranks control the dimensionality of the learned routing subspace, with full ranks recovering dense routing; while ii) the generator approximation errors bound differences in routing logits and, consequently, in the routed-block outputs. Comprehensive experiments show that TEMPER matches or outperforms existing methods across language modeling and commonsense reasoning tasks, while requiring substantially fewer additional parameters. At eight residual streams, TEMPER achieves the best CORE score while using about $84\%$ fewer additional parameters than mHC, thus showing a stronger performance-parameter efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [89] [CommitKV: Lifecycle-Aware KV Cache Compression via Commit Transitions for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07855)
*Weizhong Huang,Jinchao Zhang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommitKV通过识别键值（KV）缓存的"提交转换"生命周期来区分"暂时休眠"的信息与"已完成使命"的信息，在保证推理精度的前提下高效压缩多轮ReAct智能体的KV缓存，降低内存使用并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮ReAct智能体的KV缓存随轨迹累积而增长，导致内存和注意力计算成本上升。现有基于注意力分数的缓存压缩方法只关注当前轮的注意力，无法区分"暂时休眠"（未来可能再次重要）的信息与"已完成作用"的信息，导致不必要的缓存保留或错误压缩。

Method: CommitKV首先将已完成的智能体事件划分为token页，在工具调用提交前后分别测量每页的删除影响（成对测量）。基于此区分休眠页与高到低完成候选页。然后应用贪心联合测试，仅当候选页在提交后的联合影响保持有界时才接受其退役。在后续压缩检查点，排除已接受退役的页，保护等待提交后测量的有界页集，其余KV状态在缓存预算内以相同token索引（键、值、绝对位置）保留。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CommitKV减少了智能体内存使用，加速了端到端推理，并比现有KV缓存压缩方法获得更高的准确率。

Conclusion: CommitKV通过显式建模KV生命周期的"提交转换"机制，成功区分了休眠信息与完成作用的信息，实现了更精准、更高效的KV缓存压缩，在内存、速度和准确性上全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-turn Reasoning-and-Acting (ReAct) agents accumulate growing trajectories of reasoning, tool calls, and observations. Their key-value (KV) caches grow accordingly, increasing memory use and attention cost during model inference. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce these costs by evicting states with low attention scores. However, low attention in the current turn does not imply future irrelevance, as temporarily inactive information may become important later. Snapshot-based eviction methods therefore do not explicitly distinguish temporarily dormant information from information that appears to have completed its role. In this paper, we present CommitKV, which identifies KV lifecycles through commit transitions. Specifically, CommitKV first divides completed agent events into token pages and compares each eligible page's deletion effect before a tool-call commit and after the commit's returned observation has been incorporated. Based on these paired measurements, CommitKV distinguishes dormant pages from high-to-low completion candidates. It then applies a greedy joint test, accepting candidates for retirement only when their combined post-commit effect remains bounded. Finally, at a later compression checkpoint, accepted pages are excluded, a bounded set of pages awaiting post-commit measurement is protected, and the remaining KV states are retained within the cache budget using the same token indices for keys, values, and absolute positions. These mechanisms ensure that CommitKV can distinguish dormant information from information that has completed its observed role and can be safely removed. Experiments on various benchmarks show that CommitKV reduces agent memory use, accelerates end-to-end inference, and achieves higher accuracy than existing KV cache compression methods.

</details>


### [90] [Adaptive KappaSharp: Condition-Number Shaping for Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07859)
*Ketong Shao,Jialu Wang,Xuekai Pei,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KappaSharp方法，通过对Hessian矩阵进行对角修正（在不确定性高的方向施加更大修正）来解决偏好贝叶斯优化中PairwiseGP因比较图不连通导致的Hessian秩亏问题，并进一步提出自适应变体仅在代理模型对近期比较有把握时激活修正。在11个基准（5-20维）上优于标准PBO基线，最高提升+10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好贝叶斯优化（PBO）的标准方法PairwiseGP使用拉普拉斯近似时，EUBO采集函数生成的查询对不共享候选点，导致比较图产生孤立分量，使得似然Hessian矩阵秩亏（缺少一个自由度）。这种缺陷是结构性的，无法通过更换代理建模方式解决。现有方案要么浪费查询预算强制保持连通，要么使用均匀正则化同时扰动已被良好约束的方向。

Method: 提出KappaSharp方法，对Hessian施加对角修正以降低条件数，在先验不确定性更高的方向施加更大的修正。修正仅在模型拟合步骤应用，不影响查询选择。同时提出自适应变体，仅在代理模型对近期比较结果有信心时激活修正，避免在问题条件良好时进行不必要的修正。

Result: 在11个基准测试（5-20维）上，包括一个16维的等离子医学控制器调参问题，自适应KappaSharp优于标准PBO基线，最大提升达+10.9%（p=0.003）。

Conclusion: KappaSharp通过结构感知的对角Hessian修正有效解决了PBO中因比较图不连通导致的秩亏问题，其自适应变体在保持鲁棒性的同时提升了性能，在包括高维实际应用在内的多个基准上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) optimizes objectives accessible only through pairwise user comparisons. The standard approach fits a Gaussian process surrogate for observed pairwise comparisons (PairwiseGP) using the Laplace approximation and selects queries with the Expected Utility of Best Option (EUBO) acquisition function. EUBO queries new candidates at each step, producing pairs that share no candidates with previous queries. Each such pair forms an isolated component in the comparison graph, removing one degree of freedom from the likelihood Hessian and making it rank-deficient. This deficiency is structural and cannot be resolved by changing the surrogate modeling approach. Existing approaches to remedy this issue either waste query budget by forcing comparisons to stay connected, or apply uniform regularization that also perturbs directions already well-constrained by the observed comparisons. We propose KappaSharp that enables a diagonal correction to the Hessian to reduce its condition number, with larger corrections where the prior uncertainty is higher. The correction is only applied in the model fitting step, not query selection. An adaptive variant of KappaSharp is also presented that activates the correction only when the surrogate is confident about recent comparisons, avoiding unnecessary corrections when the problem is well-conditioned. On 11 benchmarks (5--20 dimensions), including a 16-dimensional controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline, with up to +10.9% ($p{=}0.003$).

</details>


### [91] [CONFER: Conflict-Aware Evidence Negotiation for Regime-Calibrated Weak Supervision in Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07867)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CONFER框架，一种基于图的冲突感知证据协商方法用于弱监督多模态情感识别，通过模态专家间的信念传播、不确定性量化和弱标签校准来应对自我报告标签不可靠和跨模态冲突问题，在AMIGOS、MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP数据集上取得先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别通常将自我报告标签视为可靠监督，忽视了标签不可靠性（如主观偏差、噪声）和跨模态冲突（各模态表达的信息不一致）这两个关键问题。现有方法缺乏对这两种挑战的系统性建模与处理框架。

Method: CONFER将每个模态专家建模为图中的一个节点，每个节点包含预测信念、基于边界的置信度不确定性，以及结合历史交叉验证表现和当前样本不确定性估计的运行时可靠性。通过不确定性感知的兼容性和可靠性导向的非对称边权重来调控迭代消息传递协商过程，再进行同伴支持的预测读出。根据冲突减少量、残余分歧和平均模态不确定性，将样本划分为共识（Consensus）、异议（Dissent）和模糊（Ambiguity）三种状态，用于样本特定的弱标签校准。

Result: 在AMIGOS、MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP数据集上，分别在subject-dependent 10折和严格的LOSO协议下评估。在严格LOSO评估下，AMIGOS-V达到0.873准确率，MAHNOB-V达到0.854准确率。高阶分析表明，高冲突样本上的协商收益更大，且对弱标签损坏的鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 跨模态冲突为方向性模态协调和监督可靠性估计提供了有用信息。CONFER验证了通过图协商机制利用冲突信号，既能提升多模态情感识别的性能，也能增强对不可靠弱标签的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition often treats self-reported labels as reliable supervision while overlooking self-report unreliability and cross-modal conflict. We propose \textbf{CONFER}, a graph-based conflict-aware evidence negotiation framework for weakly supervised multimodal emotion recognition. CONFER represents each modality expert as a node with a predictive belief, boundary-based uncertainty, and runtime reliability estimated from historical out-of-fold performance and current-sample uncertainty. Uncertainty-aware compatibility and reliability-directed asymmetric edge weights govern iterative message-passing negotiation, followed by peer-supported prediction readout. Conflict reduction, residual disagreement, and mean modality uncertainty further characterize three regimes---Consensus, Dissent, and Ambiguity---for sample-specific weak-label calibration. We evaluate CONFER on AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, and DEAP under subject-dependent 10-fold and strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocols. CONFER achieves competitive performance, reaching \textbf{0.873} accuracy on AMIGOS-V and \textbf{0.854} accuracy on MAHNOB-V under strict LOSO evaluation. Further analyses show larger negotiation gains on high-conflict samples and improved robustness to weak-label corruption, indicating that cross-modal conflict provides useful information for both directional modality coordination and supervision-reliability estimation.

</details>


### [92] [V-Simba: Unleashing the Architectural Potential of RL in Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07870)
*Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,Hojoon Lee,Johan Obando-Ceron,Byungkun Lee,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro,Jaegul Choo,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: V-Simba是一种简单而高效的视觉强化学习架构，受状态基RL的Simba架构启发，通过添加归一化层稳定训练并使用逐点卷积减少计算量，在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World基准上匹配或超越现有最先进方法，同时比DrQ-v2更计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本效率是强化学习的核心挑战，尤其在机器人等真实场景中数据采集成本高昂。视觉RL中高维输入掩盖学习信号，现有工作多集中于算法层面（如更好的动力学模型或探索策略），但状态基RL的最新进展表明，仅架构设计就能带来显著的样本效率提升。作者希望探究这些架构原则能否迁移到视觉RL中。

Method: V-Simba基于Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）和数据增强构建，受状态基RL中Simba架构启发，通过两项关键架构修改实现：一是添加归一化层以稳定训练，二是使用逐点卷积（pointwise convolutions）减少计算量。

Result: 尽管实现简单，V-Simba在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World基准上匹配或超越了最先进的视觉RL方法，并且在计算效率上优于DrQ-v2。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，状态基RL中的架构设计原则（如Simba）可以有效迁移到视觉RL领域，仅通过架构层面的简单修改即可在无需复杂算法创新的情况下显著提升样本效率，为视觉RL提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Improving sample efficiency remains a core challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially in real-world settings like robotics, where data collection is costly. This challenge is pronounced in visual RL, where high-dimensional inputs often obscure learning signals. While prior work in visual RL has focused on algorithmic solutions, such as better dynamics models or exploration strategies, recent advances in state-based RL show that architectural design alone can lead to significant gains in sample efficiency. This raises an important question: Can these architectural principles transfer to visual RL? In response, we introduce V-Simba, a simple yet effective visual RL architecture inspired by the Simba architecture from state-based RL. Built on top of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with data augmentation, V-Simba modifies the architecture by adding normalization layers to stabilize training and using pointwise convolutions to reduce computation. Despite its simplicity, V-Simba matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods across the DMC, Adroit, and Meta-World benchmarks, while being more computationally efficient than DrQ-v2. We make our code publicly available at https://github.com/DAVIAN-Robotics/V-Simba.

</details>


### [93] [Router Sensitivity Under Lightweight Fine-Tuning Identifies Prunable Experts in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07890)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Xinyi Luo,Wenyuan Shen,Owen Zou,Yankai Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探索了在轻量级微调（参数高效适配）下能否恢复专家修剪所需的路由器敏感性信号。研究表明，仅使用路由器LoRA（仅训练0.002%的参数）微调后，按ℓ2路由器变化量对专家排序并一次性修剪，在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct上可移除半数专家且性能优于全模块LoRA；信号质量随适配范围扩大而下降，而IA3（冻结路由权重）与直接路由器适配效果相当。修剪后的MMLU-Pro准确率随压缩度准线性衰减而非崩溃，最大压缩下仍约为基于幅度或随机修剪的1.8倍，同时减少49%内存和37%延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型将总参数量与每token计算量解耦，但部署时仍需存储所有专家。近期理论表明，在微调中对路由器范数变化最小的专家进行修剪可保持精度，但该假设基于全量微调。作者希望验证轻量级适配（参数高效微调）是否也能恢复这一修剪信号，使专家修剪在实际大规模场景中变得可行和实用。

Method: 使用路由器专用LoRA进行极简微调（仅训练约0.002%的参数），通过LoRA引入的ℓ2路由器变化量对专家进行排序，在单次（one-shot）操作中修剪变化最小的专家。实验对比了不同适配方式（路由器LoRA、全模块LoRA、IA3、无约束加性适配器）及不同LoRA秩对修剪信号质量的影响，并在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct和Qwen1.5-MoE上进行验证。

Result: 在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct上，路由器专用LoRA在移除半数专家后MMLU-Pro达27.54%，优于同秩的全模块LoRA（24.42%）；准确率随LoRA秩单调提升至28.76%。IA3（冻结路由权重）与直接路由器适配效果相当，而无约束加性适配器会降低信号质量。修剪后MMLU-Pro准确率准线性衰减，最大压缩时仍约为基于幅度或随机修剪的1.8倍，减少49%内存和37%延迟。在Qwen1.5-MoE数学微调场景中，移除半数专家后保留49.7%平均准确率，随机修剪则跌至个位数。

Conclusion: 轻量级微调（尤其是仅路由器LoRA）足以恢复有理论依据的专家修剪信号，使得在实际规模下进行有效的MoE专家修剪成为可能。路由器的敏感性信号质量取决于适配方式，专注于路由器参数的适配策略（路由器LoRA、IA3）效果最佳，为大规模MoE部署的内存和延迟优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models decouple total parameters from per-token compute, but deployment still requires storing every expert. Recent theory shows that pruning experts with the smallest router-norm changes during fine-tuning can preserve accuracy, but assumes full fine-tuning. We test whether lightweight adaptation can recover this signal. We briefly fine-tune with a parameter-efficient adapter, rank experts by the induced $\ell_2$ router change, and prune the least-changed experts in one shot. On Mixtral-8$\times$7B-Instruct (44.83% MMLU-Pro), router-only LoRA trains 0.002% of parameters and outperforms all-module LoRA at matched rank with half the experts removed (27.54% vs. 24.42%); signal quality declines as adaptation spreads to attention and expert weights. Accuracy improves monotonically with LoRA rank, reaching 28.76%. IA3, which leaves router weights frozen, matches direct router adaptation, whereas unconstrained additive adapters degrade the signal. Router-guided MMLU-Pro accuracy decays quasi-linearly rather than collapsing, remains nearly 1.8 times that of magnitude-based or random pruning at maximal compression, and reduces memory by 49% and per-token latency by 37%. At 25% compression, retention is competitive with methods using full activation statistics. The criterion also transfers to Qwen1.5-MoE fine-tuned for mathematics, retaining 49.7% mean accuracy over eleven benchmarks with half the experts removed while random pruning falls to single digits. Router sensitivity under lightweight fine-tuning therefore makes provably motivated expert pruning practical at scale.

</details>


### [94] [LLM-Based Embeddings for Program Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07894)
*Calvin Higgins,Marco Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将LLMCompiler（在大规模中间表示IR代码上预训练的LLM）产生的程序嵌入应用于程序分析与优化任务。通过简单的分块嵌入与聚合方法，结合源代码和IR代码嵌入，在算法分类任务中达到1.54%的错误率，较当前最优方法提升12%，并在异构设备映射上取得有竞争力的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大规模语言模型（尤其是针对IR代码预训练的LLM）在程序分析和优化中的潜力，特别是利用LLMCompiler这一专门在中间表示代码上预训练的模型来生成程序嵌入，以改善传统程序分析和优化任务的性能。

Method: 从源代码和IR代码中直接生成程序嵌入。具体方法为：将程序分割成多个块（chunks），使用预训练LLM分别对每个块进行嵌入，然后将各块的嵌入聚合成单一的程序级嵌入。该研究中使用的预训练LLM基于LLMCompiler，即一个在IR代码上进行大规模预训练的模型。

Result: 结合源代码和IR代码嵌入在算法分类任务中达到1.54%的错误率，比当前最先进方法提高了12%；在异构设备映射任务中取得有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合源代码和IR代码的程序嵌入在程序分析与优化任务中表现优异。训练一个性能感知的LLM用于IR代码嵌入，可能为代码优化任务带来最先进的结果。

Abstract: Recent advances have highlighted the potential of machine learning, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), for analyzing and optimizing programs. We present the first application of program embeddings from LLMCompiler---an LLM massively pretrained on intermediate representation (IR) code---to representative program analysis and optimization tasks. We generate program embeddings directly from source and IR code using a simple approach: split programs into chunks, independently embed each chunk with pretrained LLMs, and then aggregate the chunk embeddings into a single program embedding. Our experiments show that combining source and IR code embeddings achieves an error rate of 1.54\% in algorithm classification, a 12\% improvement over the current state-of-the-art, and a competitive accuracy on heterogeneous device mapping. These findings suggest that training a performance-aware LLM for embedding IR code might yield state-of-the-art results in code optimization tasks.

</details>


### [95] [When Does Trace-Driven Evaluation Mislead MoE Expert Caching? Replay Semantics, Workload Contamination, and Operating Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07911)
*Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出MoE专家缓存管理评估中测量方法十分脆弱，识别了三个会改变结论（而非仅数值）的评估轴线：重放语义、工作负载污染和运行体制。修正后，与离线最优解之间仍存在稳定的44.2-45.9%的差距，但该差距大部分由"未来最远使用的驻留专家"这一信息驱动，而代表性轻量级因果机制仅能恢复很小一部分差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型已超出加速器内存，将专家权重卸载到主机内存已成为标准做法，这使得专家缓存管理成为重要的优化杠杆。然而，评估缓存管理策略的测量方法本身存在缺陷，不同评估方式会得出截然不同的结论，因此需要系统地识别和修正这些测量问题。

Method: 使用基于轨迹驱动、事件原子级的模拟器，对三个MoE模型（40、64、128个专家）进行系统评估。作者隔离了三个评估轴线：(1) 重放语义：在融合事件流量契约下，不一致的逐访问重放会使基于频率和静态策略的结论偏差达27-29%；(2) 工作负载污染：使用每个类别单一指令模板的探测集产生逐字相同的生成前缀，匹配对渲染干预可使早期窗口效应移动19.4-31.9个百分点；(3) 运行体制：归一化未命中分数无法跨模型迁移，仅置换事件流的时间顺序即可使离线最优差距从44.9%变为30.8%。

Result: 修正后，在13个冻结工作负载组合上，与离线最优的差距稳定在44.2-45.9%。强制准入预言机显示84.3-96.6%的差距来自"知道哪个驻留专家在未来最远被使用"。因果性下次使用预测器作为驱逐规则仅恢复-11.4%的差距；最优受害者选择率仅3.4%（随机驻留块为2.4%，LRU和LFRU为20.6-22.1%）。

Conclusion: 作者的立场是狭义的：在所评估的设置中，较大的离线最优解差距基本上高估了代表性轻量级因果机制所能恢复的收益。评估方法学必须纠正重放语义、工作负载污染和运行体制三个维度的偏差，否则会得出相反的结论。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have outgrown accelerator memory, and offloading expert weights to host memory is now standard. This makes expert cache management an attractive lever: a policy that raised the hit rate would cut expert traffic per token. Evaluating that is a measurement problem, and we find the measurement fragile.
  With a trace-driven, event-atomic simulator over three MoE models (40, 64, 128 experts), we isolate three evaluation axes that change conclusions, not just numbers. Replay semantics: under a fused-event traffic contract, an inconsistent per-access replay inflates recency-based policies by 27-29% while leaving frequency-based and static ones within 4%, inverting the policy ranking. Workload contamination: probe sets using one instruction template per category produce verbatim-identical generation prefixes; a matched-pair rendering intervention moves the measured early-window effect by 19.4-31.9 points and reverses which workloads look most cache-friendly. Operating regimes: normalized miss fractions do not transfer across models, so the per-step expert union relative to per-layer capacity must be reported -- yet permuting only the temporal order of an identical event stream moves the offline-optimal gap from 44.9% to 30.8%, so it is not sufficient.
  Corrected, a stable gap to the offline optimum remains (44.2-45.9% over 13 frozen workload compositions). A forced-admission oracle attributes 84.3-96.6% of it to knowing which resident expert is used furthest in the future. A causal next-use predictor, used as an eviction rule, recovers -11.4% of the gap; it picks an optimal victim 3.4% of the time, against 2.4% for a random resident block and 20.6-22.1% for LRU and LFRU. Our position is narrow: in our evaluated settings a large offline-optimal gap substantially overstates the gains recovered by representative lightweight causal mechanisms.

</details>


### [96] [ZeroLock: Concurrent Memory-Efficient LLM Training via Modular Update Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07974)
*Wentao Dai,Xuanran Li,Yuxiang Zhang,Ming Tang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZeroLock，一种无反向传播（BP-free）的边缘端 LLM 微调算法，通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的块更新，打破 BP 的更新锁定，从而在算法层面提升吞吐量并降低激活存储带来的内存开销。论文首次为这类基于局部目标构造的方法在通用模型分块下提供了理论框架，证明了其收敛率为 O(1/√T)（与 BP 仅差多对数因子），并实现了包含提前转发和故障恢复等技术的真实原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端 LLM 微调需要适配场景数据并保护隐私，但边缘设备内存和算力有限。现有工作虽提出流水线并行来解决资源约束，却普遍依赖反向传播（BP）训练，BP 存在更新锁定（update locking）这一根本性缺陷，导致严重的吞吐量和内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出 ZeroLock 算法，通过构建局部目标（local objective）将模型更新解耦为独立的块更新（chunk updates），从而打破 BP 的更新锁定。论文建立了将局部目标映射到全局目标的理论框架，适用于通用模型分块，并证明其收敛率为 O(1/√T)。此外设计了配套系统，集成提前转发（early forwarding）和故障恢复（failure recovery）技术，并构建了真实原型。

Result: 在真实原型上的实验表明，与基于 BP 的基线相比，ZeroLock 将内存占用降低了 26.5%，吞吐量提升了 4.9%。理论方面证明了收敛率与 BP 仅差多对数因子。

Conclusion: ZeroLock 提供了一种 BP-free 的边缘端 LLM 微调方案，在维持近似 BP 收敛性能的同时显著降低内存占用并提升吞吐量，为资源受限边缘场景下的模型微调提供了新的思路和理论支撑。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning at the edge adapts the model to scenario-specific data while preserving privacy. Although existing studies proposed pipeline parallelism to address the limited memory and computing resources of edge devices, they commonly rely on backpropagation (BP) training, which has a fundamental limitation of update locking and could experience severe throughput and memory bottlenecks. In this work, we propose a BP-free algorithm, called ZeroLock, that decouples the model updates into independent chunk updates by local objective construction. It breaks the update locking of BP and hence can improve throughput at the algorithm level and lower memory usage by reducing activation storage. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first theoretical framework for such local objective construction-based approaches under general model chunk division by mapping local objectives to the global objective. We prove that ZeroLock has a convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$, which differs from BP only by polylogarithmic factors. We design a system for ZeroLock and build real-world prototypes, incorporating techniques such as early forwarding and failure recovery for efficient and robust implementation. Experiments on the prototype show that compared to BP-based baselines, ZeroLock reduces the memory by 26.5% and improves throughput by 4.9%.

</details>


### [97] [SPECTRA: Pushing the KV Cache Beyond the 2-Bit Cliff via Spectral Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07915)
*Jiamu Zhang,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Hanjie Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPECTRA是一种免训练的KV缓存压缩编解码器，通过将缓存旋转到由其自身统计量计算的坐标系中消除通道间相关性，将比特预算集中在少数承载主要信息的通道上，突破了2比特量化瓶颈，在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B长上下文基准上实现4倍近无损压缩、8倍竞争性压缩，最高可达12倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长上下文中推理时，KV缓存占据主要GPU内存，且随上下文长度线性增长。现有量化压缩方法将每个值降到低精度，但在约2比特处出现质量急剧下降的"2比特悬崖"：离群值过多的通道使少数大值消耗了量化层级，其余值被压缩成噪声。单纯按通道分配更多比特无法奏效，因为原始缓存中通道间高度相关，没有哪个通道天然更重要。

Method: SPECTRA首先分析发现，将KV缓存旋转到由其自身统计量计算出的坐标系后，通道间的相关性被消除，此时极少数通道承载了几乎所有信息。基于此，SPECTRA将该坐标变换作为编解码器，将比特预算集中分配给携带主要信号的少数通道，同时减少对冗余通道的分配。该方法无需训练，可直接插入现有推理流程。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的长上下文基准上，SPECTRA在4倍压缩时接近无损；在均匀量化已失效的8倍压缩下仍具竞争力；最高可达12倍压缩，使可用压缩率超越2比特悬崖，让同一GPU能够容纳更长的上下文和更大的批次。

Conclusion: 通过旋转坐标系消除KV缓存通道间的相关性，可以揭示真正重要的维度，从而以非均匀比特分配突破均匀量化的精度瓶颈，将可用KV缓存压缩率从约2比特/值提升至12倍，显著扩展LLM在长上下文场景下的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly read long inputs in the agentic era, from whole documents and codebases to conversations across many turns. Their inference memory is then dominated by the key-value (KV) cache, the stored attention keys and values of every token the model has read and generated. Because the cache grows with context length and is re-read in full at every generated token, a longer context means more GPU memory.
  To reduce this cost, most existing methods compress the KV cache by lowering every stored value to the same low precision, a technique known as quantization. They can push this to nearly two bits per value, but rarely further, because quality drops sharply at this 2-bit cliff: four levels are too few for the cache's outlier-heavy values, where a few large entries consume the levels and collapse the rest into noise. A natural remedy is to spend more bits on the channels (feature dimensions) that matter and fewer on the rest, but the raw cache offers no handle: its channels are strongly correlated, so none stands out as more important.
  Our analysis shows that this handle appears once the cache is rotated into a coordinate system computed from its own statistics, removing these correlations. There, a small fraction of channels carries almost all the information, and spending the budget on those few is far more accurate than spreading it evenly.
  Guided by this analysis, we develop SPECTRA, a training-free, drop-in codec that re-encodes the cache into this coordinate system and concentrates the bit budget on the channels that carry the signal. On Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B over long-context benchmarks, SPECTRA is near-lossless at 4x compression, competitive at 8x where uniform quantization has collapsed, and reaches up to 12x, pushing usable compression past the 2-bit cliff so the same GPU holds longer contexts and larger batches.

</details>


### [98] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一种基于学习的SLO感知自适应检索策略，通过动态选择每个查询的检索段落数量k来平衡尾部延迟与答案质量，在保持最小质量损失的同时显著提升SLO合规率并降低检索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统受严格的SLO约束（尾部延迟和基础设施成本），但标准检索管线使用固定检索预算，忽略查询难度差异——简单查询过度检索，困难查询检索不足，迫使运维人员在答案质量与SLO合规之间艰难取舍。

Method: SAGE利用初始检索得到的轻量级特征（如分数分布、排名间隙、词法信号），通过离线模仿学习从近似最优延迟-质量权衡的oracle策略中训练，推理时无需额外LLM调用且开销极小。

Result: 在Natural Questions上，在5秒P95延迟SLO下，SAGE达到95%的SLO合规率（最佳静态基线k=20仅为30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，仅损失2个百分点的EM分数。单一策略可跨HotpotQA、UnSeenTimeQA和四个LLM家族（Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Gemma）泛化，一致获得+45-52点的SLO改进且无质量下降。

Conclusion: SAGE证明学习驱动的自适应检索预算选择是可行的：通过模仿学习将oracle的延迟-质量权衡知识压缩为轻量级策略，不仅能在单一数据集上显著优于静态基线，还能跨数据集和LLM家族泛化，为生产环境RAG系统的SLO管理提供了务实有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [99] [Spectral Outliers Reveal Dominant Learned Structure in Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07921)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva,T. H. Bandara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文应用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力权重进行谱分解，将每个投影矩阵分离为随机噪声部分（bulk）和谱异常值（signal），并通过因果实验验证了该分解的有效性，揭示了多个跨模型一致的结构性规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预训练Transformer中注意力权重的内在结构，特别是区分哪些权重分量承载了真实的学习信号、哪些只是随机噪声。这有助于解释模型泛化能力的来源，并为参数高效微调和结构化剪枝提供理论依据。

Method: 1）应用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力投影矩阵（Q、K、V、O）进行谱分解；2）将每个投影矩阵分解为随机状bulk部分和谱异常值（outliers）集合；3）通过因果消融实验验证分解有效性——将MP识别出的异常值置零与将同等数量的bulk奇异值置零作对比；4）在11个预训练Transformer模型上跨模型分析，识别重现的结构性模式。

Result: 1）在Mistral-7B上，将MP识别的异常值置零可使HellaSwag、MMLU和PIQA性能跌至接近随机猜测水平；2）将同等数量的bulk奇异值置零仅造成较小但不可忽略的性能下降；3）跨11个模型发现五个重现模式：谱异常值编码了大部分学习结构；Q投影含最多异常值；GQA中的V投影缺乏清晰的信号/噪声分离；entry-level异常值在Q中形成行带、在O中形成列带结构；特定残差流维度在K和O的多个层间持续作为带状异常值存在。

Conclusion: MP谱分解为理解预训练注意力权重的信号/噪声结构提供了有效的因果验证框架，所得结构性发现可为参数高效微调（针对信号分量）和结构化剪枝（针对噪声分量）提供指导。

Abstract: We apply Marchenko-Pastur (MP) random matrix theory to pre-trained attention weights in order to separate each projection matrix into a random-like bulk and a set of spectral outliers. We validate this decomposition causally: zeroing the MP-identified outliers (signal) in Mistral-7B drives HellaSwag, MMLU, and PIQA close to random-chance performance, whereas zeroing a count-matched subset of bulk singular values causes smaller but non-negligible degradation. Across 11 pre-trained transformers we identify five recurring patterns: spectral outliers encode a dominant component of the learned structure; Q projections carry the most outliers; V projections under grouped-query attention lack a clean signal/noise separation; entry-level outliers form structured row-bands in Q and column-bands in O; and specific residual-stream dimensions persist as band outliers across layers in K and O. We close by outlining how these observations could inform parameter-efficient fine-tuning and structured pruning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将（计算）论证作为评估式AI（EAI）的正式、可计算基础，以实现可解释、可质疑的EAI系统，并为面向分布式和人本化的EAI研究议程奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估式AI（EAI）作为一种新兴的人机决策支持方式，不是给出单一推荐，而是呈现竞争性假设及其正反证据。然而，EAI缺乏正式的可计算基础，需要一种能够支持可解释性与可质疑性的形式化框架。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的形式，论证（计算）论证（computational argumentation）作为EAI形式化基础的适用性，并勾勒面向分布式与人本化EAI系统的长期研究议程。

Result: 提出将计算论证作为EAI的形式化与可计算基础，为可解释、可质疑的EAI提供理论支撑，并指出未来分布式、人本化EAI系统的研究方向。

Conclusion: 计算论证是EAI的自然且合适的理论基础，能够支撑可解释性与可质疑性的需求，有望推动分布式、人本化的EAI系统发展。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [101] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Flow-by-Flow"治理范式，用于应对高损失场景下AI输出速度超过人类认知能力的监督困境。核心观点是真正约束不是AI输出速度V本身，而是V×L（每项认知负荷），且AI能力提升并不能降低L，反而会重组其结构。Flow-by-Flow通过基于形式化可计数特征的认知成本评分和机构容量上限，在不评估内容的前提下控制监督负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，当AI输出速度V超过人类认知容量C_max时，人在回路的监督在结构上不可行。然而关键约束并非V本身而是V×L。作者指出能力提升对L的三个组成部分（分诊、判断、响应）影响不对称：分诊成本不随模型能力提升而下降（语义不确定性固有问题），响应成本不受准确性改进影响，只有判断成本面临下行压力且常通过遗漏实现而非真正降低。因此需要新的治理范式。

Method: 提出Flow-by-Flow治理范式：(1) 基于形式化可计数特征的认知成本评分，对高产量生产施加非线性成本；(2) 机构容量上限将处理量维持在C_max内。推导出四条设计不变量：无内容判断、不可规模化消耗审查能力、身份绑定的每应用摩擦、无批量放行。通过一个参考实现证明这些不变量可联合满足，并使用1,000次参数抽取的蒙特卡洛分析验证复合多指标流量控制优于单纯监督强化。

Result: 蒙特卡洛分析显示，在1,000次参数抽取中，复合多指标流量控制策略在90.8%的试验中优于单纯监督强化。参考实现表明四条设计不变量可以联合满足，但作者也明确承认了实际实施中的困难。

Conclusion: 能力提升不会降低监督负荷，只会重组其结构。基于内容正确性评估的治理机制要么委托AI（继承幻觉风险）要么委托人类（遭遇V×L上限）。Flow-by-Flow通过绕过内容判断来控制监督负荷，为高损失AI领域的治理提供了新路径，但其实际可行性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [102] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个形式化框架，用于从多元结构理论中构建典范解释。将结构理论定义为三元组(签名、公理、推理策略)，区分闭包稳定化、全局完备化和确定化三个典范化层次，分类不确定性为认知多元性(Type E)与结构多元性(Type S)，并给出两种典范化机制(算子式完备化与选择式构造)的存在性条件。该框架还应用于LLM辅助推理中幻觉问题的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元结构理论中，如何统一且规范地选择解释是一个基础性问题。现有方法缺乏对不确定性类型的形式化分类，也未能系统刻画典范化的层次与机制，因此需要构建一个统一的形式化框架来解决解释选择的规范性问题，并拓展到LLM推理中幻觉现象的建模。

Method: 提出结构理论T=(Σ,A,I)的三元组形式化框架；定义许可解释族作为所有全局一致性结构结论的集合；将典范化划分为三个层次(闭包稳定化、全局完备化、确定化)；将不确定性分类为Type E(认知多元性)与Type S(结构多元性)及其强子类Type S-strong(以共同上界缺失为特征)；提出两种典范化机制：算子式完备化与选择式构造，给出纯推理式完备化归结为饱和闭包算子的充分条件；通过分段算子证明多级典范化的结构非交换性；给出条件分类定理将理论内在机制归结为闭包或选择。

Result: 证明了在正、非内缩规则下纯推理式完备化归结为饱和闭包算子(需要附加可靠性条件)；Type E理论满足闭包稳定化，完全确定化依赖一个尚未解决的全局合流性质；Type S-strong理论通过典范选择实现确定化；多级典范化表现为结构非交换的分段算子系统；任务II类型的LLM推理中，幻觉可建模为不支持典范化的结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为多元结构理论提供了系统化的典范化方法论，区分了不确定性类型与典范化层次，并建立了闭包与选择两种机制的充分条件。全局合流性质(Type E完全确定化)仍为开放问题。该框架为LLM推理中的幻觉现象提供了形式化的理论视角，将其视为不可支持典范化。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [103] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的主动推断驾驶模型，将情感状态（效价和唤醒度）纳入连续状态空间中，通过同时基于当前状态和预测未来结果来估计情感信号，并在两个交互驾驶场景中验证了其与真实情感模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推断驾驶模型未考虑情感状态这一影响交通决策的重要因素，而先前在非交通领域中的情感建模仅限于离散状态空间的简化设置，无法适用于连续状态的复杂驾驶场景。

Method: 提出一种扩展的效价和唤醒度公式，可从具有连续状态的复杂主动推断驾驶模型中提取情感信号。关键在于情感估计不仅以当前状态为条件，还考虑预测的未来结果，从而在交互式驾驶环境中产生更丰富的情感表征。

Result: 在两个交互驾驶场景中评估了所提出的方法，结果显示生成的情感信号与相似场景中报告的真实情感模式相符。

Conclusion: 该工作将主动推断框架下的情感建模从简化的离散状态空间扩展到连续的驾驶场景，证明了结合未来预测的条件化情感估计能够有效捕捉驾驶中的情感动态，为更符合人类行为的自动驾驶决策提供了新思路。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [104] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Data-Centric Parallel (DCP)，一种以数据为中心的并行训练范式，通过根据每个batch的序列长度动态调整运行时设置（如并行大小、梯度累积、重计算），突破了变长序列训练中效率与易用性之间的困境，在32块H200 GPU上实现高达2.88倍加速，且仅需10行代码即可集成到任意模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变长序列的深度学习训练存在计算挑战。现有的静态配置方法会导致工作负载不均衡和低效率，而复杂的方法又引入了显著的实现复杂度和代码改动。作者希望打破效率与易用性之间的权衡。

Method: DCP的核心原则是"让数据本身驱动运行时"。它根据每个batch的序列长度动态调整直接运行时设置，包括并行大小（parallel size）、梯度累积（gradient accumulation）和重计算（recomputation）等参数，从而使运行时配置与数据特征自适应匹配。

Result: 在32块H200 GPU上实现了最高2.88倍的加速比。该方法设计用于泛化，可以仅通过10行代码集成到任何模型中。

Conclusion: 作者期望这种简单而有效的方法能够作为稳健的基线，促进分布式训练中变长序列处理的未来进展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [105] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 线性探针能以近乎完美的准确率检测语言模型中的上下文损坏，但无法可靠预测最终答案的正确性，形成"知道但不说"的认知与行为分离。该模式在多跳算术链、推理模型等不同设置下普遍存在；探针基于的干预措施高度依赖模型和错误类型，任何单一干预都无法全面主导，实际部署需要模型感知和错误类型感知的路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针检测上下文损坏的准确性很高，但这是否能转化为可靠的故障预测仍不清楚。作者旨在探究探针信号与最终答案正确性之间的关联，以评估探针在部署监控中的实际价值。这一分离现象对AI系统的可靠监控具有直接的实际意义。

Method: 在跨模型家族（包括推理模型）的多跳算术链上实施预注册实验，包括：(1) 线性探针检测上下文损坏；(2) 强制结构化置信度输出格式；(3) 检验跨跳探针持续性的"持久性胜过峰值"假设；(4) 比较多种干预策略（branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）在实时监控中的效果。

Result: 探针检测损坏近乎完美但不区分答案正误；结构化置信格式崩溃为两个值且错误率无差异；探针持续性不能区分正确与错误结果，否定了预注册假设。干预效果上，branch-and-pick在所有模型中净正面且在Llama-3.1-8B上唯一不破坏模型（4挽救、0破坏），而reprompt和replace-prior在挽救错误答案的同时几乎同等比例地破坏正确轨迹。

Conclusion: 探针监测是口头置信度表达的必要补充，但没有任何单一干预在所有情境下占优。可部署的解决方案是基于模型的、基于错误类型的路由策略，而非追求通用干预。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [106] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NL2SHACL-Bench，一个用于自然语言到SHACL翻译的基准测试套件，并基于该基准评估了四个SOTA大语言模型，发现当前LLM能生成语法有效的SHACL，但在复杂逻辑和结构模式的语义等价约束生成上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL是RDF知识图谱验证的核心技术，但编写SHACL形状需要专业技术知识，领域专家通常缺乏该能力。将自然语言需求翻译为SHACL（NL2SHACL）可以降低这一门槛。然而，目前不存在专门的NL2SHACL基准，且生成形状的评估需要超越字符串比较的方法，因为语义等价的形状可能在序列化和结构上不同。

Method: 构建NL2SHACL-Bench基准测试套件，用于自然语言到SHACL翻译任务的评估；利用该基准评估四个SOTA大语言模型在NL2SHACL任务上的表现。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前LLM在生成语法有效的SHACL方面表现高度出色，但在复杂逻辑和结构模式下生成语义等价的约束方面仍有困难。这表明NL2SHACL-Bench为衡量NL2SHACL技术进展提供了有意义的基础。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench填补了NL2SHACL领域缺乏专门基准的空白，并通过超越字符串比较的评估方法验证了其有效性。当前LLM在NL2SHACL任务上仍有提升空间，尤其在与复杂语义等价相关的约束生成方面。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多智能体LLM系统中的智能体选择与通信结构建模为合作博弈，权衡任务收益与智能体激活/通信成本，提出边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器，并从理论上联系子模最大化问题给出近似保证。实验表明贪心路由在合成场景下达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均仅激活8个智能体中的1.96个，优于全广播的38.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体AI系统组合多个异构LLM智能体，但现有架构要么预先固定通信拓扑，要么允许全广播。两者都会导致令牌成本、延迟、冗余和错误传播随活跃智能体数和通信链路数增长而急剧增加。因此需要一个自适应的、任务条件化的智能体选择和通信优化机制。

Method: 将智能体选择和通信建模为条件于任务的合作博弈，净效用U(C|x)=V(C|x)-Σ_{i∈C}c_i，区分联盟级成本与智能体激活成本。提出边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器；扩展模型以优化通信边（每条边含独立成本）；使用估计的Shapley值在任务执行前和执行中预测哪些智能体值得联系。理论上将其连接至子模最大化，证明曲率修正的近似界（单调、基数约束情形）以及通过双重贪心得到的紧1/2近似（无约束非单调情形，含符号目标修正）。另证明Shapley-子模三明治界，将边际价值路由误差关联到逐智能体边际递减量。

Result: 合成实验中，贪心路由达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均激活8个中的1.96个智能体，全广播仅达38.8%。对激活成本和冗余权重鲁棒，但在子模性被严重违反或价值估计含噪时性能降至66%。

Conclusion: 该框架区别于Shapley定价、hedonic联盟形成和通信图剪枝等现有方法，提出在真实多智能体LLM基准上进行评测。主路由算法目前仍是启发式方法，两个理论近似保证不直接适用于主路由器，但提供了理论支撑和特例分析。

Abstract: Modern agentic AI systems combine multiple large language model agents with heterogeneous skills, yet most architectures either fix communication in advance or allow full broadcast. Both can be inefficient because token cost, latency, redundancy, and error propagation increase with the number of active agents and communication links. We model agent selection and communication as a cooperative game with task-conditioned net utility $U(C\mid x)=V(C\mid x)-\sum_{i\in C}c_i$, separating coalition-level costs from agent activation costs. We propose a marginal-value activation rule and greedy router, extend the model to optimize communication edges with per-edge costs, and use estimated Shapley values to predict which agents are worth contacting before and during execution. We connect the problem to submodular maximization and prove two limited guarantees: a curvature-refined bound for a monotone, cardinality-constrained special case, and a tight $1/2$-approximation, with a correction for signed objectives, for an unconstrained non-monotone case via double greedy. Neither guarantee applies directly to the main router, which remains a heuristic. We also prove a Shapley-submodularity sandwich bound linking the error of marginal-value routing to a per-agent diminishing-returns quantity. In synthetic experiments, greedy routing achieves $99.5%$ of brute-force-optimal utility while activating $1.96$ of $8$ agents on average, compared with $38.8%$ for full broadcast. Performance is robust to activation cost and redundancy weight but falls to $66%$ under strong violations of submodularity or noisy value estimates. We distinguish the framework from Shapley pricing, hedonic coalition formation, and communication-graph pruning, and propose evaluation on real multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [108] [ArchAgent v2: A Case Study with the Data Prefetching Championship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09874)
*Abraham Gonzalez,Raghav Gupta,Akanksha Jain,Hanna Alam,Alexander Novikov,Po-Sen Huang,Matej Balog,Marvin Eisenberger,Sergey Shirobokov,Ngân Vũ,Hank Levy,Borivoje Nikolić,Sagar Karandikar,Martin Dixon,Parthasarathy Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ArchAgent v2，一个将自动化微架构搜索扩展到多级数据预取（prefetching）的框架，通过级联进化搜索和硬件可实现性反馈循环，在DPC4竞赛规则下自动设计出三级预取器，性能超越人工设计的冠军方案BertiGO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在算法设计自动化方面已展现潜力，但扩展到计算机微架构发现仍面临搜索空间巨大、硬件预算严格和仿真耗时长的挑战。原始ArchAgent能够发现单级缓存替换策略，但无法扩展到多级预取场景，因为该设计空间的自由度和复杂度更大。

Method: ArchAgent v2引入两项关键创新：(1) 级联进化搜索（cascaded evolutionary search），通过逐一进化并冻结各缓存级别的预取器来细分设计空间；(2) 硬件可实现性反馈循环，将实时面积/规模估计直接嵌入进化过程。在DPC4相同规则下对三级预取器进行自动搜索设计。

Result: ArchAgent v2自动设计的策略在几何平均IPC上相比基线获得3.8%加速，比先前冠军BertiGO提升0.3%。在低带宽单核配置下获得4.6%性能提升，远超BertiGO的2.6%。但对12000多个候选设计的分析表明，多核进化仍因仿真延迟而面临重大挑战。

Conclusion: 自动化智能体发现可作为计算机架构师的有力工具，能够超越人工设计的最优方案；同时分析12000+候选设计的进化过程为理解自动化进化智能体如何探索和综合复杂微架构逻辑提供了关键洞见。多核进化的可扩展性仍是未来需解决的重要问题。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence has shown great promise in automating algorithm design, but scaling similar techniques to computer microarchitecture discovery remains challenging due to vast search spaces, strict hardware budgets, and long simulation times. In this work, we present ArchAgent v2, a framework which scales automated microarchitecture search to multi-level data prefetching. While the original ArchAgent successfully discovered single-level cache replacement policies in competition settings, it does not scale to multi-level prefetching where the design space and degrees of freedom are larger. To overcome this, we introduce two new additions to ArchAgent: a cascaded evolutionary search that subdivides the design space by sequentially evolving and freezing prefetchers at individual cache levels, and a hardware-realizability feedback loop that embeds real-time size-estimation directly into the evolution process.
  Evaluated under identical rules of the 4th Data Prefetching Championship (DPC4), ArchAgent v2 automatically designs a three-level prefetcher that outperforms the winning hand-designed solution, further demonstrating automated agentic discovery as a useful tool for computer architects. Our discovered policy achieves a 3.8\% geometric mean IPC speedup over the baseline overall and a 0.3\% improvement over the prior champion, BertiGO. On low-bandwidth single-core configurations, our policy yields a 4.6\% performance speedup compared to only 2.6\% for BertiGO. However, multi-core evolution still remains a significant challenge due to simulation latency impeding evolution speed. Finally, our profiling of an ArchAgent evolution of over 12,000 candidate designs provides key insights into how automated evolutionary agents explore and synthesize complex microarchitectural logic.

</details>


### [109] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaSpace框架，基于蜕变测试原理自动评估具身智能体的空间认知能力，通过逻辑编程语言编码蜕变关系并检测违反情况，在三个具身场景中发现SOTA多模态大模型智能体的90,422个空间认知错误，其平均空间认知得分仅0.44-0.52，远低于人类基准0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估方法存在两大缺陷：一是依赖人工标注的VQA对，耗时且质量不稳定；二是依赖高层任务完成指标（如导航成功率），可能掩盖次优路径或安全违规等关键漏洞。由于空间认知是具身任务执行的基石，亟需一种能够自动、可靠地评估智能体在执行任务过程中空间认知能力的方法。

Method: 受软件工程中蜕变测试原理启发，MetaSpace利用真实执行轨迹中的时空多模态状态，基于逻辑规则和物理定律预定义的蜕变关系（MRs）自动生成测试用例。关键在于将MRs编码为逻辑编程语言Prolog中的可执行规则，通过检测蜕变关系的违反来判断空间认知失败。

Result: 在三个具身场景中的实证评估表明，MetaSpace能够在SOTA的MLLM驱动智能体中检测出90,422个空间认知错误。提出的空间认知（SC）得分显示，所有SOTA智能体平均得分在0.44-0.52之间，显著低于人类基准的0.96。

Conclusion: 现有SOTA具身智能体的空间认知能力远低于人类水平，MetaSpace提供了一种无需人工标注、可自动化且具有可解释性的评估框架，能够有效暴露传统指标所忽视的空间认知缺陷。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution.
  Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>


### [110] [When LLM Agents Negotiate: Private Information and Dynamic Bargaining in Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07538)
*Chen Liang,Fasheng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在典型供应链讨价还价场景中，对9个LLM智能体（来自OpenAI、Google、阿里巴巴）在9,840次智能体间谈判中的表现进行了均衡基准审计。研究发现：能力决定价值创造（达成率98.9%、捕获95.4%一阶最优剩余，但谈判轮次延迟侵蚀21-34%剩余）；盈余分配具有供应商关系性（供应商身份比能力排名更能预测盈余归属）；提示词是战略性杠杆（委托人经济耐心与智能体提示策略耐心分离，解释90%的盈余分配方差）。据此建立三维审计框架：贴现效率、分配特征、运营可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从决策支持转向自主采购，企业需要了解被授权的谈判智能体是否能创造价值、能否可预测地分配价值、以及能否避免亏损合同。此前缺乏对LLM智能体谈判行为进行均衡参照的量化审计研究。

Method: 在买方拥有私人需求信息、卖方不知情的经典供应链讨价还价问题（数量-付款合约）中，将9个LLM（OpenAI、Google、阿里巴巴）与经过验证的完美贝叶斯均衡（PBE）进行基准对比，通过9,840次LLM对LLM谈判实验，沿贴现效率、分配特征、运营可靠性三个维度进行系统性审计。

Result: (1) 能力决定价值创造与可靠性：达成率98.9%、无贴现捕获95.4%一阶最优剩余，但平均2.98轮谈判（基准为1.25轮）导致21-34%剩余被侵蚀；基线模型19.2%接受个体非理性合同，旗舰模型仅0-0.6%，需自动化利润验证作为护栏。(2) 盈余分配具有供应商关系性：OpenAI自博弈买方份额40%、Google约50%、Qwen约70%，供应商切换使分配偏移7-18个百分点，Qwen旗舰是表现最弱的跨家族卖方。(3) 提示词是战略性杠杆：委托人经济耐心与智能体提示策略耐心的解耦解释90%的分配方差。

Conclusion: 研究确立了基于均衡参照的AI智能体三维审计框架（贴现效率、分配特征、运营可靠性），揭示了在LLM自主采购中，供应商选择是首要的分配性决策，提示工程是可免费利用的分配杠杆，且低于能力门槛时需要自动化利润验证作为关键护栏。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from decision support to autonomous procurement, firms need to know whether delegated negotiators create value, divide it predictably, and avoid money-losing contracts. We study this in a canonical supply chain bargaining problem: a buyer with private demand information negotiates a quantity-payment contract with an uninformed seller. We benchmark nine LLMs from OpenAI, Google, and Alibaba against a validated Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium across 9,840 LLM-to-LLM negotiations. First, capability governs value creation. Agents agree in 98.9% of negotiations and capture 95.4% of first-best surplus undiscounted, but average 2.98 rounds against the benchmark's 1.25, and this delay erodes 21-34% of surplus. Capability also governs reliability: baseline models accept individually irrational contracts in 19.2% of cases, versus 0.0-0.6% at mid-tier and flagship, making automated profit verification the binding guardrail below that threshold. Second, surplus capture is relational. Provider identity predicts who captures surplus better than capability rank: self-play buyer shares average 40% for OpenAI, 50% for Google, and 70% for Alibaba's Qwen, an ordering that survives restricted communication and no discounting. Reversing which provider sells moves the division by 7-18 percentage points, and the capable Qwen flagship is the weakest cross-family seller: vendor choice is a first-order distributional decision. Third, the prompt is a strategic lever. Delegation separates the principal's economic patience from the agent's prompted strategic patience, a free deployment choice that is the single strongest driver of surplus division (90% of explained variance). Together these establish an equilibrium-referenced audit of AI agents along three dimensions: discounted efficiency, distributional profile, and operational reliability.

</details>


### [111] [TREAT: Evaluating Access to Formal Knowledge across Equivalent Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07540)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TREAT基准，用于评估大型语言模型在等价保持的数学公式变换下，能否恢复已知定理的身份标识。测试显示最佳模型仅有60.73%的准确率，表明语言模型对定理的知识在表示形式变化下是脆弱的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在灵活输入表示与下游工具使用的形式化对象之间运行，关键挑战是识别不熟悉的公式形式是否对应已知的形式化对象（如定理）。已有工作仅关注文本层面的改写，而忽视了数学形式本身变换对模型识别能力的影响，缺乏一个受控的测试平台来衡量模型在等价变换下访问形式知识的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者构建TREAT基准：(1)从抓取的定理页面中筛选具有可用数学表达式的条目；(2)提取标准定理条件；(3)生成多种等价保持的公式级变换，包括残差方程、见证语句、优化恒等式、集合关系、算子形式和证明中间特征；(4)记录假设和逆映射。最终包含737个定理身份和29,480个变换后的数据行。模型任务是根据变换后的公式恢复标准表述对应的定理身份。

Result: 在测试集上，最佳模型的定理身份检索准确率仅为60.73%。其他系统表现出不同的失败模式，包括弃权（abstention）、错误检测（wrong detection）和格式异常输出（malformed outputs）。

Conclusion: TREAT提供了一个受控测试平台，用于评估表示鲁棒性的形式知识访问能力，揭示了定理知识在等价表示变换下的脆弱性。该基准具有更广泛的相关性，适用于需要稳定目标对象、显式等价关系、验证程序和可审计评分的领域。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly operate between flexible input representations and formal objects used by downstream tools. A key challenge is recognizing when an unfamiliar formulation denotes a known formal object. We study this challenge through theorem recognition: given an equivalence-preserving transformation of a theorem condition, a model must recover the theorem identity associated with the standard statement. We introduce TREAT, a benchmark for evaluating whether large language models can recover known theorem identities from equivalence-preserving formula-level transformations. Rather than paraphrasing theorem text, TREAT changes the mathematical form of theorem conditions themselves, expressing known results through residual equations, witness statements, optimization identities, set relations, operator forms, and proof-intermediate characterizations. Starting from scraped theorem pages, we filter for entries with usable mathematical expression forms, extract canonical theorem conditions, and generate transformed variants with recorded assumptions and inverse mappings. The final corpus contains 737 theorem identities and 29,480 transformed rows. On a test panel, the best model retrieves the correct theorem identity in only 60.73% of cases. Other systems reveal different failure modes, including abstention, wrong detection, and malformed outputs. These suggest that theorem knowledge can be fragile under equivalent changes in representation. TREAT therefore provides a controlled testbed for evaluating representation-robust access to formal knowledge, with broader relevance to domains that require stable target objects, explicit equivalence relations, validation procedures, and auditable scoring.

</details>


### [112] [An AI Scientist that Doesn't Drift: Taste, Structure, and Falsifiable Findings in a Quadruped Navigation Research Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07542)
*Yiwen Zhang,Eloise Zeng,Jaeha Lee,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向四足机器人导航策略泛化研究的AI科学家系统，通过不可变实验卡片、专职子代理和kkanbu偏好预言机三个组件来解决自主研究循环中"目标漂移"问题。实验表明该框架能保证研究诚实性，而kkanbu则决定研究探索方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的自主研究循环虽然能大规模运行机器学习实验，但倾向于对所优化的指标进行局部改进，而非真正检验驱动实验的假设。这种"目标漂移"导致研究失焦，需要结构性解决方案。

Method: 论文构建基于Karpathy自动研究范式的AI Scientist循环，加入三个组件：(1)不可变实验卡片——以固定schema配对每次迭代的预测与结果，防止已证伪的假设被事后改写；(2)专职子代理——限制在机械性角色中运作；(3)kkanbu——将用户研究品味以类型化知识图谱存储的偏好预言机，是唯一被允许做主观判断的组件。为隔离kkanbu的影响，在11个研究流上运行完全相同的循环两次，有/无kkanbu各一次。

Result: 两条实验分支均未发生漂移：两者都证伪了自身约四分之三的假设，最佳训练策略来自无预言机分支。kkanbu改变的是研究探索方向而非得分：它独自从探索了测试时适应，在其主导的分支中产出了获胜设计，并将跨研究流的经验传递给其他分支反复重新推导。

Conclusion: 脚手架（框架组件）保证研究循环的诚实性，防止假设被篡改或漂移；kkanbu（偏好预言机）决定研究探索的方向。二者分工明确：一个负责诚实记录，一个负责引导探索。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops driven by large language models can run machine-learning experiments at scale but tend to drift toward local refinements of whichever metric they optimise rather than testing the hypotheses that motivate the experiments. We address this structurally and present an AI Scientist for studying generalisation in quadruped robot navigation policies in simulation. Building on the autoresearch paradigm of Karpathy, our loop adds three components: an immutable experiment card that pairs each iteration's prediction with its outcome under a fixed schema, so a falsified hypothesis cannot be retconned; specialised subagents restricted to mechanical roles; and kkanbu, a preference oracle that holds the user's research taste as a typed knowledge graph and is the only component permitted to make subjective judgements. To isolate the oracle we run the identical loop twice across eleven research streams, with and without kkanbu. Neither arm drifts: both falsify roughly three quarters of their own hypotheses, and the best trained policy comes from the oracle-less arm. What the oracle changes is direction, not score: it alone explores test-time adaptation, it authored the winning designs where its arm led, and it carried lessons across streams that the other arm repeatedly re-derived. The scaffold keeps the loop honest; kkanbu decides where it looks.

</details>


### [113] [The Field Knows: Cross-Dimensional Geometry from Navigation to Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07566)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种连续度量场框架，通过单一因果对比损失训练，将场景编码为固定对称矩阵基的系数，组装成李代数元素并指数化为黎曼或洛伦兹度量，在机器人导航和黑洞时空中展现出广泛的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以通过单一的统一因果对比损失框架，在不同维度和场景中发现完整的几何结构谱系——从机器人导航的避障测地线到黑洞的事件视界，从而验证"场理解几何，几何理解物理"的前提。

Method: 构建连续度量场框架：场景被编码为固定对称矩阵基的系数，组装成李代数元素后通过指数映射得到黎曼度量（正定）或洛伦兹度量（具特征符号），并以单一因果对比损失进行训练。

Result: 在不同维度下，该框架发现了从平面和机械臂构型空间的避障测地线到洛伦兹时空中黑洞事件视界的完整几何结构。广泛的零样本泛化研究表明该场捕获了可迁移的几何结构而非记忆特定配置；在黑洞场景中，因果损失自发演化了具有正确洛伦兹符号的类黑洞结构。

Conclusion: 相同损失、架构和训练协议在不同维度下产生了完整的几何现象谱系，验证了度量场能够自主发现真实的几何结构，且这种几何结构与物理规律之间存在内在联系。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous metric field framework trained by a single causal contrastive loss. The framework encodes a scene into coefficients of a fixed symmetric matrix basis, assembles them into a Lie algebra element, and exponentiates the result to a Riemannian or Lorentzian metric. Across dimensions, this field discovers the full spectrum of geometric structures: from obstacle-avoiding geodesics in robot navigation across planar and manipulator configuration spaces, to event horizons of black holes in Lorentzian spacetime. Extensive zero-shot generalization studies demonstrate that the field captures transferable geometric structure rather than memorizing specific configurations. In the black hole setting, the causal loss spontaneously evolves genuine black-hole-like structures with the correct Lorentzian signature. The same loss, the same architecture, and the same training protocol produce the full range of geometric phenomena across dimensions. The field knows geometry, and geometry knows physics.

</details>


### [114] [TeXFix-Bench: An Empirically Grounded Multi-Format Benchmark for LLM-Based Document Source Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07617)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TeXFix-Bench，一个基于挖掘故障分类法的多格式文档修复基准。通过扎根理论研究168个真实LaTeX崩溃故障构建18类分类法，并实例化为DocMut（48个AST感知算子）。实验表明DocMut故障比模式突变更难修复5.6-9.2个百分点，真实错误案例成功率67.0%位于两类合成集之间。10,437个实例、7个LLM、48,651次尝试的评估发现：仅编译成功率会高估修复质量（13.6-18.5%的编译成功修复实质改变文档文本），Typtst比LaTeX和Markdown更难修复，且内容恢复排名与编译排名存在分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档修复评估使用ad-hoc编辑注入故障，缺乏经验故障模型，无法真实反映LLM在实际源代码修复中的能力。作者希望通过挖掘真实故障构建有实证依据的故障分类法，为文档修复评估提供更可靠的基准。

Method: 采用扎根理论对TeX Stack Exchange、GitHub提交和包文档中的168个已验证硬崩溃LaTeX故障进行双重开放式编码（κ=0.34），构建18类故障分类法。将其实例化为DocMut，包含48个跨三格式（LaTeX/Typst/Markdown）的AST感知变异算子。构建10,437个实例、评估7个LLM（48,651次尝试，成本约200美元），采用固定零样本协议和供应商固定路由，并通过恢复预言机（28,129个编译修复）衡量内容保真度。

Result: DocMut故障比模式突变难修复5.6-9.2个百分点；真实错误（67.0%修复成功率）从下方约束两类合成集。固定引擎门控下编译成功率跨度27.5个百分点（56.7-84.2%）。13.6-18.5%的编译成功修复实质性改变文档文本，编译排名与内容恢复排名分歧（编译率最低的模型恢复内容最佳）。Typtst显著难于LaTeX和Markdown。

Conclusion: 编译成功不能作为文档修复质量的充分指标；需要引入内容保真度评估。所发布的分类法、DocMut和活动工件为未来文档修复研究提供了可复现的基准基础设施。

Abstract: Scientific and technical writing depends on markup sources that must compile: LaTeX, Typst, and Markdown pipelines fail on missing delimiters, mismatched environments, broken imports, or package conflicts. Existing document-repair evaluations inject faults with ad-hoc edits that lack an empirical fault model. We present TeXFix-Bench, a multi-format benchmark for LLM-based full-source document repair grounded in a mined fault taxonomy. A Grounded-Theory study of localized hard-crash LaTeX faults from TeX Stack Exchange, GitHub commits, and package documentation (168 verified faults, dual open coding at $κ$=0.34) yields an 18-category taxonomy instantiated as DocMut: 48 AST-aware operators across three formats. A three-model cross-benchmark shows DocMut faults are 5.6-9.2 pp harder to repair than pattern-based mutations on the same seeds, and a real-error case study (88 mined human crashes, 67.0% repair success) brackets both synthetic sets from below. We construct 10,437 instances from 743 openly licensed seeds and evaluate seven LLMs under a fixed zero-shot protocol with provider-pinned routing, collecting 48,651 attempts at about USD 200 total inference cost. A complete 6,613-instance x 7-model balanced matrix confirms all rankings. A pinned engine gate yields a 27.5-point intention-to-treat compile spread (56.7-84.2%). Typst is markedly harder than LaTeX and Markdown. A restoration oracle over 28,129 compiling repairs shows that 13.6-18.5% of compiling repairs materially alter document text, and restoration rank diverges from compile rank: the model with the lowest compile rate restores content best among its successes. Compile success alone overstates repair quality. We release the taxonomy, DocMut, and all campaign artifacts.

</details>


### [115] [CMU-Drive and V2V-VLA: Cooperative Multi-agent Unified Driving with Reasoning Benchmark and Vehicle-to-Vehicle Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07621)
*Hsu-kuang Chiu,Stephen F. Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CMU-Drive，一个用于评估多智能体协同自动驾驶的闭环端到端基准，并提出了V2V-VLA协同VLA模型，该模型通过单次前向传播同时生成驾驶动作、未来路点、语言推理和通信策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要针对单一自动驾驶智能体设计，缺乏对协同感知、推理和规划的支持，无法应对多车互联自动驾驶场景下的安全关键型驾驶任务。

Method: 1) 构建CMU-Drive闭环端到端基准，包含多个互联自动驾驶车辆（CAVs）在安全关键驾驶场景中的评估；2) 提出V2V-VLA协同VLA模型，将协同驾驶集成到单次前向传播中，联合生成驾驶动作、未来路点、语言推理和通信策略。

Result: 在CMU-Drive上建立了首个协同VLA驾驶基准和基线，为多智能体闭环端到端协同自动驾驶的未来研究提供基础。

Conclusion: 本文首次将VLA模型扩展到多智能体协同自动驾驶场景，同时开源代码、基准和模型权重以促进该领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved impressive performance for end-to-end autonomous driving, yet existing approaches are primarily designed for an individual single autonomous driving agent with limited support for cooperative perception, reasoning, and planning. We present Cooperative Multi-agent Unified Driving with Reasoning (CMU-Drive), a closed-loop end-to-end benchmark for evaluating cooperative autonomous driving with multiple connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) operating in safety-critical driving scenarios with background traffic participants. We further propose Vehicle-to-Vehicle Vision-Language-Action (V2V-VLA), a cooperative VLA model that integrates cooperative driving into a single forward pass by jointly generating driving actions, future waypoints, language reasoning, and communication policies. Experiments on CMU-Drive establish the first benchmark and baseline for cooperative VLA driving and provide a foundation for future research on multi-agent, closed-loop, end-to-end cooperative autonomous driving. Our code, benchmark, and model checkpoint will be publicly released to facilitate open-source research.

</details>


### [116] [Controlled Memory Interference in Continual LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07622)
*Ao Ding,Hongzong LI,Shiqin Tang,Li Zhang,Liang Chen,Xuyang Chen,Zi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出受控记忆干扰（CMI）框架，系统研究AI智能体长期记忆在不同记忆关系下的演化机制，发现良性积累影响有限，而关系特异的干扰会严重抑制更新能力且几乎不带来稳定性收益；CMI还提供针对性的干扰感知记忆学习样例，在保持原有记忆性能的同时提升合法更新与干扰记忆的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆对AI智能体至关重要，但记忆演化不仅是存储更多信息的过程——新经验可能强化、修正或干扰已有记忆状态。现有系统主要关注记忆构建和相关性检索，却忽略了多个记忆可能同时相关但在状态、时间有效性和权威性上存在差异的问题，缺乏对记忆干扰的系统性研究。

Method: 提出受控记忆干扰（CMI）诊断与数据生成框架，在受控条件下研究不同记忆关系（如良性积累、关系特异性干扰、毒化攻击）对记忆演化的影响；区分词汇检索和稠密检索两种检索路径的干扰机制；并基于诊断结果生成针对性的干扰感知训练样例。

Result: 研究发现：良性积累对记忆演化的影响有限，而关系特异性干扰通过阻断目标记忆暴露或破坏其下游使用两条路径显著抑制更新塑性且几乎没有稳定性收益；词汇与稠密检索表现出不同的干扰通路；毒化对更新权威线索比单纯的时间新近性更敏感；干扰感知记忆学习能改善有效更新与干扰记忆的区分，同时保持原有记忆任务性能。

Conclusion: 记忆演化不仅受记忆规模影响，还取决于累积经验之间的交互作用。记忆干扰是构建可靠持续智能体记忆系统的重要考量因素，为未来记忆系统的更新、稳定和安全性设计提供了新的研究方向和框架基础。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables AI agents to maintain continuity across sessions, personalize behavior, and evolve through accumulated experience. Yet memory evolution is not simply a process of storing more information: new experiences may reinforce, revise, or interfere with existing memory states. Existing systems mainly emphasize memory construction and relevance-based retrieval, but several memories may remain simultaneously relevant while differing in state, temporal validity, or authority. We introduce Controlled Memory Interference (CMI), a controlled diagnostic and data-generation framework for studying how agent memory evolves under different memory relationships. Across controlled memory evolution, benign accumulation has limited effects, whereas relationship-specific interference sharply suppresses update plasticity with little stability gain, either by blocking target-memory exposure or by disrupting its downstream use. Lexical and Dense retrieval exhibit distinct interference pathways, while poisoning is more sensitive to update-authority cues than to recency alone. Beyond diagnosis, CMI provides targeted examples for interference-aware memory learning, improving the distinction between valid updates and interference-inducing memories while preserving performance on original memory tasks. These findings show that memory evolution is shaped not only by memory scale, but also by interactions among accumulated experiences. More broadly, memory interference emerges as an important factor for reliable continual agent memory systems.

</details>


### [117] [From Single Chatbots to Governed Agent Ecosystems: An Agentic AI Pattern Catalogue and Orchestration Framework for Mission-Critical Hospital Information Management Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07627)
*Manideep Dhar,Ritwik Singh,Sharat Chandra Kumar Manikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向医院信息管理系统（HIMS）的合规优先Agentic AI模式目录与编排框架，旨在将碎片化的AI试点转化为可治理、可审计、可扩展的生产级系统。框架包含Agentic角色分类、基于风险的AI模式分层模型，以及支持Epic/Cerner/MEDITECH等多EHR系统的统一编排运行时，融合vLLM推理、机密计算、MCP本地部署及政策即代码控制，对标HIPAA、GDPR、EU AI Act等多项法规。该方案有望减少文档时间、集成工作量与AI试点流失率，同时加强治理与审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院纷纷将AI嵌入分诊管理、病历文档、排程和收入周期等工作流，但大多数部署仍是停留在生产边缘的碎片化试点，带来运营脆弱性、无监管风险和持续增长的技术债务。同时，全球AI医疗市场预计到2034年接近1万亿美元，架构失误和扩展战略失败的财务后果被大幅放大，亟需系统性、合规驱动的AI治理架构。

Method: 提出合规优先的Agentic AI模式目录与编排框架，包含：（1）Agentic角色分类学；（2）形式化风险分层模型，将每种模式映射到风险层级、人在环路检查点和治理钩子；（3）统一编排运行时，可在多EHR/HIMS环境中协调多智能体工作流。技术上融合vLLM推理、优化分页内存、机密计算和基于MCP的本地部署，实施端到端加密及政策即代码控制，对齐HIPAA、GDPR、EU AI Act等法规。

Result: 展示该架构能够高效减少文档处理时间、降低集成工作量、抑制AI试点流失率，同时强化治理与可审计性，为医院领导者和监管机构提供将AI投资转化为可持续临床、运营和财务ROI的蓝图。

Conclusion: 该框架为医疗AI从试点走向生产提供了合规优先的、可扩展的架构蓝图，有望将AI投资转化为可持续的临床、运营与财务收益。

Abstract: Hospitals are racing to embed AI, while coping with the surge in adaptation of the technology in other industries, into the triage management, documentation, scheduling, and revenue-cycle workflows, yet most deployments remain as fragmented pilots that stall at the edge of production, exposing patients and institutions to operational fragility, ungoverned risk, and mounting technical debt. At the same time, the global AI-in-healthcare market is projected to exceed nearly USD 1 trillion by 2034, according to the report of Fortune Business Insights, amplifying the financial consequences of architectural missteps and failed scaling strategies. This research proposes a compliance-first Agentic AI pattern catalogue and orchestration framework, purposely built for HIMS, moving beyond the single LLM chatbots and towards a governed ecosystem of autonomous and semi-autonomous agents. The framework extends by adding (i) a taxonomy of Agentic roles, (ii) a formal risk-stratification model that maps each pattern to risk tiers, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and governance hooks, and (iii) a unified orchestration runtime capable of coordinating multi-agent workflows across EHR/HIMS landscapes such as Epic, Cerner, and MEDITECH. Technically the framework combines vLLM-based inference, optimized paging memory, confidential computing, and MCP based on-premise deployment, enforcing end-to-end encryption and policy-as-code controls aligned with HIPAA, GDPR, the EU AI Act, India's DPDP and DISHA Acts, ISO 27001, ISO 27002, ISO 14971 and IEC 62304. We exhibit how the proposed architecture is capable and efficient to reduce the documentation time, integration effort, and AI pilot attrition while constricting the governance and auditability, offering hospital leaders and governing authorities an urgently needed blueprint to convert AI investment into sustainable clinical, operational, and financial ROI

</details>


### [118] [Agent-MD: Selective LLM Intervention with Event-Driven Escalation for Stateful GCMC--MD Campaigns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07637)
*Yijie Wang,Zhen-Yu Yin,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-MD框架，将大语言模型推理选择性嵌入长期运行的分子模拟工作流中，仅用于战役构建和事件触发的审查，而日常模拟、分析、续算、归档等由基于规则的持久化代理执行，在GCMC-MD水蒸气脱附战役（5种蒙脱石系统、3个相对湿度状态）中验证了120个分段模拟周期的高效完成与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期分子模拟战役需要重复续算、溯源感知的推进、自适应评估和偶发的工作流条件解读，而这些无法仅靠固定规则安全解决。现有方案要么完全依赖LLM推理导致成本高且不可复现，要么完全依赖硬编码规则缺乏灵活性，因此需要在确定性执行与LLM智能推理之间找到平衡点。

Method: Agent-MD采用混合架构：LLM推理被选择性放置在两个关键节点——工作流战役构建阶段和事件触发式审查阶段；而常规的模拟执行、分析、续算、归档和状态推进则由持久的基于规则的战役代理（campaign agent）负责，该代理遵循预先批准的策略并使用显式状态记录。在一次评审边界触发时，通过盲审推理代理回放（blinded reasoning-agent replay）评估两个保存的事件。

Result: 在15个系统-相对湿度状态下，工作流完成了120个分段模拟周期，具有状态特异的采样长度和溯源感知的续算继承。常规生产运行无需实时调用推理代理，仅有一个状态达到评审边界；盲审推理代理回放识别了底层工作流问题并推荐了后续行动。模拟还揭示了组成依赖的低RH响应差异：含Ca蒙脱石比Na和K系统保留更多层间水并维持更大基底间距，而最高电荷的Na系统在干燥条件下保留更多残余水。

Conclusion: 长期运行的科研工作流无需将每一步都置于LLM推理循环中；选择性推理与确定性执行、结构化证据和经过验证的控制交接相结合，即可提供可复现、可审计的代理辅助分子模拟方案。

Abstract: Long-running molecular simulation campaigns require repeated continuation from saved states, provenance-aware progression, adaptive assessment, and occasional interpretation of workflow conditions that cannot be resolved safely by fixed rules. Here, we present Agent-MD, a framework that places large language model (LLM) reasoning selectively at campaign construction and event-triggered review, while routine simulation, analysis, continuation, archiving, and state progression are handled by a persistent rule-based campaign agent using approved policies and explicit state records. Agent-MD was demonstrated in a grand canonical Monte Carlo-molecular dynamics (GCMC-MD) water-vapor desorption campaign comprising five montmorillonite systems and three sequential relative-humidity states (RH = 0.9-0.3-0.1). Across 15 system-RH states, the workflow completed 120 segmented simulation cycles with state-specific sampling lengths and provenance-aware restart inheritance. Routine production required no live reasoning-agent invocation, while one state reached a review boundary; two preserved incidents were subsequently evaluated through blinded reasoning-agent replay, which identified the underlying workflow problems and recommended appropriate follow-up actions. The simulations also revealed distinct composition-dependent low-RH responses, with Ca-bearing montmorillonite retaining more interlayer water and maintaining a larger basal spacing than the Na- and K-bearing systems, while the highest-charge Na system retained more residual water under dry conditions. These results demonstrate that long-running scientific workflows need not place every operation inside an LLM reasoning loop: selective reasoning can instead be combined with deterministic execution, structured evidence, and validated control handoffs to provide reproducible and auditable agent-assisted molecular simulation.

</details>


### [119] [Contextual Value Alignment via Multilayer Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07642)
*Yuanhong Wu,Djallel Bouneffouf,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCF-CVA框架，利用多层组合融合实现大语言模型的上下文价值对齐。该框架实例化多个代表不同价值观的道德智能体，通过评分和排序两种组合方式（扩展-缩减EAR过程）在欧几里得评分空间和Kemeny排序空间的双重架构中反复迭代，利用智能体间的认知多样性来缓解冲突和冗余，最终达到停止条件。实验表明该方法在标准指标上优于单智能体基线、多智能体单层结果及先前聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM价值对齐方法（如RLHF、CAI及其变体）依赖单一智能体框架和统一奖励系统，无法捕获道德多元主义（ethical pluralism）、难以适应多样道德语境，也无法反映多智能体道德推理的动态特性。这限制了模型在信任型AI中对人类多样化价值观的准确对齐能力。

Method: 提出的MCF-CVA框架包含多层结构：第一层实例化多个分别微调以代表特定价值观的道德智能体；其输出通过评分组合和排序组合（包括平均值和加权聚合）进行组合式扩展；随后将组合模型缩减回与初始智能体数量相同的规模。这一扩展-缩减（EAR）过程在多层级中持续迭代，直到满足停止准则。框架在欧几里得评分空间与Kemeny排序空间的双重架构上执行EAR算法。

Result: 经验评估表明，该框架在标准指标上优于单智能体基线、多智能体单层结果以及先前的聚合方法。MCF-CVA提供了稳健有效的机制，能更好地反映上下文人类价值观。

Conclusion: MCF-CVA通过利用智能体间的认知多样性来缓解多智能体间的冲突和冗余，是一种推进LLM上下文价值对齐的鲁棒且有效的框架，为处理道德多元性和动态多智能体道德推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values remains a major challenge, especially for trustworthy AI. While existing approaches such as RLHF, CAI, and their variants have achieved promising results, they often rely on a single-agent framework and a unified reward system. This limits their ability to capture ethical pluralism, adapt to diverse moral contexts, and reflect the dynamics of multi-agent moral reasoning.
  In this work, we propose a framework that utilizes multilayer combinatorial fusion for contextual value alignment (MCF-CVA). At the first layer of the framework, it instantiates multiple moral agents, each fine-tuned to represent a distinctive value.
