Their outputs are then expanded combinatorially using both score- and rank-combinations as well as average and weighted aggregations. These combined models are then reduced to the same number of initial moral agents. This expansion and reduction (EAR) process continues for multi-layers until a stopping criterion is reached.
  The MCF-CVA framework leverages cognitive diversity between agents to mitigate conflicts and redundancies across multiple agents, producing responses that better reflect contextual human values. The framework using the EAR algorithm is performed on the dual architecture of Euclidean score space and Kemeny rank space. Empirical evaluations demonstrated that the proposed framework outperforms single-agent baselines, multi-agent single-layer results, and previous aggregation approaches on standard metrics, showing that the MCF-CVA framework provides a robust and effective mechanism for advancing contextual value alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [120] [Mendel Gödel Machine: Recursive Self-Improving Coding Agents via Comparative Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07645)
*Changzhi Liu,Yilun Liu,Sikuan Yan,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mendel Gödel Machine (MGM)，一种基于孟德尔遗传原则的自我改进编码代理框架，在传统单轨迹克隆突变之外引入反应规范突变和跨谱系杂交两种新的自我修改策略，通过利用多任务和跨谱系的丰富证据提升编码代理的收敛速度与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进编码代理通常仅从单次失败轨迹中学习，忽视了代理历史尝试档案中蕴含的丰富比较信号（如同任务多轨迹、跨谱系参考轨迹），导致学习效率和泛化能力受限。

Method: 借鉴孟德尔遗传学中的受控遗传原则，提出MGM框架。它包含三种自我修改方式：(1) 克隆突变（单轨迹基线）；(2) 反应规范突变——基于代理在多个任务上的轨迹同时进行编辑；(3) 跨谱系杂交——使用同一任务上另一谱系参考代理的轨迹进行编辑。论文在加性适应度景观模型下从理论上证明新策略的收敛优势，并通过受控代理仿真和真实基准实验（SWE-bench、Polyglot）验证。

Result: 理论上证明了在加性适应度景观下新策略比单轨迹基线具有更快更好的收敛性；受控代理仿真验证了理论结果；在SWE-bench和Polyglot基准上，MGM在性能、效率和泛化能力方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 利用多轨迹和多谱系证据的自我修改策略比单纯依赖单轨迹的自我改进方法更有效，MGM验证了将遗传学原则引入编码代理自我改进的可行性，为提升代理的自适应和学习能力提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-improving coding agents that iteratively rewrite their own source code have demonstrated impressive performance on coding tasks. However, existing solutions generally derive self-modification from a single failure trajectory at a time, overlooking rich comparative signals available in the agent's expanding archive of past attempts. According to Mendelian principles of controlled inheritance, we introduce Mendel Gödel Machine (MGM). In addition to the general single-trajectory clonal mutation, MGM includes two new types of self-modification that better utilizes evidences accumulated: the reaction-norm mutation edits an agent based on its trajectories on multiple tasks simultaneously, and the cross-lineage hybridization edits an agent using the trajectory of a reference agent from another lineage on the same task. Under an additive fitness landscape model, we prove theoretically and demonstrate via controlled surrogate simulation that the new strategies facilitate a faster and better convergence over single-trajectory baselines. Experiments on SWE-bench and Polyglot confirm MGM's consistent improvement in performance, efficiency, and generalizability.

</details>


### [121] [An Agentic AI Framework Overcomes Fundamental Limitations of Large Language Models for Glaucoma Detection from Fundus Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07651)
*Jalil Jalili,Hossein Taghizad,Anuwat Jiravarnsirikul,Christopher Bowd,Akram Belghith,Raheleh Kafieh,Christopher A. Girkin,Sally L. Baxter,Robert N. Weinreb,Linda M. Zangwill,Mark Christopher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发并验证了一个将大语言模型（LLM）与专用深度学习工具相结合的智能体AI框架，用于青光眼眼底照片检测。通过与专业眼科工具（图像质量评估、分类、视盘/杯分割）的协作，该框架在分类准确率、杯盘比误差、与专家评分的相关性及运行一致性方面均显著优于单纯LLM方法，显示从单一模型向多智能体系统转变的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学图像解读中表现出潜力，但存在幻觉、准确率有限和运行间不一致三大问题。作者旨在通过将LLM与专业深度学习工具结合，构建智能体框架来解决这些限制。

Method: 构建三步智能体工作流：(1) LLM初始评估；(2) 函数调用触发专用工具——图像质量评估（QAModel, FundaQ-8）、青光眼分类（SwinV2-Tiny）、视盘/杯分割（SegFormer-B0）；(3) LLM反思整合初始印象与工具输出。在ORIGA和RIM-ONE-v3两个公开数据集上评估两个LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-5.4 mini），涵盖裁剪与非裁剪视野，所有图像由掩蔽的专家级青光眼专科医生独立标注。

Result: 智能体工作流在所有条件下将分类准确率提升16-47个百分点，接近专家水平（相差6个百分点以内）；在RIM-ONE-v3上最佳配置与专家88%准确率持平。杯盘比误差（MAE）降低15-50%，与专家评分的相关性从弱（r=0.12-0.39）提升至中强（r=0.59-0.84）。运行一致性从近随机（kappa最低-0.01）提升至近完美（kappa最高0.96）。单纯LLM方式暴露了GPT-5.4 mini的正向偏差（敏感度95-100%，特异度0-5%）和Gemini 2.5 Flash的随机波动，而智能体框架均予以纠正。

Conclusion: 将LLM与专用工具整合有效解决了LLM单独使用的关键限制（过度诊断和运行可变性），且对两个不同的LLM骨干均有效，表明方法具有良好的泛化性，可能推动医学AI从单体模型向编排式多智能体系统转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in medical image interpretation but suffer from hallucination, limited accuracy, and run-to-run inconsistency. We developed and validated an agentic AI framework integrating LLMs with specialized deep learning tools for glaucoma detection from fundus photography. The workflow had three steps: (1) LLM initial assessment; (2) function calling to invoke specialized tools for image quality (QAModel, FundaQ-8), glaucoma classification (SwinV2-Tiny), and optic disc/cup segmentation (SegFormer-B0); and (3) LLM reflection integrating the initial impression with tool outputs. Two LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-5.4 mini) were evaluated on two public datasets (ORIGA, n=100; RIM-ONE-v3, n=100) under uncropped and cropped fields of view; all images were independently graded by a masked fellowship-trained glaucoma specialist. The agentic workflow improved classification accuracy by 16 to 47 percentage points across all conditions, reaching within 6 points of the specialist; on RIM-ONE-v3 the best configurations matched the specialist accuracy of 88%. LLM-alone approaches failed in two ways: GPT-5.4 mini showed positive bias (sensitivity 95-100%, specificity 0-5%), while Gemini 2.5 Flash varied stochastically between runs; the agentic workflow corrected both. Cup-to-disc ratio error fell 15-50% (MAE 0.156-0.228 to 0.104-0.132), and correlation with specialist grading rose from weak (r=0.12-0.39) to moderate-strong (r=0.59-0.84). Run-to-run consistency rose from near-random (kappa as low as -0.01) to near-perfect (kappa up to 0.96). Integrating LLMs with specialized tools addressed key limitations of LLM-alone approaches, including over-diagnosis and run-to-run variability. Gains held for both LLMs, suggesting generalizability across backbones, and may signal a shift from monolithic models toward orchestrated multi-agent systems in medical AI.

</details>


### [122] [Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution for Verification of Distributed Parallel AI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07947)
*Gautham Koorma,Vikas Sharma,George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DNSSE，一种结合LLM引导的调度预测、符号约束求解与覆盖引导随机变异的新型混合测试框架，用于检测分布式并行AI程序中的并发缺陷，相比最强基线多检测2.9%并发bug，并将分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式并行AI程序存在常规测试无法弥合的可靠性缺口：并行执行具有非确定性，且AI工作负载包含高维输入和非线性操作，使模糊测试与符号执行单独应用均无法有效工作。

Method: 将分布式AI执行建模为非确定性迁移系统，用线性时序逻辑（LTL）指定正确性；提出混合框架DNSSE，将LLM引导的调度预测、符号约束求解与覆盖引导的随机变异三者结合；并给出混合求解器的可靠性证明（可靠性、有界完备性、概率完备性）及LLM引导调度探索的期望成本分析。在PyTorch和Ray上实现可扩展版本。

Result: 在五个真实分布式AI基准上，相比最强基线多检测2.9%的并发缺陷，平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了DNSSE在检测分布式AI并发缺陷和提升覆盖率方面均显著优于现有最强方法，证明了神经-符号混合策略对分布式AI程序测试的有效性。

Abstract: Distributed parallel Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs expose reliability gaps that conventional testing cannot close: parallel executions are non-deterministic, and AI workloads bring high-dimensional inputs and non-linear operations that defeat fuzzing and symbolic execution in isolation. We present Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution (DNSSE), a hybrid testing framework that couples schedule prediction guided by a Large Language Model (LLM) with symbolic constraint solving and coverage-guided stochastic mutation. We model distributed AI executions as non-deterministic transition systems, specify correctness in linear temporal logic, and prove soundness, bounded completeness, and probabilistic completeness of the hybrid solver, together with an expected-cost analysis of LLM-guided schedule exploration. A scalable implementation on PyTorch and Ray detects 2.9% more concurrency bugs than the strongest baseline and raises average branch coverage from 68.6 % to 91.6 % across five realistic distributed AI benchmarks.

</details>


### [123] [IntelliAudit: Using Large Language Models to Evaluate Audit Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07688)
*Allison Wilson,Sina Moradi Sabet,Diar Shakimov,Panteha Shahrivar,Mohammad Reza Bagheri,Dean Konenkamp,Mohammad A. Tayebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntelliAudit，一个基于检索的多智能体系统，用于IT审计证据评估。该系统能够检索相关证据、生成基于证据的评估、质疑不利发现、调解分歧，并产出包含引用证据、推理、缺失证据分析和 remediation 建议的审计师推荐。在ISO/IEC 27001上评估表明其可支持控制解释、证据推理和审计准备流程，但需人工监督来校准充分性判断和纠正过于宽松的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT审计要求审计师判断异构组织证据是否满足语义安全和合规控制，但证据分散在政策、记录、电子表格和运营工件中，且审计结论依赖于证据充分性而非关键词匹配，因此难以自动化。现有方法无法有效处理这一复杂判断任务。

Method: IntelliAudit是一个检索接地（retrieval-grounded）的多智能体系统，给定控制项和证据语料库后，依次执行：检索相关工件、生成基于证据的评估、挑战不利发现、调解分歧，最终产出面向审计师的推荐，包含引用证据、推理依据、缺失证据分析和补救建议。系统在ISO/IEC 27001标准上实例化，并通过专家审计师评审和审计准备用户反馈在多个模拟组织上评估。

Result: 评估表明IntelliAudit能有效支持控制项解释、基于证据的推理和审计准备流程。同时揭示了人工监督的重要性——用于校准证据充分性判断和纠正过于宽松（overly permissive）的建议。

Conclusion: 检索接地多智能体系统可以协助审计证据审查，但应保持为决策支持工具，而非自主认证系统。

Abstract: IT audits require auditors to judge whether heterogeneous organizational evidence satisfies semantic security and compliance controls. This judgment is difficult to automate because relevant evidence is distributed across policies, records, spreadsheets, and operational artifacts, and because audit conclusions depend on evidentiary sufficiency rather than keyword matching. We present IntelliAudit, a retrieval-grounded multi-agent system for IT audit evidence evaluation. Given a control and an evidence corpus, IntelliAudit retrieves relevant artifacts, generates an evidence-grounded assessment, challenges adverse findings, adjudicates disagreements, and produces an auditor-facing recommendation with cited evidence, rationale, missing-evidence analysis, and remediation guidance. We instantiate IntelliAudit on ISO/IEC 27001 and evaluate it across multiple simulated organizations using expert auditor review and audit-readiness user feedback. The evaluation shows that IntelliAudit can support control interpretation, evidence-grounded reasoning, and audit-preparation workflows, while also revealing the importance of human oversight for calibrating sufficiency judgments and correcting overly permissive recommendations. These results suggest that retrieval-grounded multi-agent systems can assist audit evidence review, but should remain decision-support tools rather than autonomous certification systems.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Researcher Agents for Knowledge-Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07700)
*Tommaso Soru,Abdulsobur Oyewale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个智能体式文本到SPARQL系统，其中"研究者智能体"能在验证集上每轮推理后自动提出并测试对自身提示、规则和工具编排代码的修改，实现自我改进循环。该系统在DBpedia上迭代出九个版本，最终达到0.22的总体准确率，并揭示出性能瓶颈在于基本图模式中的谓词选择，而非SPARQL语法或修饰符。文章还发现部分基准条目因DBpedia属性歧义而误判正确查询，建议未来基准应采用机器翻译与信息检索指标结合的评分方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言问题转换为可执行的SPARQL查询需要解决词法歧义、在目标本体中接地表层术语，以及生成语法有效且语义忠实的图模式。现有静态工具使用型智能体无法自主改进，因此需要一种能够自我迭代优化的智能体系统来突破文本到SPARQL任务的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出"研究者智能体"架构：智能体在验证集上完成一轮推理后，自动提出对自身提示（prompts）、规则（rules）和工具编排代码（tool-orchestration code）的修改假设并加以测试，形成自我改进闭环。在DBpedia数据集上，使用低成本的推理模型驱动九次连续版本演化，最终将最优配置部署到两个更强的骨干模型上。

Result: 自我改进快速收敛，在2025年DBpedia验证集上达到0.22的总体准确率。性能瓶颈一致性地出现在基本图模式（BGP）的谓词选择环节，而非SPARQL语法或修饰符层面。此外，多个基准条目因DBpedia属性歧义导致正确查询被误判，揭示现有基准评分方式存在问题。

Conclusion: 智能体自我改进机制在文本到SPARQL任务上可行且收敛迅速，但当前能力上限受限于谓词选择。未来文本到SPARQL基准应采用机器翻译与信息检索指标相结合的混合评分策略，以更公平地评估正确但受属性歧义影响的查询。

Abstract: Translating a natural-language question into a SPARQL query that can be executed against a large knowledge graph requires resolving lexical ambiguity, grounding surface terms in the target ontology, and producing graph patterns that are both syntactically valid and semantically faithful. We present an agentic text-to-SPARQL system that goes one step beyond static tool-using agents: a researcher agent that, after each round of inference on a validation set, proposes and tests changes to its own prompts, rules, and tool-orchestration code. We instantiate the loop on DBpedia, evolve nine successive versions of the agent driven by a low-cost reasoning model, and deploy the best-performing configuration with two stronger backbone models. The study yields three observations: (i) self-improvement converges quickly and then achieves 0.22 overall accuracy on the 2025 DBpedia validation set; (ii) the bottleneck is consistently in basic-graph-pattern predicate selection, not in SPARQL syntax or modifiers; and (iii) several benchmark items appear to penalise correct queries due to property ambiguity in DBpedia, suggesting that future Text-to-SPARQL benchmarks should be scored using a combination of machine translation and information retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [125] [Protecting patient privacy in clinical foundation models: Technical and legal perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07705)
*Sana Tonekaboni,Lena Stempfle,Sasha Ronaghi,Corinna Coupette,I. Glenn Cohen,Emily Alsentzer,Marzyeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用框架，用于评估临床基础模型中的隐私风险，涵盖模型介导的敏感训练数据泄漏场景，并将其映射到现有法律体系（如HIPAA和GDPR），同时提出技术与法律相结合的缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着临床基础模型在大规模患者数据上的部署日益广泛，模型介导的隐私泄漏风险（如模型暴露敏感训练数据导致患者重识别）尚未被充分量化。现有数据处理控制措施无法捕捉此类间接威胁，而HIPAA和GDPR等现有法律框架对此类间接风险的指导有限，亟需一个系统化的评估框架。

Method: 提出一个上下文感知的隐私风险评估实用框架，通过跨部署场景的实例化泄漏案例分析，将具体风险场景映射到相应法律体系，并概述互补的技术缓解措施（如差分隐私、机器遗忘等）与法律缓解措施（如合同约束、审计义务等）。

Result: 该框架提供了基于现实使用场景的情境化隐私风险评估方法，识别了不同部署设置下的典型泄漏路径，完成了法律体系的对应映射，并给出了技术与法律双轨并行的缓解策略组合。

Conclusion: 在保护患者隐私的前提下，通过情境化的风险评估框架可以维持医学基础模型的应用价值，弥补现有法律框架在间接隐私威胁方面的空白，为临床AI的安全部署提供指导。

Abstract: Clinical foundation models trained on large-scale patient data are increasingly used for decision support, screening, and public health. As deployment expands, privacy risk increasingly arises from model-mediated leakage, yet its prevalence and severity remain poorly quantified. Models can disclose sensitive training artifacts, enabling patient re-identification in ways not captured by data-handling controls alone. Existing frameworks, including HIPAA and GDPR, offer limited guidance for such indirect threats. We propose a practical framework for assessing privacy risk in clinical foundation models and illustrate realistic leakage scenarios across deployment settings, map them to legal regimes, and outline complementary technical and legal mitigations. Our analysis provides a context-aware risk assessment grounded in realistic usage to preserve the value of medical foundation models while rigorously safeguarding patient privacy.

</details>


### [126] [QuantumMind: Constraint-Grounded Agentic Reasoning for Speedup Analysis in Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07743)
*Yijing Zuo,Zhe Fu,Zihan Nie,Zhihui Zhu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumMind，一个可审计的智能体工作流，用于生成并保守筛选量子加速假设。通过固定类型化的角色专用动作序列和确定性十项校验器，在582个开放发现任务上取得53.1平均ODS，超过最强基线17.3分，图审计通过率达99.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别有意义的量子加速需要严谨的规范——必须保持任务不变、尊重访问和输出模型、暴露必要的承诺条件，并保持在可辩护的复杂度范围内。现有的方法往往将经典问题与熟悉的量子原语简单匹配，缺乏系统可审计的流程来验证量子加速主张的有效性。

Method: QuantumMind采用固定序列的类型化、角色专用动作：形式化公开任务、分析结构和经典瓶颈、匹配带来源链接的量子原语与障碍注册表、构建有范围的候选方案。确定性十项校验器给出权威判定，完成状态编译成量子加速证据图，再通过只能向下（不能强化）的研究筛选。评估采用冻结的开放发现评分（ODS），与七个任务自适应提示和智能体控制对比，在582个相同的开放发现任务上进行。

Result: QuantumMind在冻结ODS下取得53.1平均分，比最强基线高17.3分（相对提升48.2%），在582个配对任务中对最强基线获胜355个；在图审计中通过率99.8%（最强基线仅43.6%），在全部七个任务族中排名第一。

Conclusion: 结果证明类型化状态转换和确定性证据控制对量子加速假设的质量贡献超出了单纯的流畅文本生成能力，QuantumMind提供了一种可审计、可辩护的量子加速假设生成与筛选框架。

Abstract: Identifying a meaningful quantum speedup requires more than matching a classical problem to a familiar quantum primitive: the claim must preserve the task, respect access and output models, expose required promises, and remain within a defensible complexity scope. We present QuantumMind, an auditable agentic workflow for generating and conservatively screening quantum-acceleration hypotheses. A fixed sequence of typed, role-specialized actions formalizes the public task, analyzes structure and classical bottlenecks, matches a source-linked registry of quantum primitives and barriers, and constructs a scoped candidate scheme. A deterministic ten-check validator assigns the authoritative verdict; completed states are compiled into a Quantum Acceleration Evidence Graph and passed through a downward-only research screen that cannot strengthen the decision. We evaluate QuantumMind against seven task-adapted prompting and agentic controls on 582 identical open-discovery tasks. Under the frozen Open-Discovery Score (ODS), QuantumMind obtains 53.1 mean ODS, exceeding the strongest baseline by 17.3 points (48.2% relative), and wins 355 of 582 paired tasks against that baseline. It passes the graph audit on 99.8% of tasks, compared with 43.6% for the strongest baseline, and ranks first in all seven task families. The results indicate that typed state transitions and deterministic evidence control contribute beyond fluent generation alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [HINT: Toward an Executable Hardware-Intent Representation Layer for LLM-Driven RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07625)
*Tairan Cheng,Yi Liu,Dongsheng Zuo,Zhengyuan Shi,Hongji Zhang,Xiangfei Hu,Maoshuo He,Hao Yan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HINT是一种可执行的硬件意图中间表示层，位于行为规范/可执行oracle与RTL之间，将微架构决策显式化，支持RTL前的检查，并明确RTL降级义务，从而提升LLM生成实现级质量RTL的成功率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用LLM生成实现级质量的RTL仍然困难，因为直接生成需要同时解决微架构问题并生成、调试底层代码。这需要一种中间表示来解耦高层次意图与低层实现。

Method: 提出HINT，一种可执行的硬件意图中间表示层，位于行为规格或可执行oracle与RTL之间。HINT使RTL相关的微架构显式化，支持RTL前的检查（pre-RTL checking），并提供显式的RTL降级义务（RTL-lowering obligations）。评估采用最小单agent流程和完整分阶段工作流两种方式。

Result: 在7个算子案例上，HINT介导的流程在无后综合QoR优化的条件下7/7全部生成符合契约的可综合RTL；直接C2RTL和C2HLSC分别适用于5个案例，成功率分别为5/5和1/5。在匹配Design Compiler综合条件下，HINT相比5个人工RTL实现减少面积5.0%-26.2%，相比5个直接C2RTL结果减少8.9%-86.1%。RealBench AES和SDC以及综合到561.67k μm²的Vortex VPU进一步验证了规范驱动、协议丰富、存储丰富和层次化设计的能力。在受控算子研究中，HINT介导路线表现出更好的收敛性，避免了直接生成路线的严重实现质量退化。

Conclusion: HINT通过引入可执行的硬件意图中间表示层，有效提升了LLM生成RTL的合约符合性、综合质量和收敛性，相比直接C2RTL和C2HLSC方法具有明显优势，适用于包括协议丰富、存储丰富及层次化设计在内的多种实际硬件设计场景。

Abstract: Generating implementation-quality RTL with large language models (LLMs) remains difficult because direct generation must resolve microarchitecture while simultaneously producing and debugging low-level code. We present HINT, an executable hardware-intent intermediate representation layer between behavioral specifications or executable oracles and RTL. HINT makes RTL-relevant microarchitecture explicit, supports pre-RTL checking, and supplies explicit RTL-lowering obligations. We evaluate HINT using both a minimal single-agent flow and a full staged workflow. Across seven operator cases, the HINT-mediated route, with no post-synthesis QoR refinement, produces contract-compliant synthesizable RTL on 7/7 cases; Direct C2RTL and C2HLSC apply to five cases and succeed on 5/5 and 1/5, respectively. Under matched Design Compiler synthesis, HINT reduces area by 5.0\%--26.2\% relative to five manual RTL implementations and by 8.9\%--86.1\% relative to five accepted Direct C2RTL results. RealBench AES and SDC, together with a Vortex VPU synthesizing to 561.67k~$μ\mathrm{m}^2$, further demonstrate specification-driven, protocol-rich, memory-rich, and hierarchical designs. In the controlled operator study, the HINT-mediated route shows better observed convergence and avoids the severe implementation-quality degradation seen in several direct-generation results.

</details>


### [128] [Hybrid ASIC-FPAA Fabric for Performance Security Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07896)
*Ziyi Chen,Vaibhav Venugopal Rao,Kyle Juretus,Ioannis Savidis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合ASIC与FPAA的混合架构，通过可编程晶体管对阵列混淆模拟电路拓扑结构，同时利用虚拟晶体管对进一步掩盖电路规模，在性能与安全之间取得平衡。在delta-sigma调制器上验证，ENOB仅下降不到0.25%，功耗增加28.1%，安全性提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路面临知识产权盗窃和逆向工程的严重威胁，而现有的安全防护措施往往以牺牲电路性能为代价。作者希望提出一种混合架构，在提供强安全性的同时尽量减小对模拟电路性能的负面影响，解决性能与安全之间的固有矛盾。

Method: 1) 提出ASIC-FPAA混合架构，将可编程晶体管对阵列嵌入ASIC中用于拓扑混淆；2) 利用虚拟晶体管对进一步混淆电路中活跃晶体管数量，掩盖电路规模信息；3) 开发了混淆整个模拟电路拓扑的方法；4) 提出一种映射算法，针对大型电路识别最适合在FPAA织物上混淆的子模块，权衡性能与安全；5) 在delta-sigma调制器上进行验证，将比较器模块部署到FPAA上。

Result: 以delta-sigma调制器为例，在FPAA上实现比较器模块导致ENOB降低不到0.25%，功耗增加28.1%。安全性基准测试表明，所提技术相比现有方法提供5倍的安全性提升，且不损害电路性能。

Conclusion: ASIC-FPAA混合架构能够有效缓解模拟电路性能与安全之间的权衡问题。通过采用智能的模块映射算法，可以将对性能敏感的模块保留在ASIC上，而将适合混淆的模块部署到FPAA上，从而在显著提升安全性的同时几乎不影响电路性能。

Abstract: In this paper, a hybrid architecture that combines an application specific integrated circuit (ASIC) and a field-programmable analog array (ASIC-FPAA) is proposed to address the performance-security trade-off in analog circuits. A programmable transistor pair (TP) is developed that obfuscates the topology of an analog circuit within an array of transistor pairs while minimizing the degradation in circuit performance due to the implemented security features. In addition, a technique that utilizes dummy transistor pairs is proposed that further masks the topology of a circuit by obfuscating the number of active transistors that comprise the target analog circuit. A method to obfuscate the entire topology of an analog circuit is also developed. The FPAA provides the highest-level of security robustness as the entire circuit topology is programmed onto the transistor pair array, while trading-off performance. For large analog circuits requiring multiple configurable analog blocks (CABs), an algorithm is developed that identifies and maps the sub-blocks of the circuit best suited for obfuscation on the FPAA fabric, while accounting for the trade-off between performance and security. For the delta-sigma modulator, the comparator module is selected for implementation on the FPAA fabric, which results in a reduction of less than 0.25% in the effective number of bits (ENOB) and an increase of 28.1% in the power consumption as compared to an unobfuscated delta-sigma modulator. The security robustness of the proposed techniques are evaluated against the latest attack methodologies. Results indicate that the proposed techniques provide a 5x improvement in security as measured by a developed metric without compromising performance.

</details>


### [129] [ARMOR: Accelerating RTL Simulation by Mitigating the Front-End Bottleneck Using Node Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08462)
*Jiaping Tang,Jianan Mu,Zhiteng Chao,Jingzhong Wen,Jing Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ARMOR是一种高效RTL模拟器，通过节点压缩技术缓解前端瓶颈。核心思路是利用展开RTL图中暴露的数据并行性和位空间利用不足，通过位级数据并行同时压缩多个节点，使单条指令序列可服务于多个节点。实验表明在CPU设计上提升1.6倍，在AI加速器上提升2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RTL模拟器将RTL图中每个节点降到指令序列，虽然便于编译器优化但导致代码体积急剧膨胀，严重超出指令缓存容量造成前端瓶颈。作者分析发现超过50%的流水线停顿由CPU前端引起，这已成为最先进RTL模拟器的关键性能瓶颈。同时，如何在完全展开RTL图获得优化好处的同时减小代码足迹来缓解前端瓶颈极具挑战性。

Method: ARMOR采用三种关键技术：(1)模块感知的同构子图识别方法，利用模块实例间的结构同构性在子图级别系统识别压缩机会；(2)对齐感知的密集打包策略，根据数据流依赖将节点分组打包同时保留数据重用，辅以贪心合并策略增强位空间利用率；(3)统一的位级并行方案，支持压缩节点的位级并行执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，与最先进的模拟器相比，ARMOR在CPU设计上实现1.6倍加速，在AI加速器上实现2.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过节点压缩利用位级数据并行性，ARMOR成功在享受完全展开RTL图优化好处的同时减小代码足迹，有效缓解前端瓶颈并显著提升RTL模拟性能。

Abstract: RTL simulation is indispensable in chip design. High-performance simulators typically lower each node in the RTL graph into an instruction sequence. Although this per-node lowering enables aggressive compiler optimizations, it dramatically increases the code footprint, severely exceeding instruction cache capacity and causing front-end bottlenecks. Our profiling reveals that over 50% of pipeline stalls are caused by the CPU front-end, becoming a key performance bottleneck in state-of-the-art RTL simulators. However, reaping the optimization benefits of fully unrolling the RTL graph while simultaneously reducing the code footprint to mitigate front-end bottlenecks remains highly challenging. In this paper, we propose ARMOR, an efficient RTL simulator designed to alleviate the front-end bottleneck through node compression. The key idea is to exploit the data parallelism exposed by the unrolling RTL graph and the insufficient bit-space utilization revealed by per-node lowering, leveraging bit-level data parallelism to compress multiple nodes simultaneously, so that a single instruction sequence can serve multiple nodes instead of one per node. To achieve profitable node compression, we first propose a module-aware isomorphic subgraph identification method that leverages structural isomorphism across module instances to systematically identify compression opportunities at the subgraph level. We then propose an alignment-aware dense packing strategy that groups nodes into packs according to dataflow dependencies while preserving data reuse, complemented by greedy merging strategies to enhance bit-space utilization. Finally, we implement a unified bit-level parallelism scheme to support bit-level parallel execution of compressed nodes. Experimental results show that ARMOR achieves 1.6x speedup on CPU designs and 2.7x speedup on AI accelerators compared to state-of-the-art simulators.

</details>


### [130] [ReVolt: Power Delivery Network-Aware Voltage Droop Control for 2.5D PIM Chiplet Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08496)
*Vibhanshu Sharma,Alish Kanani,Miao Sun,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Umit Y. Ogras,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ReVolt是一个基于LSTM动态操作单元（OU）的框架，用于缓解PIM多芯片组平台中的电压跌落问题，通过预测芯片级供电电压轨迹并主动调节OU大小，在保持ML推理精度的同时实现了平均76倍的EDP改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIM-based 2.5D多芯片组平台是ML工作负载的关键使能技术，但其性能受到电源分配网络（PDN）中电压跌落的显著影响。芯片级电流需求的变化会导致空间和时间上变化的电压跌落，引发电压违例、性能下降和ML推理精度受损。现有方法缺乏高效且动态的缓解策略。

Method: ReVolt利用LSTM-based PDN替代模型在运行时预测每个芯片组的供电电压轨迹，将操作单元（OU）大小作为控制旋钮，通过主动调整OU大小来调节芯片级电流需求，从而缓解电压跌落事件，同时保持计算精度。

Result: 实验结果表明ReVolt能够完全预防电压跌落违例，与现有的固定和动态OU基线相比，实现了平均76倍的EDP（能量-延迟积）降低，且不影响ML模型的推理精度。

Conclusion: ReVolt通过LSTM驱动的PDN预测与动态OU调节相结合，为PIM多芯片组系统中的电压跌落问题提供了有效的运行时解决方案，在消除电压违例的同时显著提升能效，并保持ML推理准确率不受损害。

Abstract: Processing-in-memory (PIM)-based 2.5D multi-chiplet platforms are enablers for machine learning (ML) workloads. However, their performance is affected by the power delivery network (PDN), where varying chiplet-level current demand induces spatially and temporally varying voltage droop. These droop events lead to voltage violations, degrades system performance, and impact inference accuracy for ML workloads. In this work, we propose ReVolt, a dynamic operation unit (OU)-based framework for mitigating voltage droop in PIM-based multi-chiplet systems. ReVolt leverages an LSTM-based PDN surrogate to predict per-chiplet supply voltage trajectories at runtime, enabling proactive adjustment of OU size to mitigate droop events. By treating OU size as a control knob, ReVolt regulates chiplet-level current demand while maintaining computational accuracy. This approach prevents voltage droop violations and improves energy-delay product (EDP) while preserving ML model inference accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that ReVolt prevents voltage droop violations while achieving an average 76x reduction in EDP compared to existing fixed and dynamic OU-based baselines, without compromising inference accuracy of ML models.

</details>


### [131] [Eco-SoC: A Sustainable VLSI Architecture for Energy-Proportional Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08761)
*Jatin Chopra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Eco-SoC，一种面向可持续AI的高度可扩展VLSI架构，通过硬件级动态精度缩放逻辑（DPSL）基于激活稀疏度自适应调节位宽精度，在7nm FinFET工艺上降低42%的开关活动；同时引入热感知电源门控机制将硅片平均故障时间（MTTF）翻倍，为电子垃圾减缓提供可扩展策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在气候变化加剧与边缘智能广泛部署的背景下，半导体制造与运行的环境成本已达临界阈值。随着深度学习加速器占据SoC芯片主导面积，传统静态最坏情况效率设计已无法满足可持续性需求，亟需转向动态能量比例化（energy-proportionality）的设计范式。

Method: 1) 提出硬件级动态精度缩放逻辑（DPSL）框架，基于实时激活稀疏度自适应调节比特宽度精度，降低开关活动；2) 采用商业7nm FinFET工艺节点实现；3) 使用架构碳足迹工具（ACT）进行全生命周期评估（LCA），超越传统PPA指标；4) 引入热感知电源门控机制以缓解局部热点。

Result: 开关活动最多降低42%；仅增加4.8%面积开销（体现碳足迹），在边缘部署1.1年内即可抵消其增加的内涵碳足迹；热感知电源门控使硅片预计平均故障时间（MTTF）翻倍。

Conclusion: Eco-SoC证明通过自适应的精度缩放与热管理策略，可以在性能、成本与可持续性之间取得最优平衡，为下一代计算系统提供切实可行的电子垃圾减缓与碳排放抵消方案，代表了从静态效率向动态能量比例化的范式转变。

Abstract: In an era defined by escalating climate change and the pervasive deployment of edge intelligence, the environmental cost of semiconductor manufacturing and operation has reached a critical threshold. As Deep Learning (DL) accelerators dominate System-on-Chip (SoC) die area, achieving true sustainability requires a paradigm shift from static worst-case efficiency to dynamic energy-proportionality. This paper introduces Eco-SoC, a highly scalable VLSI architecture co-designed specifically for sustainable artificial intelligence. We propose a hardware-level Dynamic Precision-Scaling Logic (DPSL) framework that adaptively modulates bit-width precision based on real-time activation sparsity, successfully reducing switching activity by up to 42% on a commercial 7nm FinFET process node.
  Furthermore, we transcend traditional Power-Performance-Area (PPA) metrics by providing a comprehensive Life Cycle Assessment (LCA) using the Architectural Carbon footprint Tool (ACT). Our synthesis demonstrates that Eco-SoC offsets its increased embodied carbon footprint (a marginal 4.8% area overhead) within 1.1 years of edge deployment. Finally, by introducing a thermal-aware power gating mechanism that mitigates localized hotspots, Eco-SoC doubles the projected Mean Time To Failure (MTTF) of the silicon, providing a tangible, scalable strategy for electronic waste (e-waste) mitigation in next-generation computing systems.

</details>


### [132] [FSGen: Agile Fused and Sparse Accelerator Generator with Accurate Power Model for LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09252)
*Jay Zhe-An Mok,Qijun Zhang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FSGen是一个面向基于注意力机制的大语言模型（LLM）加速器生成的敏捷框架，配备早期PPA（功耗、性能、面积）估算器，支持融合算子数据流和稀疏性，相比先前工作实现1.4倍更好的能效或10倍加速，帕累托最优设计在广泛LLM基准上具有58倍更好的品质因数（FoM），且设计空间探索速度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI应用需求增长，LLM已成为重要工作负载，但如何高效生成最优AI芯片加速器设计仍未被解决且充满挑战。目前缺乏端到端的设计方法论来进行高效的设计空间探索（DSE）。

Method: 提出FSGen框架，这是一个基于注意力机制LLM加速器生成的敏捷框架，包含早期PPA估算器，支持融合算子数据流和稀疏性，提供多样化的设计空间。

Result: FSGen相比先前工作实现1.4倍更好的功率效率或10倍加速（具有相似的PPA指标）；帕累托最优设计在广泛LLM基准上具有58倍更好的品质因数（FoM）；由于PPA估算器比先前工作具有更好的准确性，设计空间探索更快，DSE运行时间大幅减少。

Conclusion: FSGen通过端到端的敏捷设计框架和早期PPA估算器，有效解决了LLM加速器设计空间探索效率低下的问题，在性能、能效和设计探索速度方面均显著优于先前工作。

Abstract: With the growing demand of artificial intelligence (AI) applications, large language models (LLMs) have become important workloads in many domains. The question of how to efficiently generate optimal AI chip accelerator designs remains unresolved and challenging. Currently, there is a lack of end-to-end design methodologies for efficient design space exploration (DSE). We propose FSGen, an agile framework for attention-based LLM accelerator generation with an early-stage PPA estimator. FSGen supports fused operator dataflows and sparsity with a diverse design space and finds designs with 1.4x better power efficiency or 10x speedup with similar PPA metrics compared to prior work. Pareto-optimal designs have much better performance over a wide range of LLM benchmarks and have 58x better figures of merit (FoM). Design exploration is also faster due to our PPA estimators, which have better accuracy than prior art and reduce DSE runtime drastically.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPR）的新型深度强化学习（DRL）控制方法，采用执行器级策略（ACT）而非传统整体机器人策略，使单个共享策略可泛化至任意CDPR构型（不依赖执行器数量），并依赖逆运动学而非前向运动学。该策略在仿真中训练，成功迁移到真实CDPR，在鲁棒性和精度上超越传统RL方法，并实现了从4电机2D平面仿真策略直接控制8电机3D真实机器人的跨构型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDPR具有多样化的构型和复杂的控制挑战，DRL可用于学习其非线性动力学，但现有DRL方法训练时间长，且学到的策略难以泛化到不同机器人构型或不同执行器数量的场景，亟需一种构型无关的控制方法。

Method: 提出执行器级策略（ALP）的DRL方法：与传统学习控制整个机器人到达目标末端执行器位姿不同，该方法训练每个电机独立控制其目标缆绳长度，仅依赖逆运动学（IK）避免前向运动学（FK）的难题；通过单一共享策略适用于任意CDPR构型，与执行器数量无关；在仿真中训练后迁移至真实机器人。

Result: 实验表明：执行器级策略（ALP）在鲁棒性和精度上均优于传统强化学习方法；通过在4电机2D平面CDPR仿真中训练的策略，成功控制一个真实的8电机3D运动CDPR，验证了该方法不依赖执行器数量或布置位置的跨构型适用性。

Conclusion: 这是首次将DRL以执行器级策略应用于CDPR控制，证明了该方法可推广到任意CDPR构型，独立于执行器数量和布置方式，为该领域提供了一种高泛化性、高鲁棒性的控制新范式。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [134] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SC²-WM自校正世界模型框架，通过内部反馈实现VLN-CE中的闭环决策，包含状态级规划精炼和测试时模型级条件世界感知自适应，提升导航鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法大多依赖开环执行，缺乏在推理过程中检测和纠正内部状态漂移的机制，导致在部分可观测环境下导航决策不够稳健。

Method: SC²-WM框架引入内部反馈实现闭环决策：(1) 利用世界模型预见的反馈在执行动作前进行状态级规划精炼；(2) 引入条件世界感知自适应，在反馈表明模型容量不足时，于测试阶段选择性地更新世界模型实现模型级校正。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准上的实验表明，该方法提升了导航鲁棒性和泛化性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过世界模型内部反馈机制实现状态级与模型级双重校正，有效解决了部分可观测环境下的状态漂移问题，验证了闭环决策在VLN-CE任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [135] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自监督视觉操作方法，机器人围绕目标位姿自动生成演示数据并通过腕部RGB图像学习相对位姿修正，无需相机到机器人的外参标定，在仿真和真实UR5e机器人上实现了抓取任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作通常需要特定物体的编程、人工数据标注或标定感知管线，限制了实际场景中的快速部署。虽然示教学习提供了更直接的替代方案，但收集演示仍需人工遥操作或动觉示教。本文旨在消除这些人工干预，实现自动化的示教数据生成与学习。

Method: 该方法基于自监督学习：机器人围绕目标位姿自动生成标注的图像-位姿对，利用ROS 2和Isaac Sim构建数据管线，无需显式相机-机器人外参标定。分别生成用于平面细化（planar refinement）和粗三维接近（coarse 3D approach）的两类数据集，训练卷积网络从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移和旋转。执行时采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）控制策略：先用含高度变化的模型接近物体，再用平面数据细化最终对齐。

Result: 仿真中细化阶段将最终平面离散度从9.69 mm降至5.38 mm。真实实验中，在UR5e协作臂上对三个物理物体进行端到端抓取尝试，两个物体在无旋转条件下成功率分别达到66.6%和63.6%，且在旋转条件下仍保持部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自动生成的演示数据能够以有限的设置成本支持实用的视觉操作，同时暴露了深度预测和物体依赖泛化方面仍存在的挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [136] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRobotics，一个将编码代理架构（主代理+子代理+工具）移植到机器人策略改进领域的后端无关控制平面。它通过LLM控制器驱动可替换worker执行持久的"训练-评估-改进"事务，核心贡献在于将工具质量纳入度量、记录和过期机制，实现了无需人类在场的可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理（如Claude Code、Codex）成功地将"主代理管理循环、子代理分析与执行、工具执行工作"的架构应用于软件工程闭环。机器人策略改进领域同样需要这种架构，但存在一个关键差异：机器人的工具（训练好的策略、训练流水线、数据采集）经常失败，因此工具质量必须被度量、在每次调用时记录、并在其背后的工件发生变化时过期。现有方法缺少这种工具治理机制。

Method: AgenticRobotics是一个后端无关的控制平面，包含：LLM控制器驱动可替换的worker执行持久的train-evaluate-improve事务。具体组件包括：(1)不可变的目标（immutable objective）；(2)控制器拥有的度量（controller-owned measurement）；(3)提交密钥的崩溃恢复（commit-keyed crash recovery）；(4)证据分级的技能库（evidence-graded skill library）；(5)标准化、记录调用面的工具注册表（tool registry）。

Result: 实验表明该系统实现了：硬化的假晋升控制率0.001/run，而基线为0.005-0.021；在可选停止下具备时间任意有效的决策（anytime-valid decisions）；在kill注入下零丢失或重复效应；签名验证器捕获了六类工件篡改中的全部六类。

Conclusion: 标题中的"agentic"是一个操作性声明而非选择性声明：操作者可以离开是因为晋升受证据门槛控制、状态可恢复、能力质量由记录推导——而非因为循环比人类选择更好的检查点。在作者测量的单一谱系上，它并不比人类选得更好。这些门槛可量化地带来了假晋升控制、时间任意有效决策、崩溃恢复一致性和篡改检测能力。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [137] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种零成本的自动化直接偏好优化（DPO）流程，通过物理模拟状态回滚自动生成偏好数据，解决UAV视觉-语言导航（VLN）中轻量级模型（2B参数）在纯行为克隆下缺乏负反馈导致的鲁棒性缺陷（如碰撞率高）问题，在未测绘场景下达到49.16%的成功率并大幅抑制碰撞率，实现新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN的端到端范式依赖数十亿参数的大语言模型（如7B），推理延迟高，难以在边缘设备上部署。作者通过跨尺度评估发现感知质量比语言推理能力更关键，轻量级2B模型即可匹配7B性能。但纯行为克隆（BC）缺乏负反馈，导致智能体无法内化空间约束，在分布外（OOD）场景下碰撞率极高。如何在无需人工标注的前提下克服这一鲁棒性缺陷是核心动机。

Method: AeroDPO包含四个关键组件：（1）物理模拟状态回滚：检测到碰撞时自动回退环境状态；（2）提取因果推理错误作为被拒绝动作（rejected actions）；（3）解耦特权干预（decoupled privileged interventions）合成避碰偏好动作（preferred maneuvers）；（4）离线视觉语言审查器过滤视觉歧义。以此构建自动化数据飞轮，对2B轻量模型进行直接偏好优化（DPO）训练。

Result: AeroDPO在未测绘场景下将2B模型的成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，超越了7B大规模基线模型，建立了自主空中智能体的新SOTA。该结果验证了"感知质量优于语言推理规模"的核心洞察。

Conclusion: AeroDPO证明了大规模语言模型并非UAV-VLN的必要条件，通过自动化、零人工成本的DPO数据飞轮，可以在保持轻量级模型高效部署优势的同时，弥补纯行为克隆缺乏负反馈的鲁棒性短板，为边缘场景下的自主空中导航提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-ART，一个面向触觉/力觉感知的机器人学习分类体系，从统一视角整合多模态感知与多阶段系统设计，弥补现有综述未能联合回顾力/触觉感知学习的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触敏感型操作中，任务成功不仅依赖视觉与运动生成，还依赖力调节与自适应控制。尽管近期方法在力觉、触觉、视觉、语言等多模态传感集成上取得进展，且许多系统采用多阶段架构（高层策略+动作精化+低层控制），但现有综述缺乏从统一视角同时涵盖多模态感知和多阶段系统设计的力/触觉感知学习综述。

Method: 提出TF-ART（Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy），一种将力/触觉感知机器人学习方法映射到统一分层结构的分类体系。该框架系统刻画了近期工作如何组织观测模态、编码与融合异构感知输入、跨多阶段生成与精化动作，以及将学习策略与反应式机器人末端控制相连接。此外，还从任务设置与基础设施需求两方面审视物理交互。

Result: 该论文是一篇综述性工作，提出了一个统一的分类框架TF-ART，能够将各类力/触觉感知的机器人学习方法纳入统一的结构化体系，并融合了算法层面（多模态编码与融合、多阶段动作生成）与实践层面（任务设置、基础设施）的双重视角。

Conclusion: TF-ART填补了现有文献在力/触觉感知机器人学习统一综述方面的空白，提供了一种既能按方法论组织研究进展，又能结合任务设定与工程基础设施的综合视角，为后续研究提供了系统化的参考框架。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [139] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出投影-回缩MPPI（PR-MPPI）方法，在采样动力学内部强制满足操作任务中的等式和不等式约束（闭环运动链、关节限位、避障等），通过将采样速度投影到约束子空间/半空间并回缩命令到约束流形上，使约束满足达到数值容差且不受任务权重影响。在14自由度双臂系统和Unitree H1-2人形机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制因无梯度、可并行处理非凸代价而广泛用于操作任务，但其处理约束（如闭环运动链、关节限位、避障）时仅通过代价函数以软惩罚形式近似满足，在强任务代价下约束会失效，无法保证约束在整个运动过程中被精确满足。

Method: 提出Projection-Retraction MPPI（PR-MPPI），在每次rollout步内部强制约束：将采样速度先投影到等式的线性子空间，再在该子空间内投影到各不等式的半空间（确保不等式处理不破坏等式）。由于一阶投影在有限步长下会在等式上产生漂移，最后将返回的命令回缩回约束集，达到数值容差且与任务权重无关。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统的仿真中，返回的命令在关节限位压力测试和随机避障场景下使闭环等式满足到数值容差；在真实硬件上，Unitree H1-2人形机器人的双臂能够反应式躲避移动障碍物。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI通过在采样动力学内部而非仅靠代价惩罚来强制执行约束，使得操作任务中的闭环等式和不等式约束能被精确满足（达到数值容差且独立于任务权重），克服了传统MPPI软惩罚方法的局限，并在仿真与真实硬件上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [140] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将几何智能编码于模块化材料系统的新方法，通过自对准复合嵌套晶格模块（由共联的立方八面体-八面体单元组成）实现机器人抓取、自对准、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合，并验证了其在建筑和家具尺度上的可重构、可循环应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料系统与机器人视为独立设计问题，将智能集中在硬件、传感、运动规划和控制中。本文旨在探索如何将几何智能嵌入材料系统本身，以同时满足多种建造需求，从而实现更高效、可重构的机器人建造。

Method: 设计一种自对准复合嵌套晶格模块，由共联的立方八面体-八面体单元构成。立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对准面；八面体特征集成螺旋可释放的卡扣连接器与对应接收器；嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z三轴的交锁聚合。通过机械臂和移动装配器组装家具及建筑尺度结构，并进行压缩测试验证力学性能。

Result: 成功演示了多种结构构型（座椅、跨越结构、表面和垂直框架）。复合模块压缩测试显示刚度4,556 N/mm，最大载荷3,445 N，压缩模量17.5 MPa。模块可拆卸并在不同构型间重复使用，支持可重构和循环建造。

Conclusion: 通过在材料系统中嵌入几何智能，可以在单一模块中同时实现机器人交互、结构性能和三维可重构聚合，为可循环、可重构的机器人建造提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [141] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA，一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的局部跨层动作条件机制，通过深度感知的信息路由将VLM中间层特征分配至各并行融合块，在不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和训练方式的前提下，在LIBERO、CALIBO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D及真实世界操作任务中超越VLA-Adapter基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中，VLM到动作解码器之间的接口设计尚未被充分探索。已有方案要么只暴露表征层级中的狭窄部分，要么将每个解码器块僵硬地匹配到单一VLM层，限制了对跨深度互补任务证据的访问，因此需要一种更灵活、深度感知的VLM到动作的条件路由机制。

Method: LIRA提出一种局部跨层动作条件机制，将VLM到动作的条件化建模为深度感知的信息路由。具体流程：1) 在任务token特征和由中间VLM状态导出的LIRA Query特征上操作；2) 为每个并行融合块分配一个以其对应VLM层为中心的深度对齐局部窗口；3) 并行融合块聚合邻近的LIRA Query特征，并与任务token特征和本体感受输入集成后进行动作预测。该方法保持骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练方案不变。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D及真实世界操作任务中，LIRA在相同0.5B参数配置下超越VLA-Adapter基线的主要聚合指标。在LIBERO-Plus的零样本迁移中，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提升18.9个百分点，表明在受控分布偏移下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LIRA通过深度对齐的局部跨层窗口路由机制，有效解决了VLM特征到动作解码器接口中的信息瓶颈，在不改动既有架构与训练流程的情况下提升了VLA模型的性能和分布偏移下的鲁棒性，为VLM到动作条件化提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [142] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联来紧密融合物体几何与触觉反馈，实现接触前抓取姿态生成与接触后触觉引导的自适应细化，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升抓取成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。人类通过视觉与触觉的无缝整合实现稳定自适应抓取，这一能力在机器人系统中难以复现。

Method: 提出统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含三个环节：抓取生成、可行性预测和自适应细化。核心是构建高效的视觉-触觉表示，将触觉信号与手指身份（finger identities）关联，从而紧密融合物体几何信息与触觉反馈。该表示既支持规划阶段的接触感知抓取姿态生成，也支持接触后的触觉引导细化，使系统能够推理细粒度的手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验表明，该方法显著提升了抓取成功率，并在多种不同物体上展现了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过视觉与触觉的统一融合以及接触前后的自适应调整，该框架有效克服了纯视觉抓取方法的局限，为机器人实现稳定、自适应且通用的抓取能力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [143] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于扩展现实（XR）的3D可视化平台，用于经皮心脏介入手术中导管的6自由度（6-DOF）实时追踪与可视化。该系统结合机器视觉算法与3D打印机电编码器实现完整6-DOF运动重建。在20名医学生的验证实验中，3D可视化相比传统2D视图，任务完成速度快5倍以上，导管移动距离减少约5倍，同时显著提升了靶向精度、降低了性能变异性，并改善了运动学控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术不断进步，但多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，导致操作者缺乏深度感知和空间理解能力，制约了手术精度与培训效率。本研究旨在解决这一临床痛点，开发一种能够提供实时6-DOF导管追踪与3D心脏模型可视化集成的XR平台。

Method: 系统采用双模块架构：（1）定制机器视觉算法实现导管5自由度追踪；（2）3D打印机电编码器测量导管滚动角（roll），两者结合实现完整6-DOF运动重建。在概念验证研究中，20名无经验医学生分别使用沉浸式3D可视化和传统2D导管室视图，操作心内超声（ICE）导管导航至六个解剖靶点，记录时间、路径长度、精度及性能变异性等指标，并进行运动学分析。

Result: 3D条件下平均完成时间54.6秒、导管移动距离1,939毫米，而2D条件下分别为267.5秒和7,854毫米。3D模式速度提升超过5倍，导管移动减少约5倍，同时靶向精度更高、性能变异性更小。参与者一致在准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值维度上给予3D可视化更高评分。运动学分析显示3D条件下深度轴导航更平滑，而2D用户依赖反复修正动作。

Conclusion: XR-based 3D可视化能够显著提升经皮心脏介入手术的培训效率、操作精度和运动控制能力。该系统为未来将沉浸式3D可视化引入临床实践提供了有力证据，有望改善介入训练的标准化程度和手术操作的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [144] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GWM-VLA，一种几何感知的潜在世界建模框架，通过几何感知多视角状态编码、全局上下文条件的目标视角预测和共享潜在-动作表示来提升VLA模型在视觉与环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在任务中表现优异但容易在视觉和环境变化下性能退化。现有的潜在世界建模方法通常独立编码各相机视角，预测整体场景动态，没有显式建模视角之间的几何关系，限制了模型的鲁棒性。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个关键组件：(1) VGGT-Ω在每一时间步联合聚合多视角观测，构建几何感知的多视角状态；(2) 潜在世界模型利用多视角聚合后的patch和register token预测目标视角的下一帧patch token，无需预测完整多视角状态；(3) 共享的潜在-动作表示同时条件化潜在世界模型和flow-matching动作头，使潜在预测监督和真实机器人动作监督共同塑造同一潜在表示空间。实验以腕部视角为目标，强调末端执行器运动和局部夹爪-物体交互。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人环境中均验证了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性，相比基线方法在视觉与环境变化下表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多视角几何关系并以目标视角预测替代完整多视角状态预测，配合共享潜在-动作表示的双重监督机制，GWM-VLA在保证效率的同时显著提升了VLA模型在分布外场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [145] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER，一种带有可重构脊柱的履带式机器人，能够同时调整其铰接点和内部质心位置。通过使用带簧机构将柔顺性集中在弯曲区域，同时保持其余部位的高刚度，TRASER在提高前端可达性和质心转移能力方面取得突破，实现了台阶、悬空平台和沟渠的73-74%机器人身长度的越障能力，为同类履带机器人的最高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带式机器人在非结构化环境中应用广泛，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的固有矛盾。现有设计难以同时优化这两个方面，亟需一种能够兼顾可重构性和稳定性的新型机构设计。

Method: 1) 设计TRASER，采用带簧机制将柔顺性局部化于弯曲区域，其余身体保持高刚度；2) 能够同时重新定位铰接点和内部质量；3) 建立几何模型和静力学模型，分析铰接点和质心位置对台阶和沟渠越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，TRASER可分别实现机器人身体长度74%、66%和59%的台阶、悬空平台和沟渠越障能力，据作者所述为目前公开报道的履带式移动机器人中越障能力最高的结果。

Conclusion: 通过同时调整铰接点和质心位置，配合带簧机构的柔顺性局部化设计，能够有效解决履带机器人越障时前端可达性与稳定性之间的权衡问题，为高性能越障履带机器人提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [146] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可微凸多面体带符号距离函数——Hölder signed distance，通过引入Hölder最小和最大算子替代经典SDF公式中的min-max算子，实现了闭式求解、全局可微且无需迭代算法，适用于机器人运动规划与控制中的实时优化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划与控制中需要计算集合间距离，且要求梯度可微以实现实时优化。然而经典的欧几里得带符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微，现有替代方法在可微性、符号信息或计算效率上存在取舍，难以同时兼顾。

Method: 首先提出可微的最小和最大算子（Hölder minimum与Hölder maximum），然后将经典SDF公式中的原始min-max算子替换为Hölder算子，得到Hölder signed distance。该方法是闭式（closed-form）计算的，不依赖任何迭代算法，因此不存在收敛问题，且天然适合GPU并行化。

Result: 通过与其他现有方法的运行时对比实验验证了该距离函数的实际优势和计算性能，并在机械臂操作实验中展示了其在控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: Hölder signed distance在保持可微性和符号信息的同时实现了闭式高效计算，消除了迭代方法的收敛隐患，为机器人实时运动规划与控制提供了一种理想的凸多面体距离度量。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [147] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动仓库中高密度存储（puzzle-based storage）与高检索吞吐量之间的权衡问题，提出了一种新的多机器人「有序存取最大容量」问题形式化方法，并设计了一种基于无重排布局不变量的在线优先多智能体路径规划算法，保证了完整性与无死锁性，实验表明算法在机器人数量增至网格宽度C之前可实现近线性的makespan提升，且鲁棒性开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库在最大化存储密度与实现高检索吞吐量之间存在根本性矛盾。PBS架构通过消除通道提升存储容量，但在高密度空间内协调多机器人计算上极具挑战，尤其是死锁问题。已有集中式规划器在可扩展性方面不足，需要一种能够利用存储布局特定不变量来保证完整性和避免死锁的高效方法。

Method: 提出一种新的多机器人形式化方法来解决单边界可访问矩形网格上的"有序存储与检索问题"（在最大容量约束下）。核心方法是利用无重排（relocation-free）布局的性质作为指导，构建一个在线、优先级的（prioritized）多智能体路径查找（MAPF）算法。该方法利用具体存储排列的不变量来保证完整性和防止死锁，而非依赖通用的集中式规划器。

Result: 实验表明，在机器人数量m不超过网格宽度C的情况下，该方法在makespan方面随机器人数量呈近线性改善。同时，支持鲁棒性的算法开销可忽略：系统使用鲁棒存储布局处理出发序列中的不确定性，相比非鲁棒基线在执行速度上没有显著损失。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，通过布局的不变量属性实现了兼具可扩展性、完整性和死锁规避能力的多机器人仓库检索方案，并且鲁棒性支持几乎不增加计算成本，为高密度自动仓库的实用部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [148] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回旋曲线（Euler螺旋）与自适应前视距离的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，通过准反馈控制算法生成最优回旋曲线、前馈滤波器补偿横向动力学滞后，并在TruckSim/Simulink联合仿真中验证了其在多种驾驶场景下横向控制性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶横向控制策略在路径跟踪精度与乘坐舒适性之间难以取得理想平衡，尤其在弯道曲率变化处容易产生不适感和侧翻风险，需要一种能够实现平滑曲率过渡并自适应调整控制参数的方法。

Method: 采用回旋曲线（Euler螺旋）实现平滑曲率过渡；设计自适应前视距离机制，根据实时车辆动力学状态与道路几何信息动态调整前视距离；利用准反馈控制算法在每一步构建最优回旋曲线并生成转向输入；通过超前滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学延迟，提升控制响应速度与稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证，该控制器在多种驾驶场景下均表现出显著的横向控制性能提升，包括更高的路径跟踪精度和更好的横向稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于回旋曲线和自适应前视机制的横向控制策略在提升路径跟踪精度和横向稳定性方面有效可行；未来将扩展至高速场景并优化铰接车辆的离轨误差。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [149] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCoNav是一个结合在线共形校准与运行时认证规划的群体导航框架，通过逐层共形比例-积分控制器自适应轨迹误差界，并采用"先松弛后验证"的规划器保证求解可行性，最终在仿真与四足机器人实验中展现出碰撞避免、任务成功率与导航效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式导航方法易产生振荡行为，而预测性规划器往往将预测视为精确或依赖限制性的误差模型；将保守不确定性集作为硬约束又可能导致模型预测控制（MPC）不可行。因此，需要一种既能适应动态变化的预测误差、又能保证求解可行性与安全性的群体导航方法。

Method: CoCoNav由两部分组成：(1) 逐层特定的共形比例-积分（conformal PI）控制器，用于在线校准轨迹误差界，调节长期经验覆盖率以应对变化的预测误差；(2) "先松弛后验证"（relax-then-verify）规划器，先使用软约束MPC生成名义轨迹以保持求解可行性，再连同应急机动（contingency maneuvers）一起，根据校准后的误差界对轨迹进行单独认证，通过后才执行。

Result: 在仿真和四足机器人实验中，CoCoNav相较于评估的基线方法在碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: CoCoNav通过将共形校准与运行时认证结合，有效解决了预测不确定性下的安全导航问题，在保证规划可行性的同时实现了良好的安全性与效率平衡，验证了该框架在真实机器人平台上的实用性。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [150] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高海拔雪地环境的无人机-无人车协同导航框架，通过定制的高效U-Net架构实现实时道路分割（结合合成雪数据增强技术，分割精度达96.5%），采用扩展卡尔曼滤波融合GPS与IMU数据实现无人机定位（最大位置误差±0.5米），并利用YOLOv5及RGB-D深度数据完成无人车视觉跟踪，最终通过动态路径规划算法应对雪堆障碍，在遮挡测试环境中实现成功导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔雪地环境中能见度降低且地面不稳定，传统导航方法在此类恶劣条件下失效，亟需一种多机器人协同的鲁棒导航方案。

Method: (1) 设计定制的高效U-Net架构用于实时道路分割，并引入新颖的合成雪数据增强技术；(2) 采用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）融合机载GPS与IMU数据进行无人机定位；(3) 通过YOLOv5结合无人机RGB-D相机的深度数据对无人车进行视觉跟踪定位；(4) 利用分割结果驱动动态路径规划算法以适应雪堆漂移。

Result: 道路分割准确率达96.5%，无人机最大位置误差为±0.5米，系统在能见度受限的测试环境中能够以极小偏差成功完成导航。

Conclusion: 该协同框架有效克服了高海拔雪地环境中的导航挑战，验证了合成数据增强与多传感器融合策略在恶劣地形下的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [151] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用混合刚度指尖（中心软、两侧硬）抓取薄平物体（如硬币）的方法。相比均匀柔软指尖，混合刚度指尖在无指甲辅助的情况下，在更多测试条件下能成功形成几何约束并稳定抓取硬币，但其性能在指尖轴向硬币位置变化时表现较差。经过硬币尺寸调优后，两种指尖均能抓取全部六种日本硬币。研究提出刚度边界的位置是除指甲外一个新的指尖设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在坚硬表面上抓取硬币等薄而平的物体仍然具有挑战性，传统方法需要重新定向物体、接触物体底面或增设专用指甲机构。此前研究发现刚性指甲能阻止软垫变形并形成几何约束从而提高抓取精度，但本文想探究能否通过材料选择而非解剖结构，在指尖垫内部创建额外的约束边界，从而扩展该约束成立的条件范围。

Method: 制备了仿人指尖，采用Shore E10硅胶（中心）与Shore A60硅胶（两侧）的混合刚度设计，并与均匀柔软E10指尖对比。使用自动化装置执行倾斜旋转指尖捏取，指尖垫接触硬币侧面，在硬币与表面无间隙的平贴状态下将其提起。系统变化水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转角度等参数进行测试。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持了较高的成功率（包括几何约束形成阶段和向稳定抓取的过渡阶段），而无指甲的均匀对在所有36种条件下均失败。但在改变硬币沿手指轴向位置时，均匀对表现更好，存在条件依赖的权衡。经硬币尺寸调优后，两种指尖类型均能成功抓取全部六种日本硬币面额。

Conclusion: 薄物抓取的操作范围不仅取决于垫的柔软度，还取决于刚度在指甲支撑垫内的具体分布位置，刚度边界放置可作为指尖设计的一个候选变量。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [152] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPF（多模态概率融合）框架，用于审计视觉-语言-动作策略训练数据中指令-轨迹不匹配（ITMs）问题，通过无训练地将各模态作为专家进行概率融合，实现了最强的ITM检测和标签校正精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集中常存在行为正确但与错误语言指令配对的轨迹，这种指令-轨迹不匹配（ITMs）与失败的轨迹不同，看起来合理，会破坏策略学习的语言-行为映射关系，因此需要事后审计来识别和纠正这些问题。

Method: MMPF是一种免训练的审计框架，将每个模态视为一个专家，通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布，然后利用预测熵权重在专家乘积（product of experts）框架下融合多模态信息。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和带注入指令不匹配及嘈杂的真实机器人数据上，MMPF在ITM检测和标签纠正精度上取得了最强性能；审计还能改善需要语言来消除任务歧义的场景中下游策略学习的效果；真实机器人实验验证了改进的策略性能，并展示了过滤演示与重新标记之间的权衡。

Conclusion: MMPF作为一种有效的免训练审计方法，能够可靠地检测和纠正指令-轨迹不匹配，从而提升下游机器人策略学习性能，实际应用中需在过滤演示和重新标记之间做出权衡选择。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [153] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），引入运动对齐和目标对齐两个互补目标，实现轨迹级时空表征学习，提升空间理解、执行精度和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）通常以2D像素空间表示视频，与机器人动作执行的3D空间存在表征鸿沟；近期3D方法虽引入3D信息，但未能充分挖掘3D结构的动态特性。

Method: 提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入WAMs。包含两个互补目标：1）运动对齐——对齐相邻帧间的时序特征变化，促进局部4D感知；2）目标对齐——通过最小化源帧与最终目标之间的注意力相似度分布差距，引导模型推断最终目的地。

Result: 在多种基线模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，模型在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和多功能性方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过局部运动监督和长时程目标引导的联合优化，4D-WAM使WAMs能够学习轨迹级时空表征，从而弥合2D视频表示与3D动作执行之间的鸿沟，是一种即插即用的通用训练策略。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment.
